21/// Callback that rewrites a node's child references given resolved child data,
22/// used to inline already-cached children in place of lazy hash pointers.
23pub type SwizzleFn = fn(&NodeData, &[NodeId], &[Option<Arc<NodeData>>]) -> NodeData;

25/// Result of [`PackfileStorage::plan_collection_repack`] — what a real repack
26/// of this collection would do, computed exactly (same scan + reachability
27/// pass) but without performing any writes.
28#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
29pub struct RepackPlan {
30    /// Records that would survive (be kept) by this repack.
31    pub kept: usize,
32    /// Records that would be dropped (found unreachable) by this repack.
33    pub dropped: usize,
34    /// Total on-disk frame bytes of the records that would be kept.
35    pub kept_bytes: u64,
36    /// Total on-disk frame bytes of records that would be pruned.
37    pub dropped_bytes: u64,
38    /// Every shard id at least one kept record currently lives in.
39    pub shards_touched: Vec<u16>,
40}

42/// Which index-loading path a `PackfileStorage` open took.
43#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
44pub enum OpenPath {
45    /// A persisted index checkpoint validated against the current pack set's
46    /// fingerprint was loaded in place of a rescan.
47    Checkpoint,
48    /// No usable checkpoint: every pack's records were scanned and the
49    /// per-collection indexes rebuilt from scratch.
50    FullScan,
51}
52
53/// Where a checkpoint-path open sourced each collection's per-shard counts
54/// and home-shard assignment.
55#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
56pub enum BookkeepingSource {
57    /// Reconstructed from the fingerprint-gated `shard_collections.bin`
58    /// records — no slot walk across any collection index.
59    Sidecar,
60    /// Computed by walking every slot of every collection index.
61    SlotScan,
62}
63
64/// Wall-clock breakdown of one `PackfileStorage` open, by phase. Measured
65/// with a handful of `Instant::now()` deltas on the open path only, so
66/// recording these doesn't perturb the numbers they report.
67///
68/// The per-phase fields partition the synchronous open work; see the
69/// struct-level timing doc for what each phase covers. On the checkpoint
70/// path `full_scan` is zero; on the fallback path `checkpoint_decode` and
71/// `fingerprint` still reflect the attempt that failed validation (e.g. a
72/// missing or stale checkpoint) before the scan had to run.
73#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
74pub struct OpenTimings {
75    /// Directory scan + shard file open/validation, plus writer-lock take.
76    pub shard_open: std::time::Duration,
77    /// Reading the collection-order and deleted-collections sidecars.
78    pub metadata_load: std::time::Duration,
79    /// Reading + decoding the persisted index checkpoint.
80    pub checkpoint_decode: std::time::Duration,
81    /// Recomputing the live `(pack_id, file_len)` set's fingerprint.
82    pub fingerprint: std::time::Duration,
83    /// Deserializing checkpoint index blobs into live collections (checkpoint
84    /// path) or building per-collection indexes from scanned records
85    /// (fallback path). On the checkpoint path this includes replaying any
86    /// fingerprint/generation-gated delta-log frames on top of the raw slots.
87    pub index_materialization: std::time::Duration,
88    /// Reading the delta log, validating its gates, and applying frames to
89    /// each affected collection (checkpoint path only; ZERO when there is no
90    /// committed log to replay).
91    pub delta_replay: std::time::Duration,
92    /// The full packfile scan + torn-tail recovery pass (fallback only).
93    pub full_scan: std::time::Duration,
94    /// Total synchronous wall time of the open.
95    pub total: std::time::Duration,
96    /// Which index-loading path was taken.
97    pub path: OpenPath,
98    /// Where per-shard counts and home-shard assignment came from (checkpoint
99    /// path only; the fallback path builds them from the scan).
100    pub bookkeeping_source: BookkeepingSource,
101}
102
103impl Default for OpenTimings {
104    fn default() -> Self {
105        Self {
106            shard_open: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
107            metadata_load: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
108            checkpoint_decode: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
109            fingerprint: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
110            index_materialization: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
111            delta_replay: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
112            full_scan: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
113            total: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
114            path: OpenPath::FullScan,
115            bookkeeping_source: BookkeepingSource::SlotScan,
116        }
117    }
118}
119
120/// Wall-clock breakdown of one sync — the append-path `sync()` (dirty-scoped
121/// flush/fsync) or the full `sync_all` — by phase. The pack phases come from
122/// `ShardPool::last_sync_split` (which measures the flush and fsync legs
123/// separately) and the metadata phases from the sync method itself.
124#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
125pub struct SyncTimings {
126    /// Writing buffered frames out to the pack files.
127    pub pack_flush: std::time::Duration,
128    /// fsync'ing the pack files.
129    pub pack_fsync: std::time::Duration,
130    /// Writing the shard→collection metadata sidecar.
131    pub sidecar: std::time::Duration,
132    /// Persisting the index checkpoint (complete rewrite) or appending the
133    /// incremental delta log instead (ZERO on the path not taken; the appended
134    /// case is measured when a dirty `sync()` can continue an existing log).
135    pub delta_log: std::time::Duration,
136    /// Persisting the index checkpoint.
137    pub checkpoint: std::time::Duration,
138    /// Total wall time of the `sync_all` call.
139    pub total: std::time::Duration,
140}
141
142impl Default for SyncTimings {
143    fn default() -> Self {
144        Self {
145            pack_flush: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
146            pack_fsync: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
147            sidecar: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
148            delta_log: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
149            checkpoint: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
150            total: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
151        }
152    }
153}

155/// Bounds on a [`PackfileStorage::walk_ancestors`] call.
156///
157/// Without a cap, a walk whose `stop_at` set is never reached (e.g. the
158/// caller passed a stale or wrong boundary) silently degrades into "walk
159/// the entire history of this branch" — `max_nodes` is the safety valve
160/// for that case.
161#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]
162pub struct WalkLimits {
163    /// Stop discovering new ancestors once this many nodes have been
164    /// visited. `None` means unbounded (walk until `stop_at` or the
165    /// collection's true roots are reached).
166    pub max_nodes: Option<usize>,
167}
168
169/// Lazy, ancestor-first iterator over the result of
170/// [`PackfileStorage::walk_ancestors`].
171///
172/// Every node was already resolved (hash-verified, same as [`PackfileStorage::get`])
173/// against one frozen generation snapshot while [`PackfileStorage::walk_ancestors`]
174/// built the walk — this iterator just replays that in ancestor-first
175/// order. No further store access happens here, so a repack that runs
176/// after the walk was built can't cause a node to go missing partway
177/// through iteration.
178pub struct DagWalk {
179    order: std::vec::IntoIter<[u8; 16]>,
180    resolved: HashMap<[u8; 16], NodeData>,
181}
182
183impl Iterator for DagWalk {
184    type Item = Result<(NodeId, NodeData), StorageError>;
185
186    fn next(&mut self) -> Option<Self::Item> {
187        let hash = self.order.next()?;
188        let data = self
189            .resolved
190            .remove(&hash)
191            .expect("order only contains hashes this walk itself resolved");
192        Some(Ok((hash, data)))
193    }
194}

196/// A live hash set plus the adjacency discovered while determining it, as
197/// returned by the reachability repacker's live-set computation.
198type AdjacencyResult = (Vec<[u8; 16]>, HashMap<[u8; 16], Vec<[u8; 16]>>);

200/// One shard's scanned `(hash, offset)` entries for a single collection, as
201/// produced by a repack's initial shard scan.
202type ScannedShard = (u16, Vec<([u8; 16], u64)>);
203
204/// Deduplicated live-location map for one collection during a repack scan.
205type RepackRecordMap = HashMap<[u8; 16], (u16, u64)>;
206/// Replacement index entries produced while copying one repack collection.
207type RepackOffsets = Vec<([u8; 16], u16, u64)>;
208/// Return type of [`PackfileStorage::repack_scan_incremental`]: the merged
209/// live-location map and (on the incremental path) the previous cursor state
210/// that the adjacency helpers need to skip re-reading already-seen nodes.
211type RepackScanResult = (RepackRecordMap, Option<RepackIncrementalState>);

213/// One scanned record's `(shard_id, hash, offset)`, as accumulated per
214/// collection during `PackfileStorage::open_with_options`'s initial scan.
215type ShardRecord = (u16, [u8; 16], u64, u64);
216/// `(collection_id, entry count, index memory usage in bytes, index capacity)`.
217pub type CollectionSummary = ([u8; 16], usize, usize, u32);

219/// Accumulator for `PackfileStorage::open_with_options`'s phase 2 —
220/// bundles the three maps `init_collection_from_scan` fills in per collection, so
221/// that function takes one out-parameter instead of three.
222#[derive(Default)]
223struct RoomScanOutput {
224    collections: HashMap<[u8; 16], ArcSwap<RoomGeneration>>,
225    shard_collections: HashMap<u64, HashMap<[u8; 16], u64>>,
226    collection_shards: HashMap<[u8; 16], HashSet<u64>>,
227}
228
229/// Magic bytes + version identifying the persisted shard→collection directory
230/// format (see `PackfileStorage::persist_shard_collections`).
231///
232/// Pre-release, no compatibility fallback: an unrecognized version is
233/// treated exactly like a missing/corrupt file (see
234/// `read_persisted_shard_collections`) — reset or let the next sync
235/// regenerate it, not a format this reader tries to still understand.
236/// This sidecar has changed shape three times already (adding insertion
237/// order, switching `shard_id` for `pack_id`, then v4 adding the
238/// pack-fingerprint gate); none of that carries forward, since nothing
239/// depends on reading a store from before the current format existed.
240const SHARD_ROOMS_MAGIC: &[u8; 4] = b"MSRM";
241/// v4 pins the reduced bookkeeping to the exact `(pack_id, file_len)` set by
242/// carrying the same `pack_fingerprint` as the index checkpoint.
243const SHARD_ROOMS_VERSION: u8 = 4;
244/// Header size: magic(4) + version(1) + `pack_fingerprint(8)` + `persisted_at(8)`.
245const SHARD_ROOMS_HEADER_LEN: usize = 4 + 1 + 8 + 8;
246/// One entry: `pack_id`(8) + `collection_id`(16) + count(8) + the
247/// collection's stable insertion ordinal(8).
248const SHARD_ROOMS_RECORD_LEN: usize = 8 + 16 + 8 + 8;
249
250/// The largest record offset `IndexSlot` can represent: its 28-bit offset
251/// field stores `offset + 1`, reserving the all-zeros encoding for the empty
252/// sentinel. Offsets beyond this can only come from legacy or externally
253/// created oversized packs — this engine's own writes rotate long before
254/// reaching it (`MAX_SHARD_BYTES` caps each shard's file size).
255const PACK_INDEX_OFFSET_LIMIT: u64 = (1u64 << 28) - 2;
256
257/// Byte ceiling for the incremental index delta log. Once a session's
258/// accumulated frames exceed this, the next `sync()` stops appending and does
259/// a full checkpoint rewrite (which truncates the log), keeping replay cost
260/// and the log file bounded. Frames are 36 bytes each, so this is on the order
261/// of ~230k appends between full rewrites.
262const DELTA_LOG_CAP_BYTES: u64 = 8 * 1024 * 1024;
263
264/// Reject a record whose in-shard offset the 28-bit `IndexSlot` field cannot
265/// represent, so the caller surfaces a `StorageError::Corrupt` instead of
266/// silently dropping the record (or panicking in `IndexSlot::new`).
267fn check_index_offset(shard_id: u16, hash: &[u8; 16], offset: u64) -> Result<(), StorageError> {
268    if offset > PACK_INDEX_OFFSET_LIMIT {
269        return Err(StorageError::Corrupt(format!(
270            "shard {shard_id} holds record {hash:?} at offset {offset}, beyond the index offset limit {PACK_INDEX_OFFSET_LIMIT}"
271        )));
272    }
273    Ok(())
274}
275
276#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
277struct PersistedShardRoom {
278    pack_id: u64,
279    collection_id: [u8; 16],
280    count: u64,
281    insertion_order: u64,
282}
283
284/// A decoded shard→collection directory: the pack set it was written
285/// against, when it was written, and the per-(pack, collection) records.
286#[derive(Clone)]
287struct PersistedShardDirectory {
288    /// The `pack_fingerprint` of the `(pack_id, file_len)` set the counts
289    /// apply to. Only a directory whose fingerprint equals the index
290    /// checkpoint's may serve open's per-shard bookkeeping without re-walking
291    /// every slot — any other directory is stale (or corrupt) and must be
292    /// ignored in favor of the slot walk.
293    fingerprint: u64,
294    /// Unix-seconds timestamp of when the directory was persisted.
295    persisted_at: u64,
296    /// One record per (pack, collection) the store contains.
297    records: Vec<PersistedShardRoom>,
298}
299
300/// Decode the small inspection sidecar. This is deliberately shared by all
301/// read-only CLI summary helpers so they agree on validation and format
302/// compatibility.
303fn read_persisted_shard_collections(base_dir: &std::path::Path) -> Option<PersistedShardDirectory> {
304    let buf = fs::read(base_dir.join("shard_collections.bin")).ok()?;
305    if buf.len() < SHARD_ROOMS_HEADER_LEN
306        || &buf[0..4] != SHARD_ROOMS_MAGIC
307        || buf[4] != SHARD_ROOMS_VERSION
308    {
309        return None;
310    }
311    let body = &buf[SHARD_ROOMS_HEADER_LEN..];
312    if body.len().checked_rem(SHARD_ROOMS_RECORD_LEN) != Some(0) {
313        return None;
314    }
315    let fingerprint = u64::from_le_bytes(buf[5..13].try_into().ok()?);
316    let persisted_at = u64::from_le_bytes(buf[13..21].try_into().ok()?);
317    let records = body
318        .chunks_exact(SHARD_ROOMS_RECORD_LEN)
319        .map(|chunk| {
320            let pack_id = u64::from_le_bytes(chunk[0..8].try_into().ok()?);
321            let mut collection_id = [0u8; 16];
322            collection_id.copy_from_slice(&chunk[8..24]);
323            let count = u64::from_le_bytes(chunk[24..32].try_into().ok()?);
324            let insertion_order = u64::from_le_bytes(chunk[32..40].try_into().ok()?);
325            Some(PersistedShardRoom {
326                pack_id,
327                collection_id,
328                count,
329                insertion_order,
330            })
331        })
332        .collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
333    Some(PersistedShardDirectory {
334        fingerprint,
335        persisted_at,
336        records,
337    })
338}
339
340/// The durable insertion order supplied by the inspection directory.
341fn persisted_collection_order(base_dir: &std::path::Path) -> Option<Vec<[u8; 16]>> {
342    let records = read_persisted_shard_collections(base_dir)?.records;
343    let mut orders: HashMap<[u8; 16], u64> = HashMap::new();
344    for record in records {
345        let order = record.insertion_order;
346        orders
347            .entry(record.collection_id)
348            .and_modify(|current| *current = (*current).min(order))
349            .or_insert(order);
350    }
351    let mut collections: Vec<([u8; 16], u64)> = orders.into_iter().collect();
352    collections.sort_unstable_by_key(|(collection_id, order)| (*order, *collection_id));
353    Some(
354        collections
355            .into_iter()
356            .map(|(collection_id, _)| collection_id)
357            .collect(),
358    )
359}
360
361/// Disambiguates concurrent `persist_shard_collections` tmp filenames within
362/// this process, paired with the process id for uniqueness across
363/// processes — same rationale as `shard::STATS_TMP_COUNTER`.
364static SHARD_ROOMS_TMP_COUNTER: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
365
366/// Immutable snapshot of a collection's in-memory state.
367///
368/// Index and cache are bundled so readers see a consistent triple
369/// via a single `ArcSwap::load()` — no three-lock coordination.
370///
371/// `generation` is the collection index's structural generation: it
372/// increments on every capacity-changing or shape-changing rebuild (growth,
373/// repack, refresh) and is preserved across plain COW writes. The delta log
374/// stamps every frame with the generation it was recorded under, so a log
375/// never replay-applies frames for a pre-resize table onto a resized one.
376#[derive(Clone)]
377struct RoomGeneration {
378    index: LossyIndex,
379    cache: Arc<NodeCache>,
380    generation: u64,
381}
382
383/// Session state for the incremental index delta log (`index.delta`).
384///
385/// A fresh checkpoint rewrite re-bases this structure; between rewrites, live
386/// index mutations are recorded as [`crate::index::format::DeltaFrame`]s in
387/// `pending` and appended as a single framed batch by the next `sync()`, so a
388/// dirty sync pays a few-dozen-byte append instead of a multi-megabyte
389/// checkpoint rewrite. A structurally unsafe transition sets `invalid` (see
390/// below), which forces the next sync back to a full rewrite.
391#[derive(Default)]
392struct DeltaLogState {
393    /// Fingerprint of the checkpoint this session continues. `None` when the
394    /// store opened via a full rescan and hasn't rewritten a checkpoint yet —
395    /// every such open forces the next dirty sync to a full rewrite.
396    base_fingerprint: Option<u64>,
397    /// Generation of each collection at the last full checkpoint rewrite. A
398    /// frame is only recorded when the collection's live generation still
399    /// equals this value; bumping a generation (structural change) therefore
400    /// silently stops recording for that collection until the next rewrite.
401    base_generations: HashMap<[u8; 16], u64>,
402    /// Frames accumulated since the last persist (checkpoint rewrite or delta
403    /// append). Cleared on every successful persist.
404    pending: Vec<DeltaFrame>,
405    /// Bytes already committed to the on-disk log (upper bound that a fresh
406    /// header may still need to be written). Resets to zero on rewrite.
407    log_bytes: u64,
408    /// When set, the pending log can no longer be continued correctly —
409    /// capacity growth, full rebuild, repack, refresh, and collection
410    /// create/delete all invalidate it because replay couldn't reconstruct the
411    /// index from the last checkpoint alone. Forces the next full rewrite.
412    invalid: bool,
413}
414
415/// A content-addressed packfile storage engine backed by a global shard pool.
416///
417/// All collections share a small pool of shard files (~4, each ~2GB), keeping
418/// file descriptor usage constant regardless of collection count.
419///
420/// Per-collection state (index + cache) is bundled in an immutable
421/// `RoomGeneration` and swapped atomically via `ArcSwap`:
422///
423/// - **Reads**: `load_full()` once, see a consistent snapshot.
424/// - **Writes**: build new generation under put lock, swap atomically.
425/// - **Delete**: drop the generation — cache disappears with it.
426///
427/// # Timing instrumentation
428///
429/// Every open records a wall-clock breakdown of its phases (and which
430/// index-loading path it took) into [`OpenTimings`], surfaced via
431/// [`PackfileStorage::open_timings`]; every `sync_all` records a per-phase
432/// breakdown into [`SyncTimings`], surfaced via
433/// [`PackfileStorage::sync_timings`]. These exist to decide whether startup
434/// is dominated by deserialization (justifying mmap-ready persisted
435/// indexes) or by validation I/O, and whether sync is dominated by the
436/// checkpoint rewrite — before either format change is built. The cost is a
437/// handful of `Instant::now()` deltas on the open/sync paths only, never
438/// per record.
439///
440/// The phases measured:
441/// - **shard directory discovery/open**: directory scan plus each pack
442///   file's open and (for the writer) writer-lock take.
443/// - **metadata load**: the collection-order and deleted-collections
444///   sidecars.
445/// - **checkpoint decode**: reading + decoding the persisted index
446///   checkpoint from disk.
447/// - **fingerprint**: recomputing the live set of `(pack_id, file_len)`
448///   tuples and hashing them for validation against the checkpoint.
449/// - **index materialization**: deserializing checkpoint index blobs, or
450///   (on the fallback path) building per-collection indexes from a scan.
451/// - **full scan**: the fallback path's packfile scan + torn-tail recovery
452///   (zero on the checkpoint path).
453pub struct PackfileStorage {
454    shards: ShardPool,
455    collections: RwLock<HashMap<[u8; 16], ArcSwap<RoomGeneration>>>,
456    collection_order: RwLock<Vec<[u8; 16]>>,
457    pinned: PinnedNodes,
458    base_dir: PathBuf,
459    swizzle: Option<SwizzleFn>,
460    put_locks: parking_lot::Mutex<HashMap<[u8; 16], Arc<parking_lot::Mutex<()>>>>,
461    /// Serializes the *publication* of a brand-new collection against a
462    /// checkpoint rewrite (see `persist_index_checkpoint`). Creates take this
463    /// lock shared for the whole put; the checkpoint holds it exclusive for
464    /// the fingerprint→snapshot window, so a record whose bytes land on disk
465    /// mid-rewrite can never be fingerprinted without its collection being
466    /// snapshotted in the same checkpoint. Existing-collection puts never
467    /// touch it (their `put_mutex` already gates them), so the shrink-latency
468    /// property the epoch-handoff window exists to protect is untouched.
469    collection_creation: parking_lot::RwLock<()>,
470    /// In-memory cache of the `deleted.collections` file, seeded once at
471    /// `open()` from disk. Guards both the set and the read-modify-write of
472    /// the backing file, so a plain membership check (the common case: most
473    /// collections were never deleted) never touches disk. Collection locks
474    /// protect a collection's lifecycle, but distinct collections may be
475    /// recreated or deleted concurrently, hence the separate lock here.
476    deleted_collections: parking_lot::Mutex<HashSet<[u8; 16]>>,
477    live_roots: RwLock<HashMap<[u8; 16], Vec<NodeId>>>,
478    repack_threshold_entries: AtomicU64,
479    cache_capacity: usize,
480    /// Total number of `repack_collection_reachable` calls across all collections.
481    repack_count: AtomicU64,
482    /// Total records kept (rewritten into the new generation) across all repacks.
483    repack_kept_total: AtomicU64,
484    /// Total records dropped (found unreachable) across all repacks.
485    repack_dropped_total: AtomicU64,
486    /// Per-collection repack counts, so a hot collection's churn is visible individually.
487    repack_counts_by_collection: RwLock<HashMap<[u8; 16], u64>>,
488    /// Per-collection incremental repack state. Each entry tracks the byte
489    /// offset up to which each shard has been scanned for this collection and
490    /// the deduplicated hash → (`shard_id`, offset) map from the last repack.
491    /// On the next repack, only bytes after these offsets are scanned and
492    /// merged into the existing map — turning O(n²) full rescan into
493    /// O(n) total work across all repack calls.
494    repack_incremental: RwLock<HashMap<[u8; 16], RepackIncrementalState>>,
495    /// Persisted directory: which collections have live records in each shard,
496    /// and how many. Maintained incrementally (a plain `put` just
497    /// increments one counter; a full index rebuild/repack/initial scan
498    /// replaces one collection's contribution wholesale via
499    /// `LossyIndex::shard_counts`) rather than ever re-derived by
500    /// scanning a shard file, which is what made `collections_referencing_shard`
501    /// and a `collections`-style listing expensive before this existed.
502    shard_collections: RwLock<HashMap<u64, HashMap<[u8; 16], u64>>>,
503    /// Reverse index of `shard_collections`: which shards a given collection currently
504    /// contributes a nonzero count to. Lets a collection's full-index-rebuild
505    /// path (`replace_collection_shard_counts`) clear exactly the shard entries
506    /// it used to occupy without scanning every shard in `shard_collections`.
507    collection_shards: RwLock<HashMap<[u8; 16], HashSet<u64>>>,
508    /// Wall-clock instant of the last `maybe_persist_shard_collections` flush,
509    /// used to rate-limit that timer-driven path.
510    last_shard_collections_flush: RwLock<Option<std::time::Instant>>,
511    /// Wall-clock breakdown of the most recent `open` — which index-loading
512    /// path was taken and how long each phase took — set at construction.
513    last_open_timings: parking_lot::Mutex<Option<OpenTimings>>,
514    /// Wall-clock breakdown of the most recent `sync_all`, by phase.
515    last_sync_timings: parking_lot::Mutex<Option<SyncTimings>>,
516    /// Gates the logical read-path counters (`get`/`get_many` below). Off by
517    /// default so a store doing no reads-of-record pays one relaxed load per
518    /// logical read at most, and the write/batch/sync counters are the only
519    /// always-on instrumentation (each is a single batch-granular `fetch_add`
520    /// on a write-locked or per-call path, never per-record on the hot read
521    /// path). See [`Self::set_stats_enabled`].
522    stats_enabled: AtomicBool,
523    /// Number of times this class of store was assembled in this process — 1
524    /// for a fresh open. Not reset by `reset_stats` (it counts stores, not work).
525    open_count: AtomicU64,
526
527    // Logical read-path counters. Only incremented while `stats_enabled` is
528    // true; otherwise the read path touches none of these.
529    /// Logical `get` calls (including cache hits and any collection's absence).
530    get_calls: AtomicU64,
531    /// Logical `get` calls that resolved to no record (unknown collection,
532    /// empty index probe, or unresolved candidate).
533    get_misses: AtomicU64,
534    /// Logical `get_many` calls.
535    get_many_calls: AtomicU64,
536    /// Records requested across `get_many` calls.
537    get_many_records: AtomicU64,
538    /// `get_many` results that resolved to no record.
539    get_many_misses: AtomicU64,
540
541    // Always-on write-path counters (per-record `fetch_add` only on the
542    // single-record `put`, whose hot cost is dominated by the write itself).
543    /// Single-record `put` attempts.
544    put_calls: AtomicU64,
545    /// Bytes accepted by single-record `put` attempts.
546    put_bytes: AtomicU64,
547    /// `put_many` calls (batches).
548    put_many_calls: AtomicU64,
549    /// Records written across `put_many` calls.
550    put_many_records: AtomicU64,
551    /// Bytes written across `put_many` calls.
552    put_many_bytes: AtomicU64,
553    /// `put_many` calls that started on the owned, no-clone fast path.
554    put_many_fast_path_calls: AtomicU64,
555    /// `put_many` calls that had to materialize an owned index up front
556    /// (mmap-backed first write, or a brand-new collection).
557    put_many_clone_path_calls: AtomicU64,
558    /// Cumulative nanoseconds spent materializing or growing an owned index in
559    /// `put_many` (batch-granular; drives the steady-append scaler's
560    /// resume-from-checkpoint cost).
561    index_clone_time_ns: AtomicU64,
562    /// Index grow/rebuild events in `put_many` (each invalidates the delta log).
563    index_grow_count: AtomicU64,
564    /// Fallback `rebuild_index` calls (full pack scan).
565    index_rebuild_count: AtomicU64,
566    /// `invalidate_delta_log` calls — structural changes that force the next
567    /// dirty sync onto a full checkpoint rewrite + log re-base.
568    delta_invalidations: AtomicU64,
569    /// `sync`/`sync_all` calls.
570    sync_calls: AtomicU64,
571    /// Syncs that rewrote the index checkpoint in full.
572    checkpoint_writes: AtomicU64,
573    /// Syncs that appended the incremental delta log instead.
574    delta_appends: AtomicU64,
575    /// Whether any collection data changed since the last `index.checkpoint`
576    /// write. Set by every generation swap (`put`/`put_many`/`repack`/`refresh`);
577    /// cleared only by a successful [`Self::persist_index_checkpoint`], so a
578    /// failed write is retried on the next sync. Lets `sync()`/`sync_all()`
579    /// skip rewriting the checkpoint when nothing has changed since the last
580    /// one — a steady-state writer that syncs between writes pays no checkpoint
581    /// cost, while a crash left a stale checkpoint is still always resolved by
582    /// the fingerprint → rescan fallback.
583    index_checkpoint_dirty: AtomicBool,
584    /// Session state for the incremental index delta log: the base checkpoint
585    /// fingerprint + generations the log continues, and the frames accumulated
586    /// since the last persist. See [`DeltaLogState`].
587    delta_state: parking_lot::Mutex<DeltaLogState>,
588}
589
590/// Per-collection state for incremental repack.
591#[derive(Clone)]
592struct RepackIncrementalState {
593    /// Per-shard byte offset: next scan starts here (file length at end
594    /// of last scan). Shards not in this map haven't been scanned yet.
595    // A slot can be retired and reused for a different pack.  Keep the
596    // pack_id beside the cursor so an offset from the old incarnation is
597    // never applied to the replacement file.
598    scan_offsets: HashMap<u16, (u64, u64)>,
599    /// The deduplicated hash → (`shard_id`, offset) map from the last repack.
600    /// The next repack merges newly-scanned entries into this map.
601    live_map: HashMap<[u8; 16], (u16, u64)>,
602    /// Cached edge lists for every node that survived the last repack.
603    /// Lets the BFS and full-scan adjacency walks skip re-reading disk for
604    /// nodes that were already seen — turning O(total) disk reads per repack
605    /// call into O(delta) reads for only nodes added since the last repack.
606    adjacency: HashMap<[u8; 16], Vec<[u8; 16]>>,
607    /// The live-roots snapshot from the last repack. Used to identify which
608    /// roots are genuinely new so the incremental BFS can seed only those,
609    /// rather than re-walking the entire reachable set from scratch.
610    prev_roots: Vec<[u8; 16]>,
611}
612
613const DEFAULT_REPACK_THRESHOLD_ENTRIES: u64 = 2048;
614const DEFAULT_CACHE_CAPACITY: usize = 100_000;
615
616/// Minimum `LossyIndex` capacity for a brand-new collection's first
617/// insert (`put` and `put_many`), in slots (8 bytes each).
618///
619/// Not the index's own 16-slot hard floor: at the ~75% load factor
620/// `grow()` targets, 16 slots admits only ~12 entries before the next
621/// insert forces a grow -- and every `grow()` invalidates the delta log
622/// (see its doc comment), forcing the next sync to do a full checkpoint
623/// rewrite instead of a cheap delta append. A workload that lands entries
624/// one at a time into a new collection (e.g. events arriving
625/// individually) would trade checkpoint bloat for far more frequent full
626/// rewrites. 64 slots (512 B) covers a "handful of records" collection
627/// without ever growing, while remaining ~64x smaller than the old flat
628/// 4096-slot (32 KiB) floor this replaced.
629const NEW_COLLECTION_INDEX_FLOOR: usize = 64;
630
631impl PackfileStorage {
632    /// Open a packfile storage with default settings.
633    ///
634    /// # Errors
635    /// Returns `io::Error` if the base directory cannot be created or read.
636    pub fn open(base_dir: PathBuf) -> Result<Self, std::io::Error> {
637        Self::open_with_options(
638            base_dir,
639            DEFAULT_CACHE_CAPACITY,
640            None,
641            true,
642            None,
643            true,
644            packfile::ChecksumPolicy::Full,
645        )
646    }
647
648    /// Open a packfile storage with explicit compression and checksum
649    /// policies — the advanced durability/performance entry point. See
650    /// [`crate::packfile::ChecksumPolicy`] for what giving up read-time
651    /// verification costs.
652    ///
653    /// # Errors
654    /// Same as [`Self::open`].
655    pub fn open_with_policies(
656        base_dir: PathBuf,
657        compress: bool,
658        checksum_policy: packfile::ChecksumPolicy,
659    ) -> Result<Self, std::io::Error> {
660        Self::open_with_options(
661            base_dir,
662            DEFAULT_CACHE_CAPACITY,
663            None,
664            true,
665            None,
666            compress,
667            checksum_policy,
668        )
669    }
670
671    /// Open a writable packfile storage with explicit cache, compression, and
672    /// checksum policies.
673    ///
674    /// `cache_capacity` applies independently to each collection; pass zero
675    /// to disable decoded-node caching entirely.
676    ///
677    /// # Errors
678    /// Same as [`Self::open`].
679    pub fn open_with_cache_and_policies(
680        base_dir: PathBuf,
681        cache_capacity: usize,
682        compress: bool,
683        checksum_policy: packfile::ChecksumPolicy,
684    ) -> Result<Self, std::io::Error> {
685        Self::open_with_options(
686            base_dir,
687            cache_capacity,
688            None,
689            true,
690            None,
691            compress,
692            checksum_policy,
693        )
694    }
695
696    /// Open a packfile storage with `compress` controlling whether records
697    /// are zstd-attempted on write (see
698    /// [`crate::packfile::write_record_with_options`]) — pass `false` for a
699    /// pool whose payloads (e.g. HAMT nodes/roots) never benefit, to skip
700    /// paying the compressor's cost on every put.
701    ///
702    /// # Errors
703    /// Same as [`Self::open`].
704    pub fn open_with_compression(
705        base_dir: PathBuf,
706        compress: bool,
707    ) -> Result<Self, std::io::Error> {
708        Self::open_with_options(
709            base_dir,
710            DEFAULT_CACHE_CAPACITY,
711            None,
712            true,
713            None,
714            compress,
715            packfile::ChecksumPolicy::Full,
716        )
717    }
718
719    /// Open a packfile storage that rotates shards at `max_shard_bytes`
720    /// instead of the default `~256 MB` ceiling. Intended for benchmarks
721    /// and tests that want many small packs without writing gigabytes of
722    /// data to trigger rotation — see
723    /// [`crate::shard::ShardPool::open_with_max_shard_bytes`].
724    ///
725    /// # Errors
726    /// Returns `io::Error` if the base directory cannot be created or
727    /// read, or if `max_shard_bytes` is zero or exceeds
728    /// [`crate::shard::MAX_SHARD_BYTES`].
729    pub fn open_with_max_shard_bytes(
730        base_dir: PathBuf,
731        max_shard_bytes: u64,
732    ) -> Result<Self, std::io::Error> {
733        Self::open_with_options(
734            base_dir,
735            DEFAULT_CACHE_CAPACITY,
736            None,
737            true,
738            Some(max_shard_bytes),
739            true,
740            packfile::ChecksumPolicy::Full,
741        )
742    }
743
744    /// Open a packfile storage as a read-only observer, coexisting with a
745    /// concurrent writer on the same directory (see
746    /// `ShardPool::open_read_only`'s doc for the locking contract).
747    ///
748    /// Intended for inspection tooling (e.g. a `shards`/`collections`/`info` CLI
749    /// command) that needs to run alongside a live writer process without
750    /// either racing its on-disk state or being mistaken for a second
751    /// writer and rejected.

753    /// # Errors
754    /// Returns `io::Error` if the directory can't be read, has no shards
755    /// yet, or a writer already holds the exclusive lock.
756    pub fn open_read_only(base_dir: PathBuf) -> Result<Self, std::io::Error> {
757        Self::open_with_options(
758            base_dir,
759            DEFAULT_CACHE_CAPACITY,
760            None,
761            false,
762            None,
763            true,
764            packfile::ChecksumPolicy::Full,
765        )
766    }

768    /// Open a packfile storage as a read-only observer with an explicit
769    /// checksum policy, gating per-lookup CRC verification in `read_at`.
770    /// See [`crate::packfile::ChecksumPolicy`]. Matches a writer opened with
771    /// the same policy.
772    ///
773    /// # Errors

776        base_dir: PathBuf,

784            None,
785            true,
786            checksum_policy,
787        )
788    }

833    /// Open a packfile storage with a swizzle callback for in-cache pointer resolution.
834    ///
835    /// # Errors
836    /// Returns `io::Error` if the base directory cannot be created or read.
837    pub fn open_with_swizzle(
838        base_dir: PathBuf,
839        cache_capacity: usize,
840        swizzle: SwizzleFn,
841    ) -> Result<Self, std::io::Error> {
842        Self::open_with_options(
843            base_dir,

852
853    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
854    #[allow(clippy::too_many_lines)]
855    fn open_with_options(
856        base_dir: PathBuf,
857        cache_capacity: usize,
858        swizzle: Option<SwizzleFn>,
859        writable: bool,
860        max_shard_bytes: Option<u64>,
861        compress: bool,
862        checksum_policy: packfile::ChecksumPolicy,
863    ) -> Result<Self, std::io::Error> {
864        fs::create_dir_all(&base_dir)?;
865
866        let started = std::time::Instant::now();
867        let mut timings = OpenTimings::default();
868
869        let shard_open_started = std::time::Instant::now();
870        let shards = if writable {
871            match max_shard_bytes {
872                // Custom rotation thresholds are only used by benchmarks/
873                // tests today, none of which also need to disable
874                // compression, so this combination just keeps the default.
875                Some(max_shard_bytes) => ShardPool::open_with_policies(
876                    base_dir.clone(),
877                    compress,
878                    checksum_policy,
879                    Some(max_shard_bytes),
880                )?,
881                None => ShardPool::open_with_policies(
882                    base_dir.clone(),
883                    compress,
884                    checksum_policy,
885                    None,
886                )?,
887            }
888        } else {
889            ShardPool::open_read_only_with_policies(base_dir.clone(), checksum_policy)?
890        };
891        timings.shard_open = shard_open_started.elapsed();
892
893        // Phase 1: accumulate all records per collection across every shard so
894        // the index can be sized once for the true total.
895        let mut collection_entries: HashMap<[u8; 16], Vec<ShardRecord>> = HashMap::new();
896        let metadata_started = std::time::Instant::now();
897        // Seed logical insertion order before scanning. A newly written collection
898        // cannot appear in the sidecar until the next flush, so append it at
899        // first physical sight below. Legacy v1 sidecars yield no order and
900        // therefore use the same first-seen fallback for every collection.
901        let mut collection_order = persisted_collection_order(&base_dir).unwrap_or_default();
902        let mut known_collections: HashSet<[u8; 16]> = collection_order.iter().copied().collect();
903
904        // Collect open shard info (slot, pack_id, path, length) before the scan
905        // loop so we don't hold the shards read-lock across the I/O-heavy scan.
906        // The lengths feed the checkpoint fingerprint (see `index::checkpoint`).
907        let open_shards: Vec<(u16, u64, PathBuf, u64)> = shards
908            .all_shards()
909            .into_iter()
910            .map(|(id, shard)| (id, shard.pack_id, shard.path.clone(), shard.file_len()))
911            .collect();
912
913        // A checkpoint from the previous session's last sync lets us skip the
914        // rescan and index rebuild entirely. It is only trusted when every
915        // pack on disk still matches the fingerprint it was written against.
916        let deleted_collections = Self::load_deleted_collections(&base_dir);
917        timings.metadata_load = metadata_started.elapsed();
918        if let Some((scan_out, collection_order, delta_state)) = Self::checkpoint_scan_out(
919            &base_dir,
920            cache_capacity,
921            &shards,
922            &open_shards,
923            &deleted_collections,
924            writable,
925            &mut timings,
926        ) {
927            timings.path = OpenPath::Checkpoint;
928            let store = Self::assemble(
929                shards,
930                scan_out,
931                collection_order,
932                deleted_collections,
933                base_dir,
934                swizzle,
935                cache_capacity,
936                delta_state,
937            );
938            timings.total = started.elapsed();
939            *store.last_open_timings.lock() = Some(timings);
940            return Ok(store);
941        }
942        timings.path = OpenPath::FullScan;
943        // The checkpoint/log gates rejected the persisted index state; never
944        // carry forward a stale log into the rescanned session — a writable
945        // open can delete it outright (read-only opens leave the file alone
946        // but will re-rescan, since the damaged bases can never trust it).
947        if writable {
948            Self::sweep_orphan_delta_logs(&base_dir, None);
949        }
950
951        let scan_started = std::time::Instant::now();
952        for (shard_id, shard_pack_id, path, _file_len) in open_shards {
953            // A read-only open must never touch the file at all — the
954            // truncating recovery scan below is only safe when we're the
955            // sole writer (guaranteed by the writer lock); a read-only
956            // opener can run concurrently with an active writer (no lock
957            // taken at all — see ShardPool::open_read_only), so it must
958            // use the non-mutating scan_packfile, which just stops
959            // cleanly at a torn tail (indistinguishable from a writer's
960            // in-flight append) instead of truncating it away.
961            let entries = if writable {
962                // Recovery can safely truncate only a torn final frame. Any
963                // other failure (CRC mismatch, invalid framing, permissions)
964                // means this pack cannot be indexed faithfully; fail open
965                // rather than publish a store that silently omitted it.
966                packfile::scan_and_recover_packfile(&path).map_err(|error| {
967                    std::io::Error::new(
968                        error.kind(),
969                        format!("recovery scan failed for pack {}: {error}", path.display()),
970                    )
971                })?
972            } else {
973                // Read-only mode must not truncate a possibly in-flight tail,
974                // but it also cannot safely omit a shard.  Publishing a
975                // partial index after an I/O or corruption failure makes real
976                // records look deleted.  Surface the error and let the caller
977                // retry after the writer has finished or repair the pack.
978                packfile::scan_packfile(&path).map_err(|error| {
979                    std::io::Error::new(
980                        error.kind(),
981                        format!(
982                            "read-only scan failed for shard {shard_id:02x} ({}): {error}",
983                            path.display()
984                        ),
985                    )
986                })?
987            };
988            for (collection_id, hash, offset) in entries {
989                if known_collections.insert(collection_id) {
990                    collection_order.push(collection_id);
991                }
992                collection_entries.entry(collection_id).or_default().push((
993                    shard_id,
994                    hash,
995                    offset,
996                    shard_pack_id,
997                ));
998            }
999        }
1000        collection_order.retain(|collection_id| collection_entries.contains_key(collection_id));
1001        timings.full_scan = scan_started.elapsed();
1002
1003        let mut scan_out = RoomScanOutput::default();
1004
1005        // Phase 2: build per-collection indexes sized to the true total.
1006        let materialization_started = std::time::Instant::now();
1007        for collection_id in &collection_order {
1008            if deleted_collections.contains(collection_id) {
1009                continue;
1010            }
1011            Self::init_collection_from_scan(
1012                collection_id,
1013                &collection_entries[collection_id],
1014                &shards,
1015                cache_capacity,
1016                &mut scan_out,
1017            )?;
1018        }
1019        timings.index_materialization = materialization_started.elapsed();
1020
1021        let store = Self::assemble(
1022            shards,
1023            scan_out,
1024            collection_order,
1025            deleted_collections,
1026            base_dir,
1027            swizzle,
1028            cache_capacity,
1029            // A full rescan re-cooks every index from scratch, so no delta log
1030            // can continue anything the checkpoint recorded; force the next
1031            // dirty sync into a full checkpoint rewrite that re-bases the log.
1032            DeltaLogState {
1033                invalid: true,
1034                ..DeltaLogState::default()
1035            },
1036        );
1037        timings.total = started.elapsed();
1038        *store.last_open_timings.lock() = Some(timings);
1039        Ok(store)
1040    }
1041
1042    /// Builds one collection's index, cache, and shard-directory contribution
1043    /// from its scanned records, and inserts all three into `out` — the
1044    /// per-collection body of `open_with_options`'s phase 2, factored out to
1045    /// keep that function under the line-count lint rather than
1046    /// suppressing it.
1047    fn init_collection_from_scan(
1048        collection_id: &[u8; 16],
1049        records: &[ShardRecord],
1050        shards: &ShardPool,
1051        cache_capacity: usize,
1052        out: &mut RoomScanOutput,
1053    ) -> Result<(), StorageError> {
1054        // Seed each collection's home shard from the scan: the shard of its
1055        // last-scanned record is a best-effort proxy for "most recent"
1056        // (shards are scanned in ascending ID order, and IDs generally
1057        // increase over time via rotation) — not exact chronology across
1058        // shards, but enough to keep a resumed collection's writes landing near
1059        // its existing data instead of restarting at whatever the pool's
1060        // active shard happens to be.
1061        if let Some(&(last_shard_id, _, _, _)) = records.last() {
1062            shards.set_collection_home(collection_id, last_shard_id);
1063        }
1064        let index = LossyIndex::new(
1065            records
1066                .len()
1067                .saturating_mul(2)
1068                .max(NEW_COLLECTION_INDEX_FLOOR),
1069        );
1070        for (shard_id, hash, offset, _pack_id) in records {
1071            check_index_offset(*shard_id, hash, *offset)?;
1072            let _ = index.insert(hash, *shard_id, *offset);
1073        }
1074        let counts = index.shard_counts();
1075
1076        // Build slot→pack_id lookup from the records for this collection.
1077        let slot_to_pack_id: HashMap<u16, u64> = records
1078            .iter()
1079            .map(|(slot, _, _, pack_id)| (*slot, *pack_id))
1080            .collect();
1081
1082        for (&shard_id, &count) in &counts {
1083            let pack_id = slot_to_pack_id
1084                .get(&shard_id)
1085                .copied()
1086                .unwrap_or(u64::from(shard_id));
1087            out.shard_collections
1088                .entry(pack_id)
1089                .or_default()
1090                .insert(*collection_id, count);
1091            out.collection_shards
1092                .entry(*collection_id)
1093                .or_default()
1094                .insert(pack_id);
1095        }
1096        out.collections.insert(
1097            *collection_id,
1098            ArcSwap::from_pointee(RoomGeneration {
1099                index,
1100                cache: Arc::new(NodeCache::new(cache_capacity)),
1101                generation: 1,
1102            }),
1103        );
1104        Ok(())
1105    }
1106
1107    /// Assemble a fully constructed store from the per-collection state both
1108    /// the rescan path and the checkpoint fast path produce, so the two share
1109    /// one field-for-field constructor.
1110    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
1111    fn assemble(
1112        shards: ShardPool,
1113        scan_out: RoomScanOutput,
1114        collection_order: Vec<[u8; 16]>,
1115        deleted_collections: HashSet<[u8; 16]>,
1116        base_dir: PathBuf,
1117        swizzle: Option<SwizzleFn>,
1118        cache_capacity: usize,
1119        delta_state: DeltaLogState,
1120    ) -> Self {
1121        Self {
1122            shards,
1123            collections: RwLock::new(scan_out.collections),
1124            collection_order: RwLock::new(collection_order),
1125            pinned: PinnedNodes::new(),
1126            base_dir,
1127            swizzle,
1128            put_locks: parking_lot::Mutex::new(HashMap::new()),
1129            collection_creation: parking_lot::RwLock::new(()),
1130            deleted_collections: parking_lot::Mutex::new(deleted_collections),
1131            live_roots: RwLock::new(HashMap::new()),
1132            repack_threshold_entries: AtomicU64::new(DEFAULT_REPACK_THRESHOLD_ENTRIES),
1133            cache_capacity,
1134            repack_count: AtomicU64::new(0),
1135            repack_kept_total: AtomicU64::new(0),
1136            repack_dropped_total: AtomicU64::new(0),
1137            repack_counts_by_collection: RwLock::new(HashMap::new()),
1138            repack_incremental: RwLock::new(HashMap::new()),
1139            shard_collections: RwLock::new(scan_out.shard_collections),
1140            collection_shards: RwLock::new(scan_out.collection_shards),
1141            last_shard_collections_flush: RwLock::new(None),
1142            index_checkpoint_dirty: AtomicBool::new(false),
1143            last_open_timings: parking_lot::Mutex::new(None),
1144            last_sync_timings: parking_lot::Mutex::new(None),
1145            stats_enabled: AtomicBool::new(false),
1146            open_count: AtomicU64::new(1),
1147            get_calls: AtomicU64::new(0),
1148            get_misses: AtomicU64::new(0),
1149            get_many_calls: AtomicU64::new(0),
1150            get_many_records: AtomicU64::new(0),
1151            get_many_misses: AtomicU64::new(0),
1152            put_calls: AtomicU64::new(0),
1153            put_bytes: AtomicU64::new(0),
1154            put_many_calls: AtomicU64::new(0),
1155            put_many_records: AtomicU64::new(0),
1156            put_many_bytes: AtomicU64::new(0),
1157            put_many_fast_path_calls: AtomicU64::new(0),
1158            put_many_clone_path_calls: AtomicU64::new(0),
1159            index_clone_time_ns: AtomicU64::new(0),
1160            index_grow_count: AtomicU64::new(0),
1161            index_rebuild_count: AtomicU64::new(0),
1162            delta_invalidations: AtomicU64::new(0),
1163            sync_calls: AtomicU64::new(0),
1164            checkpoint_writes: AtomicU64::new(0),
1165            delta_appends: AtomicU64::new(0),
1166            delta_state: parking_lot::Mutex::new(delta_state),
1167        }
1168    }
1169
1170    /// Path of this store's persisted-index checkpoint.
1171    fn index_checkpoint_path(base_dir: &std::path::Path) -> PathBuf {
1172        base_dir.join(crate::index::checkpoint::INDEX_CHECKPOINT_FILE)
1173    }
1174
1175    /// Path of one *epoch* of this store's incremental index delta log.
1176    ///
1177    /// Named by the checkpoint fingerprint it continues (`index.delta.<hex
1178    /// fingerprint>`) rather than a single fixed name: the epoch-handoff
1179    /// protocol (see `persist_index_checkpoint`) needs the about-to-be-retired
1180    /// epoch (D0, continuing the old checkpoint) and the freshly rotated one
1181    /// (D1, continuing the new one) to coexist on disk as distinct files
1182    /// while the new checkpoint is serialized and fsynced unlocked. Naming by
1183    /// fingerprint also *is* the reopen gate: a loader only ever looks at the
1184    /// epoch whose name matches the checkpoint's own `pack_fingerprint`, so
1185    /// a leftover D0 (crash before retirement) or an orphaned D1 (crash
1186    /// before the checkpoint that would have validated it) are both
1187    /// automatically ignored without any extra bookkeeping.
1188    fn delta_path(base_dir: &std::path::Path, fingerprint: u64) -> PathBuf {
1189        base_dir.join(format!("{INDEX_DELTA_FILE}.{fingerprint:016x}"))
1190    }
1191
1192    /// Remove every on-disk delta-log epoch file except `keep` (pass `None`
1193    /// to remove all of them, e.g. after a full rescan with no checkpoint to
1194    /// continue). Best-effort and silent on failure — an orphaned epoch file
1195    /// left behind is always safe: it is either identical to the kept one
1196    /// (redundant) or has a fingerprint that can never match the current
1197    /// checkpoint again (inert). Also opportunistically removes the single
1198    /// fixed-name log from before epoch-named logs existed; it is never read
1199    /// by this version, so leaving it around would just be clutter.
1200    fn sweep_orphan_delta_logs(base_dir: &std::path::Path, keep: Option<&std::path::Path>) {
1201        let _ = fs::remove_file(base_dir.join(INDEX_DELTA_FILE));
1202        let Ok(read_dir) = fs::read_dir(base_dir) else {
1203            return;
1204        };
1205        let prefix = format!("{INDEX_DELTA_FILE}.");
1206        for entry in read_dir.flatten() {
1207            let path = entry.path();
1208            if keep.is_some_and(|keep| keep == path) {
1209                continue;
1210            }
1211            let Some(name) = path.file_name().and_then(|n| n.to_str()) else {
1212                continue;
1213            };
1214            if name.starts_with(&prefix) {
1215                let _ = fs::remove_file(&path);
1216            }
1217        }
1218    }
1219
1220    /// Attempt to recover the per-shard/collection bookkeeping from the
1221    /// inspection sidecar, gated on the checkpoint's own pack fingerprint.
1222    ///
1223    /// Returns `BookkeepingSource::Sidecar` plus the slotted counts (for home
1224    /// shard selection) and pack-keyed entries (for the shard directory) only
1225    /// when the sidecar is gated to this exact pack set and describes every
1226    /// non-deleted collection completely — per-pack counts must sum to the
1227    /// checkpoint's own slot count. Stale, corrupt, or absent sidecars (or
1228    /// one referencing packs that aren't live open shards) fall through to
1229    /// `BookkeepingSource::SlotScan` with empty maps: correctness never
1230    /// depends on the sidecar, it only accelerates.
1231    #[allow(clippy::type_complexity)]
1232    fn gated_sidecar_bookkeeping(
1233        base_dir: &std::path::Path,
1234        local_fingerprint: u64,
1235        current_len: &HashMap<[u8; 16], u32>,
1236        open_shards: &[(u16, u64, PathBuf, u64)],
1237        deleted_collections: &HashSet<[u8; 16]>,
1238    ) -> (BookkeepingSource, HashMap<[u8; 16], HashMap<u16, u64>>) {
1239        let mut counts: HashMap<[u8; 16], HashMap<u16, u64>> = HashMap::new();
1240        let Some(directory) = read_persisted_shard_collections(base_dir) else {
1241            return (BookkeepingSource::SlotScan, counts);
1242        };
1243        if directory.fingerprint != local_fingerprint {
1244            return (BookkeepingSource::SlotScan, counts);
1245        }
1246        let pack_to_slot: HashMap<u64, u16> = open_shards
1247            .iter()
1248            .map(|(slot, pack_id, _, _)| (*pack_id, *slot))
1249            .collect();
1250        for record in &directory.records {
1251            if deleted_collections.contains(&record.collection_id) {
1252                continue;
1253            }
1254            let Some(slot) = pack_to_slot.get(&record.pack_id) else {
1255                return (BookkeepingSource::SlotScan, counts);
1256            };
1257            counts
1258                .entry(record.collection_id)
1259                .or_default()
1260                .insert(*slot, record.count);
1261        }
1262        for (collection_id, len) in current_len {
1263            if deleted_collections.contains(collection_id) {
1264                continue;
1265            }
1266            let Some(slot_counts) = counts.get(collection_id) else {
1267                return (BookkeepingSource::SlotScan, counts);
1268            };
1269            if slot_counts.values().copied().sum::<u64>() != u64::from(*len) {
1270                return (BookkeepingSource::SlotScan, counts);
1271            }
1272        }
1273        (BookkeepingSource::Sidecar, counts)
1274    }
1275
1276    /// Fast-path index loading for `open_with_options`: build the
1277    /// per-collection state directly from the persisted-index checkpoint
1278    /// instead of rescanning every packfile.
1279    ///
1280    /// Returns `None` — falling through to the full rescan — for a missing,
1281    /// malformed, or stale checkpoint, one that doesn't describe the pack set
1282    /// currently on disk, or a delta log that can't be replayed onto the
1283    /// checkpoint. The fast path deliberately skips the torn-tail recovery
1284    /// scan, which is safe: a fingerprint match means the packs are the exact
1285    /// state the previous session's last sync fsynced, so there can be no torn
1286    /// tail to recover.
1287    ///
1288    /// On success, also returns the fresh [`DeltaLogState`] for the session: the
1289    /// delta log's base fingerprint (the checkpoint's) and each collection's
1290    /// checkpoint generation, with `invalid` set only if a structural change
1291    /// (e.g. a grow) forced replay to bail into a rescan — in which case this
1292    /// function returns `None`, and the rescan path constructs the
1293    /// invalidated state instead.
1294    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
1295    #[allow(clippy::too_many_lines)]
1296    fn checkpoint_scan_out(
1297        base_dir: &std::path::Path,
1298        cache_capacity: usize,
1299        shards: &ShardPool,
1300        open_shards: &[(u16, u64, PathBuf, u64)],
1301        deleted_collections: &HashSet<[u8; 16]>,
1302        writable: bool,
1303        timings: &mut OpenTimings,
1304    ) -> Option<(RoomScanOutput, Vec<[u8; 16]>, DeltaLogState)> {
1305        let decode_started = std::time::Instant::now();
1306        let checkpoint =
1307            crate::index::checkpoint::read_checkpoint(&Self::index_checkpoint_path(base_dir));
1308        timings.checkpoint_decode = decode_started.elapsed();
1309        let checkpoint = checkpoint?;
1310        let fingerprint_started = std::time::Instant::now();
1311        let packs: Vec<(u64, u64)> = open_shards
1312            .iter()
1313            .map(|(_, pack_id, _, file_len)| (*pack_id, *file_len))
1314            .collect();
1315        let local_fingerprint = crate::index::checkpoint::pack_fingerprint(&packs);
1316        timings.fingerprint = fingerprint_started.elapsed();
1317
1318        // Generations at the checkpoint, per collection — used both to seed the
1319        // new session's `DeltaLogState` and to gate whether any delta frames may be
1320        // applied (a frame's stamp must equal the checkpoint generation it
1321        // claims to continue, which also rules out the never-recorded ancestors
1322        // of collections created or structurally changed since the checkpoint).
1323        let ckpt_generations: HashMap<[u8; 16], u64> = checkpoint
1324            .collections
1325            .iter()
1326            .filter(|loaded| !deleted_collections.contains(&loaded.collection_id))
1327            .map(|loaded| (loaded.collection_id, loaded.generation))
1328            .collect();
1329
1330        let delta_path = Self::delta_path(base_dir, checkpoint.fingerprint);
1331        // Only the epoch named for this exact checkpoint can ever be trusted
1332        // (see `delta_path`); anything else on disk — a stale predecessor
1333        // epoch left by a crash between checkpoint rename and retirement, or
1334        // an orphaned successor epoch from a rewrite that never completed —
1335        // is inert by construction, but sweep it now on a writable open so
1336        // it doesn't accumulate across sessions.
1337        if writable {
1338            Self::sweep_orphan_delta_logs(base_dir, Some(&delta_path));
1339        }
1340        // A delta log can only continue a checkpoint whose packs have advanced
1341        // (every append accompanies a flush that grows a pack, changing the
1342        // fingerprint). When the packs still match the checkpoint exactly,
1343        // any leftover log is stale — likely a crashed full rewrite that had
1344        // already committed the new checkpoint but not yet deleted its
1345        // predecessor log. Never replayed; removed on a writable open.
1346        let replay_needed = local_fingerprint != checkpoint.fingerprint;
1347        if !replay_needed && writable {
1348            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&delta_path);
1349        }
1350        // The new session continues whatever log is genuinely on disk — its
1351        // committed byte length must be inherited, not zeroed: a later append
1352        // decides whether to write a fresh base header from `log_bytes == 0`,
1353        // and re-headering an existing log would corrupt the batch framing the
1354        // next reader depends on. Zero only when the open removed the file.
1355        let mut log_bytes_on_disk = 0u64;
1356
1357        // Gate the log (case B only): it must start at this checkpoint and end
1358        // at the exact pack set now on disk, and every committed frame must
1359        // target a checkpointed collection at its recorded checkpoint
1360        // generation. Any failure rejects the log wholesale and falls through
1361        // to the rescan — never a partial replay.
1362        let mut replay_frames: Vec<DeltaFrame> = Vec::new();
1363        if replay_needed {
1364            let delta_started = std::time::Instant::now();
1365            let log = delta::read_delta_log(&delta_path);
1366            let trusted = log.as_ref().is_some_and(|log| {
1367                log.base_fingerprint == checkpoint.fingerprint
1368                    && log.tail_fingerprint == local_fingerprint
1369                    && log.frames.iter().all(|frame| {
1370                        ckpt_generations.get(&frame.collection_id) == Some(&frame.generation)
1371                    })
1372            });
1373            if trusted {
1374                // The byte frontier travels with the decode (no re-stat):
1375                // `file_len` is where the last committed trailer ends, which is
1376                // exactly where the next append must continue.
1377                let trusted_log = log.expect("trusted implies a decoded log");
1378                log_bytes_on_disk = trusted_log.file_len;
1379                replay_frames = trusted_log.frames;
1380            } else {
1381                timings.delta_replay = delta_started.elapsed();
1382                if writable {
1383                    let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&delta_path);
1384                }
1385                return None;
1386            }
1387            timings.delta_replay = delta_started.elapsed();
1388        }
1389
1390        // Group the (gated) frames by target collection once, so the
1391        // materialization loop below applies each collection's frames by hash
1392        // lookup rather than re-scanning the whole frame list per collection.
1393        let mut frames_by_collection: HashMap<[u8; 16], Vec<DeltaFrame>> = HashMap::new();
1394        for frame in &replay_frames {
1395            frames_by_collection
1396                .entry(frame.collection_id)
1397                .or_default()
1398                .push(*frame);
1399        }
1400
1401        let slot_to_pack_id: HashMap<u16, u64> = open_shards
1402            .iter()
1403            .map(|(slot, pack_id, _, _)| (*slot, *pack_id))
1404            .collect();
1405
1406        // The inspection sidecar is the same reduced per-(pack, collection)
1407        // bookkeeping the loop below would recover by walking every slot of
1408        // every collection index. Accept it only when it is gated to the pack
1409        // set now on disk and describes every non-deleted collection completely
1410        // (see `gated_sidecar_bookkeeping`); any mismatch falls through to the
1411        // faithful slot walk, because the sidecar is an acceleration and
1412        // correctness never depends on it.
1413        //
1414        // Gating needs each collection's post-replay length (the checkpoint
1415        // occupancy plus whatever fresh-slot writes the replayed frames add),
1416        // so the sidecar check runs after materialization below.
1417        let mut scan_out = RoomScanOutput::default();
1418        let mut collection_order = Vec::with_capacity(checkpoint.collections.len());
1419        let mut current_len: HashMap<[u8; 16], u32> =
1420            HashMap::with_capacity(checkpoint.collections.len());
1421        let materialization_started = std::time::Instant::now();
1422        for loaded in &checkpoint.collections {
1423            if deleted_collections.contains(&loaded.collection_id) {
1424                // The checkpoint may predate the deletion marker; the
1425                // logical-delete set is authoritative.
1426                continue;
1427            }
1428            // The checkpoint reader has already validated this range. Keep it
1429            // mmap-backed through the read-only fast path; the first writer
1430            // copy-on-writes it into the normal atomic slot array.
1431            let mmap_index = LossyIndex::from_mmap_slots(
1432                Arc::clone(&checkpoint.mmap),
1433                loaded.slots_offset,
1434                loaded.capacity,
1435                loaded.slot_count,
1436            );
1437            // Collections with replayed frames must be materialized (owned) so
1438            // the frames can be applied on top of the raw checkpoint slots;
1439            // everything else stays an O(1) mmap view.
1440            let index = if let Some(frames) = frames_by_collection.get(&loaded.collection_id) {
1441                let owned = mmap_index.clone();
1442                if owned.replay_frames(frames).is_err() {
1443                    // Structurally inconsistent with the checkpoint despite
1444                    // passing the fingerprint/generation gates (e.g. a frame
1445                    // bucket past this checkpoint's capacity). It can't be
1446                    // replayed; fall through to the rescan.
1447                    if writable {
1448                        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&delta_path);
1449                    }
1450                    return None;
1451                }
1452                owned
1453            } else {
1454                mmap_index
1455            };
1456            current_len.insert(loaded.collection_id, u32::try_from(index.len()).ok()?);
1457            scan_out.collections.insert(
1458                loaded.collection_id,
1459                ArcSwap::from_pointee(RoomGeneration {
1460                    index,
1461                    cache: Arc::new(NodeCache::new(cache_capacity)),
1462                    generation: loaded.generation,
1463                }),
1464            );
1465            collection_order.push(loaded.collection_id);
1466        }
1467        timings.index_materialization = materialization_started.elapsed();
1468
1469        let (bookkeeping_source, sidecar_counts) = Self::gated_sidecar_bookkeeping(
1470            base_dir,
1471            local_fingerprint,
1472            &current_len,
1473            open_shards,
1474            deleted_collections,
1475        );
1476        timings.bookkeeping_source = bookkeeping_source;
1477
1478        // Bookkeeping (home-shard seeding + shard directory) from the sidecar
1479        // where that passed its gates, otherwise a slot walk of the — possibly
1480        // replayed — live indexes.
1481        let counts_lookup: HashMap<[u8; 16], HashMap<u16, u64>> = match bookkeeping_source {
1482            BookkeepingSource::Sidecar => sidecar_counts,
1483            BookkeepingSource::SlotScan => {
1484                let mut out = HashMap::with_capacity(collection_order.len());
1485                for collection_id in &collection_order {
1486                    if let Some(gen) = scan_out.collections.get(collection_id) {
1487                        out.insert(*collection_id, gen.load().index.shard_counts());
1488                    }
1489                }
1490                out
1491            }
1492        };
1493        for collection_id in &collection_order {
1494            let counts = &counts_lookup[collection_id];
1495            // Seed the home shard from the highest shard a slot references —
1496            // the nearest proxy for the scan path's "shard of the last
1497            // record", since slots were appended in shard-id order.
1498            if let Some(&home_shard) = counts.keys().max() {
1499                shards.set_collection_home(collection_id, home_shard);
1500            }
1501            for (&shard_id, &count) in counts {
1502                let pack_id = slot_to_pack_id
1503                    .get(&shard_id)
1504                    .copied()
1505                    .unwrap_or(u64::from(shard_id));
1506                scan_out
1507                    .shard_collections
1508                    .entry(pack_id)
1509                    .or_default()
1510                    .insert(*collection_id, count);
1511                scan_out
1512                    .collection_shards
1513                    .entry(*collection_id)
1514                    .or_default()
1515                    .insert(pack_id);
1516            }
1517        }
1518        // `SlotScan` derives counts straight from the live indexes; the
1519        // sidecar's `counts` are consumed above either way.
1520
1521        let delta_state = DeltaLogState {
1522            base_fingerprint: Some(checkpoint.fingerprint),
1523            base_generations: ckpt_generations,
1524            log_bytes: log_bytes_on_disk,
1525            invalid: false,
1526            ..DeltaLogState::default()
1527        };
1528
1529        Some((scan_out, collection_order, delta_state))
1530    }
1531
1532    fn generation(&self, collection_id: &[u8; 16]) -> Option<arc_swap::Guard<Arc<RoomGeneration>>> {
1533        self.collections
1534            .read()
1535            .get(collection_id)
1536            .map(arc_swap::ArcSwapAny::load)
1537    }
1538
1539    /// Collection IDs currently known to this engine, sorted for deterministic output.
1540    pub fn collection_ids(&self) -> Vec<[u8; 16]> {
1541        let mut ids: Vec<[u8; 16]> = self.collections.read().keys().copied().collect();
1542        ids.sort_unstable();
1543        ids
1544    }
1545
1546    /// Collection IDs whose live index currently references at least one record
1547    /// physically stored in `shard_id`.
1548    ///
1549    /// Shards are shared, so this is normally more than one collection; it's the
1550    /// set `repack_shard` needs to touch before that shard can retire.
1551    ///
1552    /// Sweeps the shard's own file content (bounded by `MAX_SHARD_BYTES`,
1553    /// not by how many unrelated collections happen to be loaded) rather than
1554    /// scanning every collection's index to ask "does this touch shard N" — a
1555    /// candidate set from the raw scan is then filtered against each
1556    /// candidate collection's *current* live index, since the scan alone can't
1557    /// tell a still-live reference from a collection that already repacked past
1558    /// this shard (its old bytes just haven't been overwritten — they
1559    /// never are, shards are append-only).
1560    ///
1561    /// # Errors
1562    /// Returns `StorageError` if the shard's file can't be read, or if
1563    /// `shard_id` doesn't correspond to a currently-open shard.
1564    pub fn collections_referencing_shard(
1565        &self,
1566        shard_id: u16,
1567    ) -> Result<Vec<[u8; 16]>, StorageError> {
1568        let shard = self.shards.get_shard(shard_id).ok_or_else(|| {
1569            StorageError::Io(std::io::Error::new(
1570                std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound,
1571                format!("no open shard with id {shard_id}"),
1572            ))
1573        })?;
1574        // O(1) against the incrementally-maintained shard→collection directory
1575        // instead of scanning the shard file — this used to be the
1576        // dominant cost of shard retirement/evacuation-style operations
1577        // on a large shard.
1578        Ok(self
1579            .shard_collections
1580            .read()
1581            .get(&shard.pack_id)
1582            .map(|collections| collections.keys().copied().collect())
1583            .unwrap_or_default())
1584    }
1585
1586    /// Repack every collection that still references `shard_id`.
1587    ///
1588    /// A shard only ever retires once *every* collection referencing it has
1589    /// repacked past its data there (see `retire_empty_shards`) — there's
1590    /// no per-shard compaction primitive, since reachability (what's
1591    /// live vs. garbage) is inherently a per-collection concept, not a shard
1592    /// one. This is the targeted way to reclaim one specific shard: find
1593    /// every collection still pinning it live and repack each of them, instead
1594    /// of waiting for each collection to independently cross its own repack
1595    /// threshold. Live records get moved to whatever's currently that
1596    /// collection's home shard (`ShardPool::collection_home`) — not necessarily the
1597    /// pool's single "main" shard, since homes are per-collection, not global.
1598    ///
1599    /// Returns `(collection_id, kept, dropped)` for each collection repacked, in the
1600    /// order `collections_referencing_shard` returned them. Does not itself
1601    /// guarantee the shard retires — a collection with `set_live_roots` never
1602    /// called preserves everything (nothing is provably garbage) and its
1603    /// repack is a no-op for this purpose.
1604    ///
1605    /// # Errors
1606    /// Returns `StorageError` if any collection's repack fails; already-repacked
1607    /// collections in this call are not rolled back.
1608    pub fn repack_shard(
1609        &self,
1610        shard_id: u16,
1611        extract_edges: impl Fn(&[u8; 16], &[u8]) -> Vec<[u8; 16]>,
1612    ) -> Result<Vec<([u8; 16], usize, usize)>, StorageError> {
1613        let collections = self.collections_referencing_shard(shard_id)?;
1614        let mut results = Vec::with_capacity(collections.len());
1615        for collection_id in collections {
1616            let (kept, dropped) = self
1617                .repack_collection_reachable(&collection_id, |hash, data| {
1618                    extract_edges(hash, data)
1619                })?;
1620            results.push((collection_id, kept, dropped));
1621        }
1622        Ok(results)
1623    }
1624
1625    /// A collection's `(entry count, memory usage in bytes, index capacity)`,
1626    /// if the collection exists. `entry count / capacity` is the index's
1627    /// load factor.
1628    pub fn collection_index_info(&self, collection_id: &[u8; 16]) -> Option<(usize, usize, u32)> {
1629        self.collections.read().get(collection_id).map(|gen| {
1630            let g = gen.load();
1631            (g.index.len(), g.index.memory_usage(), g.index.capacity())
1632        })
1633    }
1634
1635    /// `(collection_id, entry count, memory usage in bytes, index capacity)`
1636    /// for every known collection, sorted by collection ID, in a single pass
1637    /// over the collection map.
1638    pub fn collection_summaries(&self) -> Vec<CollectionSummary> {
1639        let collections = self.collections.read();
1640        self.collection_order
1641            .read()
1642            .iter()
1643            .filter_map(|id| {
1644                collections.get(id).map(|gen| {
1645                    let g = gen.load();
1646                    (
1647                        *id,
1648                        g.index.len(),
1649                        g.index.memory_usage(),
1650                        g.index.capacity(),
1651                    )
1652                })
1653            })
1654            .collect()
1655    }
1656
1657    /// Set the live roots to preserve for a collection on its next repack.
1658    pub fn set_live_roots(&self, collection_id: &[u8; 16], roots: Vec<NodeId>) {
1659        self.live_roots.write().insert(*collection_id, roots);
1660    }
1661
1662    /// Set the repack trigger threshold directly, in index entries.
1663    pub fn set_repack_threshold_entries(&self, entries: u64) {
1664        self.repack_threshold_entries
1665            .store(entries, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
1666    }
1667
1668    /// Returns `true` if a collection's index has reached the configured repack
1669    /// threshold.
1670    ///
1671    /// Repacking is entirely caller-driven — nothing in this engine polls
1672    /// this on its own. A background GC worker is expected to call this
1673    /// periodically and issue `repack_collection_reachable` itself; nothing in
1674    /// `mtxdb-core` currently does so.
1675    #[must_use]
1676    pub fn needs_repack(&self, collection_id: &[u8; 16]) -> bool {
1677        let count = self
1678            .collection_index_info(collection_id)
1679            .map_or(0, |(len, _, _)| len as u64);
1680        count
1681            >= self
1682                .repack_threshold_entries
1683                .load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed)
1684    }
1685
1686    /// The collection-scoped node cache for `collection_id`, or a fresh empty one if the collection is unknown.
1687    pub fn collection_cache(&self, collection_id: &[u8; 16]) -> Arc<NodeCache> {
1688        self.generation(collection_id).map_or_else(
1689            || Arc::new(NodeCache::new(self.cache_capacity)),
1690            |g| g.cache.clone(),
1691        )
1692    }
1693
