3246    /// garbage collection — this is the engine's one repack entry point.
3247    ///
3248    /// If live roots are configured for this collection (see
3249    /// [`Self::set_live_roots`]), traverses outward from them via
3250    /// `extract_edges` and drops anything not reached: only reachable
3251    /// records are read from disk during the traversal, so unreachable
3252    /// data is never even fetched, not just excluded from the output.
3253    ///
3254    /// If no live roots are configured (`set_live_roots` was never called,
3255    /// or was called with an empty list), nothing is known to be garbage,
3256    /// so every scanned record is preserved, still deduplicated and
3257    /// rewritten in topological order — rather than risk deleting live
3258    /// data on the assumption that "no roots" means "nothing is live".
3259    ///
3260    /// All shards this call will read from are pinned (held via an
3261    /// `Arc<Shard>` for the whole call) before any writes happen, so a
3262    /// rotation triggered by this call's own writes can never retire a
3263    /// shard this
3264    /// call still needs to read stale data from.
3265    ///
3266    /// Returns `(kept, dropped)`: the number of records written to the new
3267    /// generation, and the number of scanned records found unreachable and
3268    /// discarded.
3269    ///
3270    /// # Errors
3271    /// Returns `StorageError` on I/O or corruption.
3272    /// Builds this repack's deduped `hash → (shard_id, offset)` map,
3273    /// incrementally where possible, and returns the previous
3274    /// [`RepackIncrementalState`] (moved out, not cloned) so the caller can
3275    /// reuse its `adjacency` cache when deciding which nodes need fresh disk
3276    /// reads.
3277    ///
3278    /// On the incremental path the previous `live_map` is extended in-place
3279    /// with only the newly-appended entries from each shard (O(delta) scan
3280    /// work per repack call rather than O(total)). On the cold-start path
3281    /// (first repack for this collection) every shard is scanned from byte
3282    /// zero.
3283    ///
3284    /// Returns `(hash_to_shard_offset, Some(prev))` on the incremental path
3285    /// and `(hash_to_shard_offset, None)` on the cold-start path. The caller
3286    /// must pass `prev` to the adjacency helpers to unlock the incremental
3287    /// BFS optimisation; when it is `None` the caller uses the full-scan
3288    /// adjacency helpers instead.
3289    ///
3290    /// # Errors
3291    /// Returns `StorageError` on I/O failure scanning a shard.
3292    fn repack_scan_incremental(
3293        &self,
3294        collection_id: &[u8; 16],
3295    ) -> Result<RepackScanResult, StorageError> {
3296        // Check for existing state under a read lock, then move it out under a
3297        // write lock if present — O(1) ownership transfer, no clone.
3298        let has_prev = self.repack_incremental.read().contains_key(collection_id);
3299        if has_prev {
3300            let prev = self
3301                .repack_incremental
3302                .write()
3303                .remove(collection_id)
3304                .expect("entry was present under read lock and collection mutex is held");
3305
3306            let shards = self.shards.all_shards();
3307            let current_pack_ids: HashMap<u16, u64> = shards
3308                .iter()
3309                .map(|(shard_id, shard)| (*shard_id, shard.pack_id))
3310                .collect();
3311            // Slot IDs are recyclable.  A cursor (and every entry in the
3312            // carried live_map) is meaningful only for the pack incarnation
3313            // that produced it.  On any replacement or disappearance, start
3314            // over rather than mixing old offsets with the new pack.
3315            let pack_changed = prev
3316                .scan_offsets
3317                .iter()
3318                .any(|(shard_id, (pack_id, _))| current_pack_ids.get(shard_id) != Some(pack_id));
3319            if pack_changed {
3320                let scanned = self.scan_collection_records(collection_id)?;
3321                let mut map = HashMap::new();
3322                for (shard_id, entries) in scanned {
3323                    for (hash, offset) in entries {
3324                        map.insert(hash, (shard_id, offset));
3325                    }
3326                }
3327                return Ok((map, None));
3328            }
3329
3330            // Scan only bytes appended since the last repack, merging into
3331            // the stolen live_map in-place — O(delta) work, not O(total).
3332            let mut map = prev.live_map.clone();
3333            for (shard_id, shard) in shards {
3334                let start = prev
3335                    .scan_offsets
3336                    .get(&shard_id)
3337                    .filter(|(pack_id, _)| *pack_id == shard.pack_id)
3338                    .map_or(0, |(_, offset)| *offset);
3339                let entries =
3340                    packfile::scan_packfile_from(&shard.path, start).map_err(StorageError::Io)?;
3341                for (rid, hash, offset) in entries {
3342                    if rid == *collection_id {
3343                        map.insert(hash, (shard_id, offset));
3344                    }
3345                }
3346            }
3347            Ok((map, Some(prev)))
3348        } else {
3349            // First repack: full scan of every shard.
3350            let scanned = self.scan_collection_records(collection_id)?;
3351            let mut map = HashMap::new();
3352            for (shard_id, entries) in &scanned {
3353                for (hash, offset) in entries {
3354                    map.insert(*hash, (*shard_id, *offset));
3355                }
3356            }
3357            Ok((map, None))
3358        }
3359    }
3360
3361    /// Incremental BFS reachability walk.
3362    ///
3363    /// Uses the adjacency cache from `prev` to avoid re-reading disk for nodes
3364    /// that were already known-live after the last repack. Only newly-added
3365    /// nodes (those absent from `prev.adjacency`) require a disk read.
3366    ///
3367    /// **Root-removal invariant (critical):** This function must NOT be called
3368    /// when the current root set is a strict subset of `prev.prev_roots` (i.e.
3369    /// any root was removed since the last repack). In that case nodes
3370    /// exclusively reachable through the removed root would remain in the live
3371    /// set indefinitely, because `visited` in `prev.adjacency` only grows —
3372    /// nothing in this incremental path forces a re-check of reachability.
3373    /// The caller ([`Self::repack_collection_reachable`]) detects root removal
3374    /// and falls back to [`Self::bfs_live_set`] (cold-start, full BFS) for
3375    /// that repack, then saves a fresh cursor so subsequent repacks can be
3376    /// incremental again.
3377    ///
3378    /// **Memory:** `prev.adjacency` holds `O(live_nodes` × `avg_fanout`) hashes
3379    /// (≈ `live_nodes × avg_fanout × 16` bytes) in RAM per collection. This
3380    /// is the cost of eliminating the O(live) disk reads per repack call —
3381    /// callers should be aware for large collections.
3382    fn bfs_live_set_incremental(
3383        pool: &ShardPool,
3384        roots: &[[u8; 16]],
3385        hash_to_shard_offset: &HashMap<[u8; 16], (u16, u64)>,
3386        prev: &RepackIncrementalState,
3387        pinned: &HashMap<u16, Arc<Shard>>,
3388        extract_edges: &impl Fn(&[u8; 16], &[u8]) -> Vec<[u8; 16]>,
3389    ) -> Result<AdjacencyResult, StorageError> {
3390        // Seed with roots that are not already in the cached live set.
3391        // Anything in prev.adjacency was reachable last time and — given no
3392        // root was removed — is still reachable now; no need to re-expand it.
3393        let mut visited: HashSet<[u8; 16]> = prev.adjacency.keys().copied().collect();
3394        let mut queue: VecDeque<[u8; 16]> = VecDeque::new();
3395        // Retain the cached edges for already-live nodes.  Initialising these
3396        // entries with empty vectors loses prior edges when they are not put
3397        // back on the queue during an incremental rooted repack.
3398        let mut adjacency = prev.adjacency.clone();
3399
3400        for root in roots {
3401            if hash_to_shard_offset.contains_key(root) && visited.insert(*root) {
3402                queue.push_back(*root);
3403            }
3404        }
3405
3406        while let Some(hash) = queue.pop_front() {
3407            // Fast path: edge list already cached — no disk read needed.
3408            if let Some(edges) = prev.adjacency.get(&hash) {
3409                for edge in edges {
3410                    if hash_to_shard_offset.contains_key(edge) && visited.insert(*edge) {
3411                        queue.push_back(*edge);
3412                    }
3413                }
3414                adjacency.insert(hash, edges.clone());
3415                continue;
3416            }
3417
3418            // Slow path: new node, must read from disk.
3419            let Some(&(shard_id, offset)) = hash_to_shard_offset.get(&hash) else {
3420                continue;
3421            };
3422            let Some(old_shard) = pinned.get(&shard_id) else {
3423                continue;
3424            };
3425            let record = pool.read_at(old_shard, offset, true)?;
3426            let edges = extract_edges(&hash, &record.data);
3427            for edge in &edges {
3428                if hash_to_shard_offset.contains_key(edge) && visited.insert(*edge) {
3429                    queue.push_back(*edge);
3430                }
3431            }
3432            adjacency.insert(hash, edges);
3433        }
3434
3435        // Restrict adjacency to the actual visited set — entries inherited
3436        // from prev that are no longer in hash_to_shard_offset (e.g. they
3437        // were in a shard that was later dropped) must not survive.
3438        adjacency.retain(|h, _| visited.contains(h) && hash_to_shard_offset.contains_key(h));
3439
3440        let mut live_hashes: Vec<[u8; 16]> = visited
3441            .into_iter()
3442            .filter(|h| hash_to_shard_offset.contains_key(h))
3443            .collect();
3444        live_hashes.sort_unstable();
3445        Ok((live_hashes, adjacency))
3446    }
3447
3448    /// Incremental full-adjacency scan (no-roots / preserve-everything path).
3449    ///
3450    /// For each hash in `hash_to_shard_offset`, reuses the cached edge list
3451    /// from `prev.adjacency` when available — avoiding a disk read for any
3452    /// node that survived the last repack. Only genuinely new hashes (not in
3453    /// the cache) require a disk read.
3454    ///
3455    /// All hashes in `hash_to_shard_offset` are live (no GC in this path),
3456    /// so the result is always the full map.
3457    ///
3458    /// **Memory:** same as [`Self::bfs_live_set_incremental`] — see that doc.
3459    fn scan_full_adjacency_incremental(
3460        pool: &ShardPool,
3461        hash_to_shard_offset: &HashMap<[u8; 16], (u16, u64)>,
3462        prev: &RepackIncrementalState,
3463        pinned: &HashMap<u16, Arc<Shard>>,
3464        extract_edges: &impl Fn(&[u8; 16], &[u8]) -> Vec<[u8; 16]>,
3465    ) -> Result<AdjacencyResult, StorageError> {
3466        let mut all_hashes: Vec<[u8; 16]> = hash_to_shard_offset.keys().copied().collect();
3467        all_hashes.sort_unstable();
3468        let mut adjacency: HashMap<[u8; 16], Vec<[u8; 16]>> =
3469            HashMap::with_capacity(hash_to_shard_offset.len());
3470
3471        for (hash, &(shard_id, offset)) in hash_to_shard_offset {
3472            // Fast path: node was live last time, edge list is cached.
3473            if let Some(edges) = prev.adjacency.get(hash) {
3474                adjacency.insert(*hash, edges.clone());
3475                continue;
3476            }
3477            // Slow path: new node, must read from disk.
3478            if let Some(shard) = pinned.get(&shard_id) {
3479                let record = pool.read_at(shard, offset, true)?;
3480                let edges = extract_edges(hash, &record.data);
3481                adjacency.insert(*hash, edges);
3482            }
3483        }
3484        Ok((all_hashes, adjacency))
3485    }
3486
3487    /// Saves the cursor state a future call to [`Self::repack_scan_incremental`]
3488    /// needs: each shard's current byte length (the next repack's scan start
3489    /// point), the deduped live map restricted to hashes that survived into
3490    /// `new_offsets`, the adjacency cache for those survivors (so the next
3491    /// repack's BFS/scan can skip re-reading disk for already-seen nodes), and
3492    /// the roots snapshot (so the next repack can detect whether any root was
3493    /// removed and fall back to a full BFS sweep if so).
3494    ///
3495    /// Anything this repack dropped must not resurface via the carried-forward
3496    /// `live_map` or adjacency cache next time.
3497    fn repack_save_incremental_state(
3498        &self,
3499        collection_id: &[u8; 16],
3500        new_offsets: &[([u8; 16], u16, u64)],
3501        adjacency: HashMap<[u8; 16], Vec<[u8; 16]>>,
3502        current_roots: &[[u8; 16]],
3503    ) {
3504        let surviving: HashSet<[u8; 16]> = new_offsets.iter().map(|&(h, _, _)| h).collect();
3505
3506        let mut scan_offsets = HashMap::new();
3507        for (shard_id, shard) in self.shards.all_shards() {
3508            scan_offsets.insert(shard_id, (shard.pack_id, shard.file_len()));
3509        }
3510        let next_live_map: HashMap<[u8; 16], (u16, u64)> = new_offsets
3511            .iter()
3512            .map(|&(hash, shard_id, offset)| (hash, (shard_id, offset)))
3513            .collect();
3514        // Evict dropped nodes from the adjacency cache so they can't
3515        // re-enter the live set on a future incremental BFS pass.
3516        let next_adjacency: HashMap<[u8; 16], Vec<[u8; 16]>> = adjacency
3517            .into_iter()
3518            .filter(|(h, _)| surviving.contains(h))
3519            .collect();
3520        self.repack_incremental.write().insert(
3521            *collection_id,
3522            RepackIncrementalState {
3523                scan_offsets,
3524                live_map: next_live_map,
3525                adjacency: next_adjacency,
3526                prev_roots: current_roots.to_vec(),
3527            },
3528        );
3529    }
3530
3531    /// Non-mutating preview of what [`Self::repack_collection_reachable`] would
3532    /// do for `collection_id`: runs the exact same scan + reachability
3533    /// computation (so kept/dropped counts are exact, not estimates), but
3534    /// performs no writes, no index swap, and no incremental-repack
3535    /// cursor update. Used to preview a repack's effect before committing
3536    /// to it — e.g. a shard-compaction preflight that needs "how many
3537    /// bytes would survive, and which shards do they currently live in"
3538    /// without actually rewriting anything.
3539    ///
3540    /// # Errors
3541    /// Returns `StorageError` on I/O or corruption.
3542    pub fn plan_collection_repack(
3543        &self,
3544        collection_id: &[u8; 16],
3545        extract_edges: impl Fn(&[u8; 16], &[u8]) -> Vec<[u8; 16]>,
3546    ) -> Result<RepackPlan, StorageError> {
3547        // `repack_scan_incremental` moves the cursor out to avoid cloning on
3548        // real repacks.  A plan must leave that state untouched, and must not
3549        // race a real repack while temporarily doing so.
3550        let collection_arc = self.put_mutex(collection_id);
3551        let _collection_guard = collection_arc.lock();
3552        // Make buffered frames visible to the packfile scan so the plan
3553        // reflects records that have actually been put (not yet flushed).
3554        self.shards.flush_all()?;
3555        let saved_state = self.repack_incremental.read().get(collection_id).cloned();
3556        let result = self
3557            .repack_scan_incremental(collection_id)
3558            .and_then(|(map, _)| {
3559                self.plan_collection_repack_from_map(collection_id, &map, &extract_edges)
3560            });
3561        let mut states = self.repack_incremental.write();
3562        if let Some(state) = saved_state {
3563            states.insert(*collection_id, state);
3564        } else {
3565            states.remove(collection_id);
3566        }
3567        result
3568    }
3569
3570    fn plan_collection_repack_from_map(
3571        &self,
3572        collection_id: &[u8; 16],
3573        hash_to_shard_offset: &RepackRecordMap,
3574        extract_edges: &impl Fn(&[u8; 16], &[u8]) -> Vec<[u8; 16]>,
3575    ) -> Result<RepackPlan, StorageError> {
3576        let pinned = self.pin_shards(hash_to_shard_offset.values().map(|&(id, _)| id));
3577
3578        let roots = self.live_roots.read().get(collection_id).cloned();
3579        let (live_hashes, _adjacency) = match roots {
3580            Some(roots) if !roots.is_empty() => Self::bfs_live_set(
3581                &self.shards,
3582                &roots,
3583                hash_to_shard_offset,
3584                &pinned,
3585                &extract_edges,
3586            )?,
3587            _ => Self::scan_full_adjacency(
3588                &self.shards,
3589                hash_to_shard_offset,
3590                &pinned,
3591                &extract_edges,
3592            )?,
3593        };
3594        let dropped = hash_to_shard_offset.len().saturating_sub(live_hashes.len());
3595
3596        let live_set: HashSet<[u8; 16]> = live_hashes.iter().copied().collect();
3597        let mut kept_bytes: u64 = 0;
3598        let mut dropped_bytes: u64 = 0;
3599        let mut shards_touched: HashSet<u16> = HashSet::new();
3600        for (hash, &(shard_id, offset)) in hash_to_shard_offset {
3601            if let Some(shard) = pinned.get(&shard_id) {
3602                // Actual on-disk bytes, not the uncompressed upper bound —
3603                // a repack preflight should report what will really be
3604                // reclaimed/kept, which is smaller than plaintext size for
3605                // any frame that compressed.
3606                if let Ok(bytes) = Self::record_disk_len_at(shard, offset) {
3607                    if live_set.contains(hash) {
3608                        shards_touched.insert(shard_id);
3609                        kept_bytes = kept_bytes.saturating_add(bytes);
3610                    } else {
3611                        dropped_bytes = dropped_bytes.saturating_add(bytes);
3612                    }
3613                }
3614            }
3615        }
3616
3617        let mut shards_touched: Vec<u16> = shards_touched.into_iter().collect();
3618        shards_touched.sort_unstable();
3619        Ok(RepackPlan {
3620            kept: live_hashes.len(),
3621            dropped,
3622            kept_bytes,
3623            dropped_bytes,
3624            shards_touched,
3625        })
3626    }
3627
3628    /// Plan several collection repacks from one physical scan of their shard
3629    /// closure. Unlike repeatedly calling [`Self::plan_collection_repack`], this
3630    /// is O(bytes scanned) rather than O(collections × bytes scanned).
3631    ///
3632    /// # Errors
3633    /// Returns `StorageError` on I/O or corruption while scanning or
3634    /// resolving a requested collection's records.
3635    pub fn plan_collections_repack(
3636        &self,
3637        collection_ids: &[[u8; 16]],
3638        extract_edges: impl Fn(&[u8; 16], &[u8]) -> Vec<[u8; 16]>,
3639    ) -> Result<Vec<RepackPlan>, StorageError> {
3640        // Planning reads packfiles directly; flush first so buffered records
3641        // are part of the plan.
3642        self.shards.flush_all()?;
3643        let maps = self.scan_collection_record_maps(collection_ids)?;
3644        collection_ids
3645            .iter()
3646            .map(|collection_id| {
3647                let map = maps.get(collection_id).ok_or_else(|| {
3648                    StorageError::Corrupt("repack batch scan lost requested collection".to_owned())
3649                })?;
3650                self.plan_collection_repack_from_map(collection_id, map, &extract_edges)
3651            })
3652            .collect()
3653    }
3654
3655    /// Finds every collection and shard transitively reachable from
3656    /// `start_shard` via the collection↔shard reference relation: collections
3657    /// referencing this shard, the other shards those collections reference,
3658    /// the collections referencing *those* shards, and so on until nothing new
3659    /// is found. In practice this converges immediately in almost every
3660    /// case — a collection typically has a live footprint on only one or two
3661    /// shards (sticky home routing) — but the closure has to be computed
3662    /// rather than assumed, since a collection-scoped repack can't correctly
3663    /// judge liveness by looking at only one shard's slice of that collection's
3664    /// data (see [`Self::collections_referencing_shard`]'s doc).
3665    ///
3666    /// This is the basis for a shard-compaction preflight: you can't
3667    /// truthfully say "this touches N collections across K shards" without
3668    /// first finding the full closure, not just the one shard the
3669    /// operation was originally pointed at.
3670    ///
3671    /// # Errors
3672    /// Returns `StorageError` if `start_shard` isn't an open shard.
3673    pub fn repack_closure(
3674        &self,
3675        start_shard: u16,
3676    ) -> Result<(Vec<[u8; 16]>, Vec<u16>), StorageError> {
3677        let mut collections: HashSet<[u8; 16]> = HashSet::new();
3678        let mut shards: HashSet<u16> = HashSet::new();
3679        let mut shard_queue = vec![start_shard];
3680        let mut collection_queue: Vec<[u8; 16]> = Vec::new();
3681
3682        loop {
3683            if let Some(shard_id) = shard_queue.pop() {
3684                if shards.insert(shard_id) {
3685                    for collection_id in self.collections_referencing_shard(shard_id)? {
3686                        if collections.insert(collection_id) {
3687                            collection_queue.push(collection_id);
3688                        }
3689                    }
3690                }
3691            } else if let Some(collection_id) = collection_queue.pop() {
3692                for shard_id in self.collection_referenced_shards(&collection_id) {
3693                    if !shards.contains(&shard_id) {
3694                        shard_queue.push(shard_id);
3695                    }
3696                }
3697            } else {
3698                break;
3699            }
3700        }
3701
3702        let mut collections: Vec<[u8; 16]> = collections.into_iter().collect();
3703        collections.sort_unstable();
3704        let mut shards: Vec<u16> = shards.into_iter().collect();
3705        shards.sort_unstable();
3706        Ok((collections, shards))
3707    }
3708
3709    /// Every shard id `collection_id`'s current index has at least one live
3710    /// entry pointing into. Empty if the collection doesn't exist.
3711    #[must_use]
3712    pub fn collection_referenced_shards(&self, collection_id: &[u8; 16]) -> Vec<u16> {
3713        let Some(gen) = self.generation(collection_id) else {
3714            return Vec::new();
3715        };
3716        gen.index
3717            .referenced_shard_ids()
3718            .iter()
3719            .enumerate()
3720            .filter_map(|(i, &referenced)| referenced.then(|| u16::try_from(i).ok()).flatten())
3721            .collect()
3722    }
3723
3724    /// Like `collection_referenced_shards`, but returns the `pack_id`
3725    /// for each shard rather than the runtime slot index.
3726    pub fn collection_referenced_pack_ids(&self, collection_id: &[u8; 16]) -> Vec<u64> {
3727        self.collection_referenced_shards(collection_id)
3728            .iter()
3729            .filter_map(|&slot| self.shards.get_shard(slot).map(|s| s.pack_id))
3730            .collect()
3731    }
3732
3733    /// Current indexed-node count for every shard.
3734    ///
3735    /// Unlike a physical packfile scan, these counts include only index
3736    /// entries that are presently live and resolve through a collection's current
3737    /// generation. Rewritten or superseded append entries are excluded.
3738    #[must_use]
3739    pub fn shard_node_counts(&self) -> HashMap<u64, u64> {
3740        self.shard_collections
3741            .read()
3742            .iter()
3743            .map(|(&shard_id, collections)| {
3744                let count = collections
3745                    .values()
3746                    .copied()
3747                    .fold(0u64, u64::saturating_add);
3748                (shard_id, count)
3749            })
3750            .collect()
3751    }
3752
3753    /// # Errors
3754    /// Returns `StorageError` on I/O or corruption.
3755    ///
3756    /// # Panics
3757    /// Panics if any hash in the CSR exceeds `u32::MAX` local ID space.
3758    // Four dispatch arms (roots/no-roots × incremental/cold-start) plus root-removal
3759    // detection make this function long; splitting it would just move the complexity
3760    // without reducing it.
3761    #[allow(clippy::too_many_lines)]
3762    pub fn repack_collection_reachable(
3763        &self,
3764        collection_id: &[u8; 16],
3765        extract_edges: impl Fn(&[u8; 16], &[u8]) -> Vec<[u8; 16]>,
3766    ) -> Result<(usize, usize), StorageError> {
3767        let collection_arc = self.put_mutex(collection_id);
3768        let _collection_guard = collection_arc.lock();
3769
3770        // The scan below reads packfiles directly, so any records still
3771        // sitting in a shard's append buffer would be invisible to it and
3772        // get silently dropped by this repack. Flush first so the whole
3773        // collection's committed bytes are scan-visible. Holding this
3774        // collection's put mutex means nothing can buffer behind it for this
3775        // collection between the flush and the scan.
3776        self.shards.flush_all()?;
3777
3778        let (hash_to_shard_offset, prev_state) = self.repack_scan_incremental(collection_id)?;
3779
3780        // Pin every shard this call could possibly read from before any
3781        // writes happen — see pin_shards' doc for why this must come
3782        // first.
3783        let pinned = self.pin_shards(hash_to_shard_offset.values().map(|&(id, _)| id));
3784
3785        let roots = self.live_roots.read().get(collection_id).cloned();
3786
3787        // Root-removal detection: if any root present in the previous repack's
3788        // snapshot is absent from the current root set, nodes exclusively
3789        // reachable through that removed root may be stuck in the cached live
3790        // set indefinitely (the incremental adjacency cache only grows — it has
3791        // no mechanism to re-derive reachability). Fall back to a full cold-start
3792        // BFS for this repack so those nodes can actually be collected. Once the
3793        // full sweep completes, the saved cursor is fresh and subsequent repacks
3794        // can be incremental again.
3795        let roots_shrunk = prev_state.as_ref().is_some_and(|prev| {
3796            let current_root_set: HashSet<[u8; 16]> =
3797                roots.as_deref().unwrap_or(&[]).iter().copied().collect();
3798            prev.prev_roots
3799                .iter()
3800                .any(|r| !current_root_set.contains(r))
3801        });
3802        // Use incremental adjacency only when prev state exists AND no root was removed.
3803        let use_incremental = prev_state.is_some() && !roots_shrunk;
3804
3805        let (live_hashes, adjacency) = match (roots.as_deref(), use_incremental) {
3806            (Some(roots), true) if !roots.is_empty() => {
3807                // Incremental BFS: only expand from roots not already in the
3808                // cached live set; reuse cached edge lists for everything else.
3809                Self::bfs_live_set_incremental(
3810                    &self.shards,
3811                    roots,
3812                    &hash_to_shard_offset,
3813                    prev_state.as_ref().expect("use_incremental implies Some"),
3814                    &pinned,
3815                    &extract_edges,
3816                )?
3817            }
3818            (Some(roots), false) if !roots.is_empty() => {
3819                // Cold-start BFS: root set shrank or first repack — full walk.
3820                Self::bfs_live_set(
3821                    &self.shards,
3822                    roots,
3823                    &hash_to_shard_offset,
3824                    &pinned,
3825                    &extract_edges,
3826                )?
3827            }
3828            (_, true) => {
3829                // No live roots, incremental path: only read disk for new nodes.
3830                Self::scan_full_adjacency_incremental(
3831                    &self.shards,
3832                    &hash_to_shard_offset,
3833                    prev_state.as_ref().expect("use_incremental implies Some"),
3834                    &pinned,
3835                    &extract_edges,
3836                )?
3837            }
3838            _ => {
3839                // No live roots, cold-start: preserve everything, read all.
3840                Self::scan_full_adjacency(
3841                    &self.shards,
3842                    &hash_to_shard_offset,
3843                    &pinned,
3844                    &extract_edges,
3845                )?
3846            }
3847        };
3848
3849        let dropped = hash_to_shard_offset.len().saturating_sub(live_hashes.len());
3850
3851        // Evict garbage-collected hashes from the collection's cache. Repack
3852        // carries the same cache forward into the new generation (below);
3853        // without this, a stale cache hit could still serve bytes for a
3854        // hash this pass just decided is unreachable, defeating GC.
3855        if dropped > 0 {
3856            let live_set: HashSet<[u8; 16]> = live_hashes.iter().copied().collect();
3857            if let Some(gen) = self.generation(collection_id) {
3858                for hash in hash_to_shard_offset.keys() {
3859                    if !live_set.contains(hash) {
3860                        let mut hex = String::with_capacity(32);
3861                        for b in hash {
3862                            let _ = write!(hex, "{b:02x}");
3863                        }
3864                        eprintln!("  dropped: {hex}");
3865                        gen.cache.remove(hash);
3866                    }
3867                }
3868            }
3869        }
3870
3871        let csr = Csr::build_from_edges(&live_hashes, &adjacency);
3872        let topo = csr.topo_order();
3873
3874        if topo.len() != live_hashes.len() {
3875            return Err(StorageError::Corrupt(format!(
3876                "repack: cyclic graph detected — topo_order produced {} nodes from {} live hashes",
3877                topo.len(),
3878                live_hashes.len(),
3879            )));
3880        }
3881
3882        // Copy into a non-source destination shard, never back into the
3883        // collection's current generation. Once the new index is published below,
3884        // this lets old source shards retire when no other collection references
3885        // them.
3886        let source_shards: HashSet<u16> = hash_to_shard_offset
3887            .values()
3888            .map(|&(shard_id, _)| shard_id)
3889            .collect();
3890        self.shards
3891            .prepare_collection_repack(collection_id, &source_shards)?;
3892
3893        let mut new_offsets: Vec<([u8; 16], u16, u64)> = Vec::with_capacity(topo.len());
3894        for &local in &topo {
3895            let hash = csr
3896                .hash_of(local)
3897                .expect("topo order contains valid local IDs");
3898            let Some(&(old_shard_id, old_offset)) = hash_to_shard_offset.get(hash) else {
3899                continue;
3900            };
3901            if let Some(entry) =
3902                self.copy_record_to_shard(collection_id, &pinned, old_shard_id, old_offset)?
3903            {
3904                new_offsets.push(entry);
3905            }
3906        }
3907
3908        let kept = new_offsets.len();
3909
3910        let index = Self::build_index(&new_offsets)?;
3911        self.replace_collection_shard_counts(
3912            collection_id,
3913            &self.slot_counts_to_pack_id_counts(&index.shard_counts()),
3914        );
3915        self.swap_generation(collection_id, index)?;
3916        self.retire_empty_shards(collection_id);
3917
3918        self.repack_count.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
3919        self.repack_kept_total
3920            .fetch_add(kept as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
3921        self.repack_dropped_total
3922            .fetch_add(dropped as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
3923        self.repack_counts_by_collection
3924            .write()
3925            .entry(*collection_id)
3926            .and_modify(|c| *c = c.saturating_add(1))
3927            .or_insert(1);
3928
3929        let current_roots: Vec<[u8; 16]> = roots.as_deref().unwrap_or(&[]).to_vec();
3930        self.repack_save_incremental_state(collection_id, &new_offsets, adjacency, &current_roots);
3931
3932        // Fsync the shards this repack just wrote into and persist the
3933        // updated stats snapshot as part of finishing the repack, rather
3934        // than leaving both to whatever the next unrelated sync_dirty()
3935        // call happens to be — a crash right after a repack should not
3936        // lose durability for data this repack itself just wrote, nor
3937        // leave the persisted stats stale relative to what's on disk.
3938        self.shards.sync_dirty()?;
3939
3940        Ok((kept, dropped))
3941    }
3942
3943    /// Repack several collections as one physical shard-compaction batch.
3944    ///
3945    /// The input is scanned once, then all replacement records are written
3946    /// through one shared destination stream. This is O(bytes + nodes), not
3947    /// O(collections × bytes), and avoids leaving one partially-filled destination
3948    /// shard behind for each collection.
3949    ///
3950    /// # Errors
3951    /// Returns [`StorageError`] on I/O, corruption, or an unavailable staging
3952    /// shard slot.
3953    ///
3954    /// # Panics
3955    /// Panics if the CSR implementation returns a local ID not present in its
3956    /// own hash table, which would violate its internal ordering invariant.
3957    pub fn repack_collections_reachable(
3958        &self,
3959        collection_ids: &[[u8; 16]],
3960        extract_edges: impl Fn(&[u8; 16], &[u8]) -> Vec<[u8; 16]>,
3961    ) -> Result<Vec<([u8; 16], usize, usize)>, StorageError> {
3962        self.repack_collections_reachable_with_progress(
3963            collection_ids,
3964            extract_edges,
3965            |_collection, _from, _to, _nodes, _complete| {},
3966        )
3967    }
3968
3969    /// As [`Self::repack_collections_reachable`], with a callback whenever the
3970    /// shared output stream rotates. The callback receives the collection whose
3971    /// copy crossed the boundary (or `None` at completion), the slot it
3972    /// rotated from, the replacement slot, the cumulative number of copied
3973    /// nodes, and whether the copy is complete.
3974    ///
3975    /// This lets an interactive caller show meaningful physical-compaction
3976    /// progress without turning the shared shard batch back into a
3977    /// collection-at-a-time operation.
3978    ///
3979    /// # Errors
3980    ///
3981    /// Returns [`StorageError`] on I/O, corruption, or an unavailable staging
3982    /// shard slot.
3983    ///
3984    /// # Panics
3985    ///
3986    /// Panics if the CSR implementation returns a local ID not present in its
3987    /// own hash table, which would violate its internal ordering invariant.
3988    fn report_repack_output_rotation(
3989        output_progress: &mut impl FnMut(Option<[u8; 16]>, u16, u16, usize, bool),
3990        active_output_shard: &mut u16,
3991        current_active_shard: u16,
3992        collection_id: &[u8; 16],
3993        copied_nodes: usize,
3994    ) {
3995        if current_active_shard != *active_output_shard {
3996            output_progress(
3997                Some(*collection_id),
3998                *active_output_shard,
3999                current_active_shard,
4000                copied_nodes,
4001                false,
4002            );
4003            *active_output_shard = current_active_shard;
4004        }
4005    }
4006
4007    fn copy_repack_batch_collection(
4008        &self,
4009        collection_id: &[u8; 16],
4010        hash_to_shard_offset: &RepackRecordMap,
4011        pinned: &HashMap<u16, Arc<Shard>>,
4012        extract_edges: &impl Fn(&[u8; 16], &[u8]) -> Vec<[u8; 16]>,
4013        output_progress: &mut impl FnMut(Option<[u8; 16]>, u16, u16, usize, bool),
4014        output_state: &mut (u16, usize),
4015    ) -> Result<(RepackOffsets, usize), StorageError> {
4016        let roots = self.live_roots.read().get(collection_id).cloned();
4017        let (live_hashes, adjacency) = match roots {
4018            Some(roots) if !roots.is_empty() => Self::bfs_live_set(
4019                &self.shards,
4020                &roots,
4021                hash_to_shard_offset,
4022                pinned,
4023                extract_edges,
4024            )?,
4025            _ => Self::scan_full_adjacency(
4026                &self.shards,
4027                hash_to_shard_offset,
4028                pinned,
4029                extract_edges,
4030            )?,
4031        };
4032        let dropped = hash_to_shard_offset.len().saturating_sub(live_hashes.len());
4033
4034        if dropped > 0 {
4035            let live_set: HashSet<[u8; 16]> = live_hashes.iter().copied().collect();
4036            if let Some(gen) = self.generation(collection_id) {
4037                for hash in hash_to_shard_offset.keys() {
4038                    if !live_set.contains(hash) {
4039                        gen.cache.remove(hash);
4040                    }
4041                }
4042            }
4043        }
4044
4045        let csr = Csr::build_from_edges(&live_hashes, &adjacency);
4046        let topo = csr.topo_order();
4047        if topo.len() != live_hashes.len() {
4048            return Err(StorageError::Corrupt(format!(
4049                "repack: cyclic graph detected — topo_order produced {} nodes from {} live hashes",
4050                topo.len(),
4051                live_hashes.len(),
4052            )));
4053        }
4054
4055        let mut new_offsets = Vec::with_capacity(topo.len());
4056        for &local in &topo {
4057            let hash = csr
4058                .hash_of(local)
4059                .expect("topo order contains valid local IDs");
4060            let Some(&(old_shard_id, old_offset)) = hash_to_shard_offset.get(hash) else {
4061                continue;
4062            };
4063            if let Some(entry) =
4064                self.copy_record_to_shard(collection_id, pinned, old_shard_id, old_offset)?
4065            {
4066                output_state.1 = output_state.1.saturating_add(1);
4067                let current_active_shard = self.shards.active_shard().slot;
4068                Self::report_repack_output_rotation(
4069                    output_progress,
4070                    &mut output_state.0,
4071                    current_active_shard,
4072                    collection_id,
4073                    output_state.1,
4074                );
4075                new_offsets.push(entry);
4076            }
4077        }
4078
4079        Ok((new_offsets, dropped))
4080    }
4081
4082    /// As [`Self::repack_collections_reachable`], with a callback whenever the
4083    /// shared output stream rotates. The callback receives the collection whose
4084    /// copy crossed the boundary (or `None` at completion), the slot it
4085    /// rotated from, the replacement slot, the cumulative number of copied
4086    /// nodes, and whether the copy is complete.
4087    ///
4088    /// # Errors
4089    ///
4090    /// Returns [`StorageError`] on I/O, corruption, or an unavailable staging
4091    /// shard slot.
4092    ///
4093    /// # Panics
4094    ///
4095    /// Panics if the CSR implementation returns a local ID not present in its
4096    /// own hash table, which would violate its internal ordering invariant.
4097    pub fn repack_collections_reachable_with_progress(
4098        &self,
4099        collection_ids: &[[u8; 16]],
4100        extract_edges: impl Fn(&[u8; 16], &[u8]) -> Vec<[u8; 16]>,
4101        mut output_progress: impl FnMut(Option<[u8; 16]>, u16, u16, usize, bool),
4102    ) -> Result<Vec<([u8; 16], usize, usize)>, StorageError> {
4103        let mut collection_ids = collection_ids.to_vec();
4104        collection_ids.sort_unstable();
4105        collection_ids.dedup();
4106        if collection_ids.is_empty() {
4107            return Ok(Vec::new());
4108        }
4109
4110        // A batch is one coherent generation change. Hold every selected
4111        // collection's writer mutex in a stable order so a concurrent put cannot
4112        // be missed by the scan or revive an old source after the swap.
4113        let mutexes: Vec<_> = collection_ids.iter().map(|id| self.put_mutex(id)).collect();
4114        let collection_guards: Vec<_> = mutexes.iter().map(|mutex| mutex.lock()).collect();
4115
4116        // Same scan visibility requirement as the single-collection repack:
4117        // every selected collection's buffered frames must be on disk before
4118        // `scan_collection_record_maps` reads packfiles, or those records
4119        // would be dropped as if never written.
4120        self.shards.flush_all()?;
4121
4122        let maps = self.scan_collection_record_maps(&collection_ids)?;
4123        let source_shards: HashSet<u16> = maps
4124            .values()
4125            .flat_map(|map| map.values().map(|&(shard_id, _)| shard_id))
4126            .collect();
4127        let pinned = self.pin_shards(source_shards.iter().copied());
4128
4129        // Establish one common destination before copying the first frame.
4130        // `put_record` moves every collection to the same active successor when a
4131        // destination fills, so output remains densely packed across collections.
4132        self.shards
4133            .prepare_collections_repack(&collection_ids, &source_shards)?;
4134
4135        let mut results = Vec::with_capacity(collection_ids.len());
4136        let mut output_state = (self.shards.active_shard().slot, 0usize);
4137        for collection_id in &collection_ids {
4138            let hash_to_shard_offset = maps.get(collection_id).ok_or_else(|| {
4139                StorageError::Corrupt("repack batch scan lost requested collection".to_owned())
4140            })?;
4141            let (new_offsets, dropped) = self.copy_repack_batch_collection(
4142                collection_id,
4143                hash_to_shard_offset,
4144                &pinned,
4145                &extract_edges,
4146                &mut output_progress,
4147                &mut output_state,
4148            )?;
4149            let kept = new_offsets.len();
4150            let index = Self::build_index(&new_offsets)?;
4151            self.replace_collection_shard_counts(
4152                collection_id,
4153                &self.slot_counts_to_pack_id_counts(&index.shard_counts()),
4154            );
4155            self.swap_generation(collection_id, index)?;
4156            // The batch path does a full cold-start scan (scan_collection_record_maps),
4157            // so we have no incremental adjacency to carry forward. Pass an empty
4158            // map so the next single-collection repack starts with a fresh cache
4159            // (it will populate it from the full BFS on that first pass).
4160            let batch_roots: Vec<[u8; 16]> = self
4161                .live_roots
4162                .read()
4163                .get(collection_id)
4164                .cloned()
4165                .unwrap_or_default();
4166            self.repack_save_incremental_state(
4167                collection_id,
4168                &new_offsets,
4169                HashMap::new(),
4170                &batch_roots,
4171            );
4172
4173            self.repack_count.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
4174            self.repack_kept_total
4175                .fetch_add(kept as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
4176            self.repack_dropped_total
4177                .fetch_add(dropped as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
4178            self.repack_counts_by_collection
4179                .write()
4180                .entry(*collection_id)
4181                .and_modify(|count| *count = count.saturating_add(1))
4182                .or_insert(1);
4183            results.push((*collection_id, kept, dropped));
4184        }
4185
4186        output_progress(None, output_state.0, output_state.0, output_state.1, true);
4187
4188        // All target collection locks remain held while source references are
4189        // replaced. Drop them before the general retirement protocol, which
4190        // obtains every collection lock itself.
4191        drop(collection_guards);
4192        self.retire_empty_shards_after_batch();
4193        self.shards.sync_dirty()?;
4194        Ok(results)
4195    }
4196
4197    /// Fetches the ancestors of `frontier`, walking `extract_edges`
4198    /// backward and stopping expansion at (and excluding) anything in
4199    /// `stop_at` — a bounded ancestor walk, e.g. for auth-chain or
4200    /// prev-event traversal from a caller-supplied frontier back to
4201    /// caller-supplied already-known boundary nodes.
4202    ///
4203    /// This is **not** a from/to range or span: if a branch's history
4204    /// never crosses any `stop_at` node (a fork off the main line, say),
4205    /// that branch is walked all the way back to the collection's true roots,
4206    /// not truncated at an implied boundary. A genuine "everything between
4207    /// these two frontiers" query is `ancestors(frontier) ∩
4208    /// descendants(stop_at)`, which needs reverse adjacency this doesn't
4209    /// build — `NodeId`s are content hashes with no key order to slice a
4210    /// real range query over, and this walk only covers the ancestor half.
4211    /// `limits.max_nodes` bounds the runaway case above.
4212    ///
4213    /// Returns nodes lazily, ancestor-first (parents before children,
4214    /// topologically), as a [`DagWalk`] iterator over data already
4215    /// resolved during the walk itself (no second per-node fetch against
4216    /// whatever generation happens to be live when the iterator is
4217    /// drained) — every record was matched by content hash, not just
4218    /// index tag, so an index-tag collision can't surface the wrong one.
4219    ///
4220    /// Read-only: resolves against one frozen generation snapshot via its
4221    /// internal breadth-first traversal and takes no `put_mutex`. Cost is
4222    /// proportional to the number of ancestors actually walked — one
4223    /// index lookup per node — not collection size, since it doesn't pre-scan
4224    /// every shard the way `repack_collection_reachable` does.
4225    ///
4226    /// # Errors
4227    /// Returns `StorageError` on I/O or corruption, or if the walk finds a
4228    /// cycle (an event graph must be acyclic; a cycle means the extracted
4229    /// edges are wrong or the data is corrupt).
4230    ///
4231    /// # Panics
4232    /// Panics if any hash in the CSR exceeds `u32::MAX` local ID space.
4233    pub fn walk_ancestors(
4234        &self,
4235        collection_id: &[u8; 16],
4236        frontier: &[[u8; 16]],
4237        stop_at: &[[u8; 16]],
4238        extract_edges: impl Fn(&[u8; 16], &[u8]) -> Vec<[u8; 16]>,
4239        limits: WalkLimits,
4240    ) -> Result<DagWalk, StorageError> {
4241        let Some(gen) = self.generation(collection_id).map(|g| Arc::clone(&g)) else {
4242            return Ok(DagWalk {
4243                order: Vec::new().into_iter(),
4244                resolved: HashMap::new(),
4245            });
4246        };
4247
4248        let ((live_hashes, adjacency), resolved) =
4249            self.bfs_ancestors(&gen, frontier, stop_at, &extract_edges, limits)?;
4250
4251        let csr = Csr::build_from_edges(&live_hashes, &adjacency);
4252        let mut topo = csr.topo_order();
4253
4254        if topo.len() != live_hashes.len() {
4255            return Err(StorageError::Corrupt(format!(
4256                "walk_ancestors: cyclic graph detected — topo_order produced {} nodes from {} live hashes",
4257                topo.len(),
4258                live_hashes.len(),
4259            )));
4260        }
4261
4262        // `extract_edges` points each node at its parents, so Kahn's
4263        // algorithm (which surfaces zero-incoming nodes first) naturally
4264        // yields children before parents. Reverse it: callers of an
4265        // ancestor walk want parents-before-children, the order data
4266        // could actually be replayed in.
4267        topo.reverse();
4268
4269        let order: Vec<[u8; 16]> = topo
4270            .into_iter()
4271            .map(|local| {
4272                *csr.hash_of(local)
4273                    .expect("topo order contains valid local IDs")
4274            })
4275            .collect();
4276
4277        Ok(DagWalk {
4278            order: order.into_iter(),
4279            resolved,
4280        })
4281    }
4282
4283    /// After a repack, scan all collections' indexes and retire any shard that
4284    /// no collection references.
4285    ///
4286    /// Acquires every collection's `put_mutex` (in sorted order, skipping the
4287    /// caller's already-held lock) to prevent a concurrent `put()` from
4288    /// landing a write in a shard whose index entry hasn't been inserted
4289    /// yet — without that, the scan could see zero references to a shard
4290    /// that a writer just committed bytes to but hasn't index-updated yet,
4291    /// causing a live shard to be retired under it.
4292    fn retire_empty_shards(&self, held_collection: &[u8; 16]) {
4293        // Collect collection IDs in sorted order for deadlock-free lock acquisition.
4294        // Skip the collection whose put_mutex the caller already holds.
4295        let mut collections_to_lock: Vec<[u8; 16]> = self
4296            .collections
4297            .read()
4298            .keys()
4299            .filter(|id| *id != held_collection)
4300            .copied()
4301            .collect();
4302        collections_to_lock.sort_unstable();
4303
4304        // Acquire all other collections' put_mutexes. The sorted order prevents
4305        // deadlocks; the held collection is skipped (parking_lot is non-reentrant).
4306        let mutexes: Vec<_> = collections_to_lock
4307            .iter()
4308            .map(|id| self.put_mutex(id))
4309            .collect();
4310        let _guards: Vec<_> = mutexes.iter().map(|m| m.lock()).collect();
4311
4312        self.retire_empty_shards_locked();
4313    }
4314
4315    /// Retire unreferenced shards after a batch has released all collection locks.
4316    /// This takes every collection lock itself, unlike [`Self::retire_empty_shards`]
4317    /// which is called while one particular collection lock is already held.
4318    fn retire_empty_shards_after_batch(&self) {
4319        let mut collection_ids: Vec<[u8; 16]> = self.collections.read().keys().copied().collect();
4320        collection_ids.sort_unstable();
4321        let mutexes: Vec<_> = collection_ids.iter().map(|id| self.put_mutex(id)).collect();
4322        let _guards: Vec<_> = mutexes.iter().map(|mutex| mutex.lock()).collect();
4323        self.retire_empty_shards_locked();
4324    }
4325
4326    /// Every collection writer lock is held by the caller.
4327    fn retire_empty_shards_locked(&self) {
4328        // Do not retain the map guard while acquiring collection locks: another
4329        // operation may need the map lock while it holds a collection lock.
4330        let collections = self.collections.read();
4331
4332        // Build the union of shard IDs referenced across all collections.
4333        let mut referenced = [false; shard::MAX_SHARDS];
4334        for gen_swap in collections.values() {
4335            let gen = gen_swap.load();
4336            let ids = gen.index.referenced_shard_ids();
4337            for (i, &has_refs) in ids.iter().enumerate() {
4338                referenced[i] |= has_refs;
4339            }
4340        }
4341
4342        // Retire any shard not in the union.
4343        for id in 0..u16::try_from(shard::MAX_SHARDS).unwrap() {
4344            if !referenced[id as usize] {
4345                self.shards.retire_slot(id);
4346            }
4347        }
4348    }
4349}
4350
4351impl StorageEngine for PackfileStorage {
4352    fn get(&self, collection_id: &[u8; 16], id: &NodeId) -> Result<Option<NodeData>, StorageError> {
4353        let track = self.stats_enabled.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
4354        let gen_guard = self.generation(collection_id);
4355        let gen = gen_guard.as_deref();
4356
4357        let Some(gen) = gen else {
4358            if track {
4359                self.get_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
4360                self.get_misses.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
4361            }
4362            return Ok(None);
4363        };
4364        if let Some(data) = gen.cache.get(id) {
4365            if track {
4366                self.get_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
4367            }
4368            return Ok(Some((*data).clone()));
4369        }
4370        let result = self.resolve_from_candidates(id, gen.index.lookup_all(id));
4371        if track {
4372            self.get_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
4373            if result.as_ref().is_ok_and(Option::is_none) {
4374                self.get_misses.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
4375            }
4376        }
4377        result
4378    }
4379
4380    fn get_many(
4381        &self,
4382        collection_id: &[u8; 16],
4383        ids: &[NodeId],
4384    ) -> Result<Vec<Option<NodeData>>, StorageError> {
4385        let track = self.stats_enabled.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
4386        let mut results: Vec<Option<NodeData>> = vec![None; ids.len()];
4387
4388        let gen_guard = self.generation(collection_id);
4389        let gen = gen_guard.as_deref();
4390
4391        let mut to_fetch: Vec<(usize, Vec<(u16, u64)>)> = Vec::new();
4392        if let Some(g) = gen {
4393            for (i, id) in ids.iter().enumerate() {
4394                // The generation-local cache only contains records from this
4395                // generation, so it is authoritative for hits. Avoid an
4396                // otherwise redundant index probe for the common warm case.
4397                if let Some(data) = g.cache.get(id) {
4398                    results[i] = Some((*data).clone());
4399                    continue;
4400                }
4401                let candidates: Vec<(u16, u64)> = g.index.lookup_all(id).collect();
4402                if !candidates.is_empty() {
4403                    to_fetch.push((i, candidates));
4404                }
4405            }
4406        }
4407
4408        to_fetch.sort_unstable_by_key(|(_, candidates)| candidates[0]);
4409
4410        for (i, candidates) in &to_fetch {
4411            results[*i] = self.resolve_from_candidates(&ids[*i], candidates.iter().copied())?;
4412        }
4413
4414        if track {
4415            self.get_many_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
4416            self.get_many_records
4417                .fetch_add(ids.len() as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
4418            self.get_many_misses.fetch_add(
4419                results.iter().filter(|r| r.is_none()).count() as u64,
4420                Ordering::Relaxed,
4421            );
4422        }
4423
4424        Ok(results)
4425    }
4426
4427    fn put(
4428        &self,
4429        collection_id: &[u8; 16],
4430        id: &NodeId,
4431        data: &NodeData,
4432    ) -> Result<(), StorageError> {
4433        self.put_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
4434        self.put_bytes
4435            .fetch_add(data.bytes.len() as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
4436        let collection_arc = self.put_mutex(collection_id);
4437        let _collection_guard = collection_arc.lock();
4438
4439        // New-collection puts must not interleave their record flush with a
4440        // concurrent checkpoint's fingerprint→snapshot window (see
4441        // `persist_index_checkpoint`). Existing collections are already gated
4442        // by their `put_mutex`; a brand-new one isn't in that set yet, so we
4443        // hold the publication lock shared for the whole put instead — the
4444        // checkpoint holds it exclusive, excluding this put from the window.
4445        let _create_guard = if self.generation(collection_id).is_none() {
4446            Some(self.collection_creation.read())
4447        } else {
4448            None
4449        };
4450
4451        let record = Record {
4452            collection_id: *collection_id,
4453            hash: *id,
4454            data: data.bytes.clone(),
4455        };
4456        let (shard_id, offset) = self.shards.put_record(&record)?;
4457        if let Some(gen) = self.generation(collection_id) {
4458            if !gen.index.is_mmap_backed() {
4459                if let Ok((bucket, slot)) =
4460                    self.insert_index(collection_id, &gen.index, id, shard_id, offset)?
4461                {
4462                    self.record_delta(collection_id, gen.generation, bucket, slot);
4463                    let pack_id = self
4464                        .shards
4465                        .get_shard(shard_id)
4466                        .map_or(u64::from(shard_id), |shard| shard.pack_id);
4467                    self.record_new_shard_collection(pack_id, collection_id);
4468
4469                    let mut data_to_cache = data.clone();
4470                    for child in &mut data_to_cache.children {
4471                        if let NodeRef::Lazy(child_id) = child {
4472                            if let Some(child_data) = self.pinned.get(child_id) {
4473                                *child = NodeRef::Resolved(*child_id, child_data);
4474                            }
4475                        }
4476                    }
4477                    gen.cache.insert(*id, Arc::new(data_to_cache));
4478                    self.index_checkpoint_dirty.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
4479                    return Ok(());
4480                }
4481            }
4482        }
4483        let (index, cache) = {
4484            let old_gen = self.generation(collection_id);
4485            let mut index = match &old_gen {
4486                Some(g) => g.index.clone(),
4487                // A brand-new collection's first insert: no count hint to
4488                // size from (unlike `put_many`, which knows `entries.len()`),
4489                // so start at a small fixed floor and let `grow()` size it
4490                // up as real entries land. Previously this was a flat
4491                // 4096-slot (32 KiB) floor per collection regardless of how
4492                // many records it would ever hold -- workloads with many
4493                // small/near-empty collections (e.g. per-room collections
4494                // holding a handful of records each) paid that 32 KiB up
4495                // front per collection, dwarfing the actual data by orders
4496                // of magnitude.
4497                //
4498                // See `NEW_COLLECTION_INDEX_FLOOR` for why this isn't the
4499                // index's own smaller hard floor.
4500                None => LossyIndex::new(NEW_COLLECTION_INDEX_FLOOR),
4501            };
4502            let inserted = self.insert_index(collection_id, &index, id, shard_id, offset)?;
4503            if let Ok((bucket, slot)) = inserted {
4504                let generation = old_gen.as_ref().map_or(1, |g| g.generation);
4505                self.record_delta(collection_id, generation, bucket, slot);
4506                let pack_id = self
4507                    .shards
4508                    .get_shard(shard_id)
4509                    .map_or(u64::from(shard_id), |s| s.pack_id);
4510                self.record_new_shard_collection(pack_id, collection_id);
4511            } else {
4512                // The insert was rejected (table full). The collection's shape
4513                // is about to change, so any delta frames would no longer be
4514                // replayable against a checkpoint at this generation.
4515                self.invalidate_delta_log();
4516                if let Some(grown) = index.grow() {
4517                    // The failed insert did not mutate the table, so retry it
4518                    // after the in-memory rehash. This is the normal capacity
4519                    // path and must not turn into a full-pack scan.
4520                    let _ = grown.insert(id, shard_id, offset);
4521                    index = grown;
4522                } else if let Ok(Some(grown)) = self.grow_checkpoint_index(collection_id, &index) {
4523                    // A checkpoint-backed index has locations but not homes.
4524                    // Recovering the hashes from those locations is bounded
4525                    // by this collection, unlike `rebuild_index`'s pack scan.
4526                    let _ = grown.insert(id, shard_id, offset);
4527                    index = grown;
4528                } else {
4529                    index = self.rebuild_index(collection_id)?;
4530                    let _ = index.insert(id, shard_id, offset);
4531                }
4532                // The rebuild re-derived the collection's entire live set from
4533                // scratch, so its shard distribution needs a full
4534                // recompute too, not just crediting this one record.
4535                self.replace_collection_shard_counts(
4536                    collection_id,
4537                    &self.slot_counts_to_pack_id_counts(&index.shard_counts()),
4538                );
4539            }
4540            let cache = match &old_gen {
4541                Some(g) => g.cache.clone(),
4542                None => Arc::new(NodeCache::new(self.cache_capacity)),
4543            };
4544
4545            let mut data_to_cache = data.clone();
4546            for child in &mut data_to_cache.children {
4547                if let NodeRef::Lazy(child_id) = child {
4548                    if let Some(child_data) = self.pinned.get(child_id) {
4549                        *child = NodeRef::Resolved(*child_id, child_data);
4550                    }
4551                }
4552            }
4553            cache.insert(*id, Arc::new(data_to_cache));
4554
4555            (index, cache)
4556        };
4557
4558        self.store_generation(collection_id, index, Some(cache), false)?;
4559
4560        Ok(())
4561    }
4562
4563    #[allow(
4564        clippy::too_many_lines,
4565        reason = "the live-index fast path and its clone-on-demand fallback read clearest \
4566                  kept together rather than split across helpers that would each need most \
4567                  of the same state (old_gen, owned_index, structural_change) threaded through"
4568    )]
4569    fn put_many(
4570        &self,
4571        collection_id: &[u8; 16],
4572        entries: &[(NodeId, NodeData)],
4573    ) -> Result<(), StorageError> {
4574        if entries.is_empty() {
4575            return Ok(());
4576        }
4577
4578        self.put_many_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
4579        self.put_many_records
4580            .fetch_add(entries.len() as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
4581        self.put_many_bytes.fetch_add(
4582            entries
4583                .iter()
4584                .map(|(_, data)| data.bytes.len() as u64)
4585                .sum::<u64>(),
4586            Ordering::Relaxed,
4587        );
4588
4589        let collection_arc = self.put_mutex(collection_id);
4590        let _collection_guard = collection_arc.lock();
4591
4592        // Same comment as in `put`: a brand-new collection must be excluded
4593        // from a concurrent checkpoint's fingerprint→snapshot window.
4594        let _create_guard = if self.generation(collection_id).is_none() {
4595            Some(self.collection_creation.read())
4596        } else {
4597            None
4598        };
4599
4600        let old_gen = self.generation(collection_id);
4601        let generation = old_gen.as_ref().map_or(1, |g| g.generation);
4602        let cache = match &old_gen {
4603            Some(g) => g.cache.clone(),
4604            None => Arc::new(NodeCache::new(self.cache_capacity)),
4605        };
4606
4607        // Batches use copy-on-write even when their current index is already
4608        // materialized. That is deliberately unlike `put`: a batch can fail
4609        // after an earlier record append, so its index changes must remain
4610        // private until every fallible operation has succeeded.
4611        let materialize_started = std::time::Instant::now();
4612        // A batch is published as one generation. In particular, never
4613        // insert directly into the currently published index: a later shard
4614        // append can fail, and callers must not observe the successful prefix
4615        // of that failed batch.
4616        let mut owned_index: Option<LossyIndex> = match &old_gen {
4617            Some(g) => Some(g.index.clone()),
4618            // Unlike `put`'s single-record path, we know exactly how many
4619            // records this brand-new collection is about to receive -- size
4620            // from that instead of the old flat 4096-slot (32 KiB) floor,
4621            // matching the `records.len().saturating_mul(2).max(16)` pattern
4622            // used elsewhere in this file (e.g. checkpoint/pack rebuild).
4623            // Floor is 64, not 16 -- see the matching comment on `put`'s
4624            // `None` arm above: a small batch (e.g. a single-event
4625            // `put_many` call) would otherwise still hit `grow()` almost
4626            // immediately, invalidating the delta log and forcing a full
4627            // checkpoint rewrite on the next sync.
4628            None => Some(LossyIndex::new(
4629                entries
4630                    .len()
4631                    .saturating_mul(2)
4632                    .max(NEW_COLLECTION_INDEX_FLOOR),
4633            )),
4634        };
4635        // Every non-empty batch materializes an owned index up front so its
4636        // contents can be published atomically.
4637        if owned_index.is_some() {
4638            self.put_many_clone_path_calls
4639                .fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
4640            self.index_clone_time_ns.fetch_add(
4641                u64::try_from(materialize_started.elapsed().as_nanos()).unwrap_or(u64::MAX),
4642                Ordering::Relaxed,
4643            );
4644        } else {
4645            self.put_many_fast_path_calls
4646                .fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
4647        }
4648        // The owned index is always published after a successful batch.
4649        let mut structural_change = owned_index.is_some();
4650        let mut index_needs_rebuild = false;
4651        let mut pending_deltas = Vec::with_capacity(entries.len());
4652        let mut pending_shard_collections = Vec::with_capacity(entries.len());
4653        let mut invalidate_delta = false;
4654
4655        for (id, data) in entries {
4656            let record = Record {
4657                collection_id: *collection_id,
4658                hash: *id,
4659                data: data.bytes.clone(),
4660            };
4661
4662            let (shard_id, offset) = self.shards.put_record(&record)?;
4663
4664            if index_needs_rebuild {
4665                continue;
4666            }
4667
4668            // Whichever index is authoritative for this insert: the owned
4669            // copy once one exists, else the collection's live, still-shared
4670            // index — only reachable here when it's neither mmap-backed nor
4671            // absent, both of which already forced `owned_index` above.
4672            let live: &LossyIndex = match owned_index.as_ref() {
4673                Some(index) => index,
4674                None => {
4675                    &old_gen
4676                        .as_ref()
4677                        .expect("live path implies an existing generation")
4678                        .index
4679                }
4680            };
4681
4682            let inserted = if let Ok((bucket, slot)) =
4683                self.insert_index(collection_id, live, id, shard_id, offset)?
4684            {
4685                pending_deltas.push((bucket, slot));
4686                true
4687            } else {
4688                // Growth (either flavor) materializes a fresh owned index; the
4689                // O(n) copy is exactly what the steady-append scaler tracks,
4690                // so bag its time alongside the up-front materialization.
4691                let grow_started = std::time::Instant::now();
4692                let grown = if let Some(grown) = live.grow() {
4693                    // `insert_tracked` leaves the table unchanged on
4694                    // TableFull, so retrying with the record's
4695                    // still-available location is sufficient; no pack scan is
4696                    // needed for pure growth. The grow changes the
4697                    // collection's shape, so the delta log is invalidated and
4698                    // no frame is recorded for this (or any later)
4699                    // overwrite in the batch.
4700                    invalidate_delta = true;
4701                    structural_change = true;
4702                    grown
4703                } else if let Ok(Some(grown)) = self.grow_checkpoint_index(collection_id, live) {
4704                    // The checkpoint does not retain homes, but its slots
4705                    // retain locations. Recover only those identities and
4706                    // retry; do not scan every pack in the store.
4707                    invalidate_delta = true;
4708                    structural_change = true;
4709                    grown
4710                } else {
4711                    index_needs_rebuild = true;
4712                    structural_change = true;
4713                    invalidate_delta = true;
4714                    continue;
4715                };
4716                self.index_grow_count.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
4717                self.index_clone_time_ns.fetch_add(
4718                    u64::try_from(grow_started.elapsed().as_nanos()).unwrap_or(u64::MAX),
4719                    Ordering::Relaxed,
4720                );
4721                let inserted = grown.insert(id, shard_id, offset).is_ok();
4722                owned_index = Some(grown);
4723                inserted
4724            };
4725            if inserted {
4726                let pack_id = self
4727                    .shards
4728                    .get_shard(shard_id)
4729                    .map_or(u64::from(shard_id), |s| s.pack_id);
4730                pending_shard_collections.push(pack_id);
4731            }
4732        }
4733
4734        if index_needs_rebuild {
4735            let rebuilt = self.rebuild_index(collection_id)?;
4736            // rebuild_index automatically discovers all the records we just appended
4737            self.replace_collection_shard_counts(
4738                collection_id,
4739                &self.slot_counts_to_pack_id_counts(&rebuilt.shard_counts()),
4740            );
4741            owned_index = Some(rebuilt);
4742        }
4743
4744        // All fallible work is complete. Only now make the batch visible to
4745        // the shared index, delta state, and shard bookkeeping.
4746        if invalidate_delta {
4747            self.invalidate_delta_log();
4748        } else {
4749            for (bucket, slot) in pending_deltas {
4750                self.record_delta(collection_id, generation, bucket, slot);
4751            }
4752        }
4753        for pack_id in pending_shard_collections {
4754            self.record_new_shard_collection(pack_id, collection_id);
4755        }
4756
4757        // Apply cache mutations only after all disk writes succeed, so a
4758        // failed batch does not leak partial state into the shared cache.
4759        // Resolve and insert one entry at a time: retaining a prepared clone
4760        // of the entire batch here would defeat the cache's size bound.
4761        for (id, data) in entries {
4762            let mut data_to_cache = data.clone();
4763            for child in &mut data_to_cache.children {
4764                if let NodeRef::Lazy(child_id) = child {
4765                    if let Some(child_data) = self.pinned.get(child_id) {
4766                        *child = NodeRef::Resolved(*child_id, child_data);
4767                    }
4768                }
4769            }
4770            cache.insert(*id, Arc::new(data_to_cache));
4771        }
4772
4773        if structural_change {
4774            let index = owned_index
4775                .expect("structural_change is only set once owned_index is materialized");
4776            self.store_generation(collection_id, index, Some(cache), false)?;
4777        } else {
4778            // Every record landed on the live, already-published index in
4779            // place -- no new generation to publish, matching `put`'s
4780            // in-place success path.
4781            self.index_checkpoint_dirty.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
4782        }
4783
4784        Ok(())
4785    }
4786
4787    fn delete_collection(&self, collection_id: &[u8; 16]) -> Result<(), StorageError> {
4788        // Acquire the collection's put mutex to serialize with any in-flight put,
4789        // preventing a concurrent put from resurrecting the collection after we
4790        // remove it from the generation map.
4791        let collection_arc = self.put_mutex(collection_id);
4792        let _collection_guard = collection_arc.lock();
4793
4794        self.collections.write().remove(collection_id);
4795        self.live_roots.write().remove(collection_id);
4796        // A deletion changes the collection directory the same way a refill
4797        // does, so any pending delta frames are no longer replayable against
4798        // the checkpoint they were recorded against.
4799        self.invalidate_delta_log();
4800        self.index_checkpoint_dirty.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
4801        // Keep lock entries for the storage lifetime. Removing an entry while
4802        // a caller still owns its Arc permits a later put to obtain a second
4803        // mutex and bypass this deletion's serialization.
4804        self.remove_collection_shard_counts(collection_id);
4805        self.persist_deleted_collection(collection_id)?;
4806        Ok(())
4807    }
4808
4809    fn sync(&self) -> Result<(), StorageError> {
4810        let started = std::time::Instant::now();
4811        let mut timings = SyncTimings::default();
4812        self.shards.sync_dirty()?;
4813        if let Some((flush, fsync)) = self.shards.last_sync_split() {
4814            timings.pack_flush = flush;
4815            timings.pack_fsync = fsync;
4816        }
4817        self.persist_index_checkpoint_or_delta(&mut timings);
4818        timings.total = started.elapsed();
4819        self.count_sync_persistence(&timings);
4820        *self.last_sync_timings.lock() = Some(timings);
4821        Ok(())
4822    }
4823
4824    fn refresh_collection(&self, collection_id: &[u8; 16]) -> Result<(), StorageError> {
4825        Self::refresh_collection(self, collection_id)
4826    }
4827}
4828
4829impl PackfileStorage {
4830    /// Sync all open shards to disk (full pool, not just dirty).
4831    ///
4832    /// Also persists the shard→collection directory as a side effect, same as
4833    /// `ShardPool::sync_all` persists shard IO stats — an explicit sync is
4834    /// a natural point to flush this observability data too.
4835    ///
4836    /// # Errors
4837    /// Returns `StorageError` on I/O failure.
4838    pub fn sync_all(&self) -> Result<(), StorageError> {
4839        let started = std::time::Instant::now();
4840        let mut timings = SyncTimings::default();
4841        self.shards.sync_all()?;
4842        if let Some((flush, fsync)) = self.shards.last_sync_split() {
4843            timings.pack_flush = flush;
4844            timings.pack_fsync = fsync;
4845        }
4846        let sidecar_started = std::time::Instant::now();
4847        self.persist_shard_collections_best_effort();
4848        timings.sidecar = sidecar_started.elapsed();
4849        self.persist_index_checkpoint_or_delta(&mut timings);
4850        timings.total = started.elapsed();
4851        self.count_sync_persistence(&timings);
4852        *self.last_sync_timings.lock() = Some(timings);
4853        Ok(())
4854    }
4855
4856    /// Batch-granular sync accounting: every sync counts once, and the
4857    /// checkpoint-vs-delta discriminator comes from which phase
4858    /// `persist_index_checkpoint_or_delta` actually ran (a non-dirty sync runs
4859    /// neither). `sync` (dirty-scoped) and `sync_all` both funnel through here.
4860    fn count_sync_persistence(&self, timings: &SyncTimings) {
4861        self.sync_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
4862        if !timings.checkpoint.is_zero() {
4863            self.checkpoint_writes.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
4864        } else if !timings.delta_log.is_zero() {
4865            self.delta_appends.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
4866        }
4867    }
4868    /// Whether the pending mutation set must be persisted as a full index
4869    /// checkpoint rather than a delta append. A delta append can only cover a
4870    /// log that has been continued continuously from the last checkpoint: any
4871    /// structural invalidation, a missing log base, an empty frame set, or a
4872    /// log grown past `DELTA_LOG_CAP_BYTES` breaks that chain — the delta
4873    /// path would either replay garbage or never terminate (grow forever).
4874    fn delta_state_needs_full_rewrite(&self) -> bool {
4875        let state = self.delta_state.lock();
4876        if state.invalid || state.base_fingerprint.is_none() || state.pending.is_empty() {
4877            return true;
4878        }
4879        let Some(batch_bytes) = delta::batch_len(state.pending.len()) else {
4880            return true;
4881        };
4882        state
4883            .log_bytes
4884            .saturating_add(u64::try_from(batch_bytes).unwrap_or(u64::MAX))
4885            .saturating_add(if state.log_bytes == 0 {
4886                DELTA_LOG_HEADER_LEN as u64
4887            } else {
4888                0
4889            })
4890            > DELTA_LOG_CAP_BYTES
4891    }
4892
4893    /// Persist the dirty index state for a sync barrier — a delta append when
4894    /// the log can be continued, otherwise a full checkpoint rewrite — and
