4895    /// record which path ran in `timings`. No-op when nothing is dirty.
4896    fn persist_index_checkpoint_or_delta(&self, timings: &mut SyncTimings) {
4897        if !self.index_checkpoint_dirty.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
4898            return;
4899        }
4900        if self.delta_state_needs_full_rewrite() {
4901            let checkpoint_started = std::time::Instant::now();
4902            self.persist_index_checkpoint_best_effort();
4903            timings.checkpoint = checkpoint_started.elapsed();
4904            return;
4905        }
4906        // The frames were recorded against the pack set the preceding flush
4907        // made durable, so the tail fingerprint is computed after that flush.
4908        let tail_fingerprint = self.current_pack_fingerprint();
4909        let delta_started = std::time::Instant::now();
4910        if let Err(error) = self.append_index_delta(tail_fingerprint) {
4911            // An append failure took the frames with it, so the pending state
4912            // no longer reflects the live indexes. Fall back to a full
4913            // rewrite rather than leaving the acceleration files stale until
4914            // the next sync notices the gap.
4915            eprintln!("mtxdb: delta log append failed, rewriting checkpoint: {error}");
4916            let checkpoint_started = std::time::Instant::now();
4917            self.persist_index_checkpoint_best_effort();
4918            timings.checkpoint = checkpoint_started.elapsed();
4919        } else {
4920            timings.delta_log = delta_started.elapsed();
4921        }
4922    }
4923
4924    /// Fingerprint of the current on-disk pack set, computed from each
4925    /// shard's committed length. Must be called after the flush that made the
4926    /// bytes corresponding to outstanding index frames durable, so the file
4927    /// lengths reflect exactly what the indexes' offsets point at.
4928    fn current_pack_fingerprint(&self) -> u64 {
4929        let packs: Vec<(u64, u64)> = self
4930            .shards
4931            .all_shards()
4932            .into_iter()
4933            .map(|(_, shard)| (shard.pack_id, shard.file_len()))
4934            .collect();
4935        crate::index::checkpoint::pack_fingerprint(&packs)
4936    }
4937
4938    /// Wall-clock breakdown of this store's most recent `open`, including
4939    /// which index-loading path was taken. Set at construction; every store
4940    /// returned by a `PackfileStorage::open*` constructor carries it.
4941    #[must_use]
4942    pub fn open_timings(&self) -> Option<OpenTimings> {
4943        *self.last_open_timings.lock()
4944    }
4945
4946    /// Wall-clock breakdown of the most recent sync — `sync()`, `sync_all`,
4947    /// or the bench's internal sync calls — by phase.
4948    #[must_use]
4949    pub fn sync_timings(&self) -> Option<SyncTimings> {
4950        *self.last_sync_timings.lock()
4951    }
4952
4953    /// Commit every open shard's buffered frames to the page cache without
4954    /// fsyncing (see [`ShardPool::flush_all`]). No-op under the default
4955    /// [`crate::shard::AppendPolicy::Eager`], where every put already wrote
4956    /// its own frame.
4957    ///
4958    /// Under [`crate::shard::AppendPolicy::Buffered`], a put's bytes live
4959    /// only in process memory until a flush, and only in the page cache
4960    /// until a `sync_all`. This is the explicit way to advance that boundary
4961    /// for all shards. A flushed store also means a subsequent
4962    /// `PackfileStorage::open`/`open_read_only` against the same directory
4963    /// sees the data.
4964    ///
4965    /// # Errors
4966    /// Returns `StorageError` on I/O failure.
4967    pub fn flush_all(&self) -> Result<(), StorageError> {
4968        Ok(self.shards.flush_all()?)
4969    }
4970
4971    /// Replace this store's append policy — whether frames go to disk per
4972    /// put (the default [`crate::shard::AppendPolicy::Eager`], historical
4973    /// behavior) or accumulate for one positioned write per flush
4974    /// ([`crate::shard::AppendPolicy::Buffered`]). See
4975    /// [`crate::shard::AppendPolicy`] for the visibility/durability trade.
4976    ///
4977    /// Only affects future puts, so call it before the store starts handling
4978    /// concurrent writes. For a batch import that ends in `sync_all` — or a
4979    /// benchmark that syncs explicitly — `Buffered` usually wins:
4980    ///
4981    /// ```
4982    /// use mtxdb_core::shard::AppendPolicy;
4983    /// use mtxdb_core::PackfileStorage;
4984    /// # let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join("mtxdb-doc-with-append-policy");
4985    /// let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone())
4986    ///     .unwrap()
4987    ///     .with_append_policy(AppendPolicy::buffered());
4988    /// # std::fs::remove_dir_all(dir).ok();
4989    /// ```
4990    #[must_use]
4991    pub fn with_append_policy(mut self, policy: shard::AppendPolicy) -> Self {
4992        self.shards.set_append_policy(policy);
4993        self
4994    }
4995
4996    /// Best-effort, rate-limited flush of the shard→collection directory for a
4997    /// writer's own periodic tick — same contract as
4998    /// `ShardPool::maybe_persist_stats`: a no-op if called again before
4999    /// `min_interval` has passed since the last flush from here.
5000    pub fn maybe_persist_shard_collections(&self, min_interval: std::time::Duration) {
5001        let now = std::time::Instant::now();
5002        {
5003            let mut last = self.last_shard_collections_flush.write();
5004            if last.is_some_and(|prev| now.duration_since(prev) < min_interval) {
5005                return;
5006            }
5007            *last = Some(now);
5008        }
5009        self.persist_shard_collections_best_effort();
5010    }
5011
5012    /// Snapshot IO/sync stats for every currently-open shard.
5013    ///
5014    /// Shards are shared across collections, so this is per-shard, not per-collection.
5015    #[must_use]
5016    pub fn shard_stats(&self) -> Vec<(u16, crate::shard::ShardStats)> {
5017        self.shards.all_stats()
5018    }
5019
5020    /// Get IO/sync stats for a single shard by ID.
5021    #[must_use]
5022    pub fn shard_stats_for(&self, shard_id: u16) -> Option<crate::shard::ShardStats> {
5023        self.shards.stats(shard_id)
5024    }
5025
5026    /// Total number of shards retired (garbage-collected after a repack)
5027    /// over this storage's lifetime.
5028    #[must_use]
5029    pub fn shards_retired(&self) -> u64 {
5030        self.shards.retired_count()
5031    }
5032
5033    /// List every currently-open shard with basic size, generation, and
5034    /// IO/sync stats — the data behind a `shards` CLI listing.
5035    ///
5036    /// This needs no collection data at all; a caller that only wants shard-level
5037    /// info (e.g. a `shards`-only inspection tool that must coexist with a
5038    /// live writer) should call `ShardPool::summaries` directly instead of
5039    /// opening a full `PackfileStorage`, which rebuilds every collection's index.
5040    #[must_use]
5041    pub fn shard_summaries(&self) -> Vec<shard::ShardSummary> {
5042        self.shards.summaries()
5043    }
5044
5045    /// Snapshot global repack stats across all collections.
5046    #[must_use]
5047    pub fn repack_stats(&self) -> RepackStats {
5048        RepackStats {
5049            repack_count: self.repack_count.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5050            kept_total: self.repack_kept_total.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5051            dropped_total: self.repack_dropped_total.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5052        }
5053    }
5054
5055    /// Opt in to the logical read-path counters (`get`/`get_many`). Read
5056    /// counters are off by default so the read hot path pays a single relaxed
5057    /// load per logical read at most (never a per-record `fetch_add`); write,
5058    /// batch, and sync counters are always on regardless.
5059    ///
5060    /// Changing the flag is safe at any point and affects only subsequent
5061    /// reads; counters are monotone, so reads disabled by a false-to-true
5062    /// transition are reflected as fewer `get_*` calls, not as zeros.
5063    pub fn set_stats_enabled(&self, enabled: bool) {
5064        self.stats_enabled.store(enabled, Ordering::Relaxed);
5065    }
5066
5067    /// Point-in-time snapshot of this store's runtime counters, plus the
5068    /// always-persisted pool stats embedded alongside them ([`RepackStats`],
5069    /// aggregate [`CacheStats`], per-shard `ShardStats`, index bytes).
5070    ///
5071    /// The logical read counters reflect only reads performed while stats were
5072    /// enabled ([`Self::set_stats_enabled`]); all other counters are lifetime
5073    /// totals since the store was assembled. See [`Self::reset_stats`] for the
5074    /// reset semantics — `shards`/`cache`/`repack` reflect persisted state and
5075    /// are deliberately excluded from the reset.
5076    #[must_use]
5077    #[allow(
5078        clippy::cast_precision_loss,
5079        reason = "a hit-rate is inherently an approximate floating-point presentation; counters retain their exact u64 values"
5080    )]
5081    pub fn stats(&self) -> RuntimeStats {
5082        let mut cache = CacheStats::default();
5083        let mut index_bytes: u64 = 0;
5084        let summaries = self.collection_summaries();
5085        for summary in &summaries {
5086            index_bytes = index_bytes.saturating_add(u64::try_from(summary.2).unwrap_or(u64::MAX));
5087            if let Some(stats) = self.cache_stats_for(&summary.0) {
5088                cache.hits = cache.hits.saturating_add(stats.hits);
5089                cache.misses = cache.misses.saturating_add(stats.misses);
5090            }
5091        }
5092        let cache_accesses = cache.hits.saturating_add(cache.misses);
5093        cache.hit_rate = if cache_accesses > 0 {
5094            cache.hits as f64 / cache_accesses as f64
5095        } else {
5096            0.0
5097        };
5098        RuntimeStats {
5099            open_count: self.open_count.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5100            get_calls: self.get_calls.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5101            get_misses: self.get_misses.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5102            get_many_calls: self.get_many_calls.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5103            get_many_records: self.get_many_records.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5104            get_many_misses: self.get_many_misses.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5105            put_calls: self.put_calls.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5106            put_bytes: self.put_bytes.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5107            put_many_calls: self.put_many_calls.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5108            put_many_records: self.put_many_records.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5109            put_many_bytes: self.put_many_bytes.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5110            put_many_fast_path_calls: self.put_many_fast_path_calls.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5111            put_many_clone_path_calls: self.put_many_clone_path_calls.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5112            index_clone_time: std::time::Duration::from_nanos(
5113                self.index_clone_time_ns.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5114            ),
5115            index_grow_count: self.index_grow_count.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5116            index_rebuild_count: self.index_rebuild_count.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5117            delta_invalidations: self.delta_invalidations.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5118            checkpoint_writes: self.checkpoint_writes.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5119            delta_appends: self.delta_appends.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5120            sync_calls: self.sync_calls.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
5121            last_open_timings: self.open_timings(),
5122            last_sync_timings: self.sync_timings(),
5123            repack: self.repack_stats(),
5124            cache,
5125            shards: self.shard_stats(),
5126            index_bytes,
5127            collection_count: summaries.len(),
5128        }
5129    }
5130
5131    /// Zero every runtime counter except `open_count` (counts stores
5132    /// assembled, not work) and the persisted pool stats — `shards`,
5133    /// `cache`, `repack`, `index_bytes`, and the collection count reflect
5134    /// on-disk state plus this process's decoded-cache and repack history and
5135    /// are kept as-is so a reset means "restart the runtime accounting," not
5136    /// "lie about the database."
5137    ///
5138    /// Also clears the retained `last_open_timings`/`last_sync_timings`
5139    /// breakdowns and leaves the `stats_enabled` flag untouched.
5140    pub fn reset_stats(&self) {
5141        for counter in [
5142            &self.get_calls,
5143            &self.get_misses,
5144            &self.get_many_calls,
5145            &self.get_many_records,
5146            &self.get_many_misses,
5147            &self.put_calls,
5148            &self.put_bytes,
5149            &self.put_many_calls,
5150            &self.put_many_records,
5151            &self.put_many_bytes,
5152            &self.put_many_fast_path_calls,
5153            &self.put_many_clone_path_calls,
5154            &self.index_clone_time_ns,
5155            &self.index_grow_count,
5156            &self.index_rebuild_count,
5157            &self.delta_invalidations,
5158            &self.checkpoint_writes,
5159            &self.delta_appends,
5160            &self.sync_calls,
5161        ] {
5162            counter.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
5163        }
5164        *self.last_open_timings.lock() = None;
5165        *self.last_sync_timings.lock() = None;
5166    }
5167
5168    /// Number of times a specific collection has been repacked. 0 if it has
5169    /// never been repacked (or doesn't exist).
5170    #[must_use]
5171    pub fn repack_count_for_collection(&self, collection_id: &[u8; 16]) -> u64 {
5172        self.repack_counts_by_collection
5173            .read()
5174            .get(collection_id)
5175            .copied()
5176            .unwrap_or(0)
5177    }
5178
5179    /// Cache hit/miss stats for a collection's decoded-node cache, if the collection
5180    /// currently has one loaded.
5181    #[must_use]
5182    pub fn cache_stats_for(&self, collection_id: &[u8; 16]) -> Option<CacheStats> {
5183        let gen = self.generation(collection_id)?;
5184        Some(CacheStats {
5185            hits: gen.cache.hits(),
5186            misses: gen.cache.misses(),
5187            hit_rate: gen.cache.hit_rate(),
5188        })
5189    }
5190}

5192/// Snapshot of global repack activity across all collections.
5193#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]
5194pub struct RepackStats {
5195    /// Total number of `repack_collection_reachable` calls across all collections.
5196    pub repack_count: u64,
5197    /// Total records kept (rewritten into a new generation) across all repacks.
5198    pub kept_total: u64,
5199    /// Total records dropped (found unreachable) across all repacks.
5200    pub dropped_total: u64,
5201}
5202
5203/// Snapshot of a collection's decoded-node cache hit/miss stats.
5204#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]
5205pub struct CacheStats {
5206    /// Number of cache hits.
5207    pub hits: u64,
5208    /// Number of cache misses.
5209    pub misses: u64,
5210    /// Hit rate as a fraction in `[0.0, 1.0]`.
5211    pub hit_rate: f64,
5212}
5213
5214/// Point-in-time snapshot of a store's runtime counters and the persisted
5215/// pool stats embedded alongside them — see [`PackfileStorage::stats`].
5216///
5217/// All fields are relaxed reads of independent atomics (no shared snapshot
5218/// lock), so a snapshot is a consistent-enough picture, not a single instant
5219/// in time. The read-path counters (`get_*`) are only meaningful while
5220/// [`PackfileStorage::set_stats_enabled`] was true.
5221#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
5222pub struct RuntimeStats {
5223    /// Stores assembled in this process against this directory (1 for a fresh
5224    /// open; not reset by `reset_stats`).
5225    pub open_count: u64,
5226    /// Logical `get` calls (while stats enabled).
5227    pub get_calls: u64,
5228    /// Logical `get` calls that resolved to no record.
5229    pub get_misses: u64,
5230    /// Logical `get_many` calls (while stats enabled).
5231    pub get_many_calls: u64,
5232    /// Records requested across `get_many` calls.
5233    pub get_many_records: u64,
5234    /// `get_many` results that resolved to no record.
5235    pub get_many_misses: u64,
5236    /// Single-record `put` attempts.
5237    pub put_calls: u64,
5238    /// Bytes accepted across single-record `put` attempts.
5239    pub put_bytes: u64,
5240    /// `put_many` calls (batches).
5241    pub put_many_calls: u64,
5242    /// Records written across `put_many` calls.
5243    pub put_many_records: u64,
5244    /// Bytes written across `put_many` calls.
5245    pub put_many_bytes: u64,
5246    /// `put_many` calls on the owned no-clone fast path.
5247    pub put_many_fast_path_calls: u64,
5248    /// `put_many` calls that materialized an owned index up front.
5249    pub put_many_clone_path_calls: u64,
5250    /// Cumulative time spent materializing/growing owned indexes in `put_many`.
5251    pub index_clone_time: std::time::Duration,
5252    /// Index grow events in `put_many`.
5253    pub index_grow_count: u64,
5254    /// Fallback full-scan `rebuild_index` calls.
5255    pub index_rebuild_count: u64,
5256    /// Structural `invalidate_delta_log` calls.
5257    pub delta_invalidations: u64,
5258    /// Syncs that rewrote the index checkpoint in full.
5259    pub checkpoint_writes: u64,
5260    /// Syncs that appended the incremental delta log instead.
5261    pub delta_appends: u64,
5262    /// `sync`/`sync_all` calls.
5263    pub sync_calls: u64,
5264    /// Per-phase breakdown of the most recent open.
5265    pub last_open_timings: Option<OpenTimings>,
5266    /// Per-phase breakdown of the most recent sync.
5267    pub last_sync_timings: Option<SyncTimings>,
5268    /// Cumulative repack activity (persisted across opens).
5269    pub repack: RepackStats,
5270    /// Aggregate decoded-node cache hit/miss across loaded collections.
5271    pub cache: CacheStats,
5272    /// Persisted per-shard write/sync counters.
5273    pub shards: Vec<(u16, crate::shard::ShardStats)>,
5274    /// Total live index bytes across collections.
5275    pub index_bytes: u64,
5276    /// Number of live collections.
5277    pub collection_count: usize,
5278}
5279
5280impl Default for RuntimeStats {
5281    fn default() -> Self {
5282        Self {
5283            open_count: 0,
5284            get_calls: 0,
5285            get_misses: 0,
5286            get_many_calls: 0,
5287            get_many_records: 0,
5288            get_many_misses: 0,
5289            put_calls: 0,
5290            put_bytes: 0,
5291            put_many_calls: 0,
5292            put_many_records: 0,
5293            put_many_bytes: 0,
5294            put_many_fast_path_calls: 0,
5295            put_many_clone_path_calls: 0,
5296            index_clone_time: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
5297            index_grow_count: 0,
5298            index_rebuild_count: 0,
5299            delta_invalidations: 0,
5300            checkpoint_writes: 0,
5301            delta_appends: 0,
5302            sync_calls: 0,
5303            last_open_timings: None,
5304            last_sync_timings: None,
5305            repack: RepackStats::default(),
5306            cache: CacheStats::default(),
5307            shards: Vec::new(),
5308            index_bytes: 0,
5309            collection_count: 0,
5310        }
5311    }
5312}

5314#[cfg(test)]
5315#[cfg_attr(coverage_nightly, coverage(off))]
5316mod tests {
5317    use super::*;
5318
5319    const TEST_COLLECTION: [u8; 16] = [0x01; 16];
5320    const SECOND_COLLECTION: [u8; 16] = [0x02; 16];
5321
5322    /// A node id distinct across both the bucket bytes (0..8) and tag bytes
5323    /// (8..12) the lossy index actually reads — an id that only varies byte
5324    /// 0 collapses every entry onto the same 24-bit tag, which is not a
5325    /// realistic content hash and defeats the index in ways unrelated to
5326    /// whatever a test using it is meant to check.
5327    fn distinct_id(byte: u8) -> [u8; 16] {
5328        let mut id = [0u8; 16];
5329        id[0] = byte;
5330        id[9] = byte.wrapping_mul(37).wrapping_add(11);
5331        id[15] = byte.wrapping_mul(7);
5332        id
5333    }
5334    const OTHER_COLLECTION: [u8; 16] = [0x02; 16];
5335
5336    fn test_dir(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
5337        use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
5338        static COUNTER: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
5339        let id = COUNTER.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
5340        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("mdb_test_pfs_{name}_{id}"));
5341        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
5342        fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
5343        dir
5344    }
5345
5346    fn ten_record_fixture() -> Vec<(NodeId, NodeData)> {
5347        (0..10u8)
5348            .map(|i| {
5349                let mut id = [0u8; 16];
5350                id[0] = i;
5351                id[8..12].copy_from_slice(&(u32::from(i) + 1).to_le_bytes());
5352                (id, NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from(format!("node {i}"))))
5353            })
5354            .collect()
5355    }

5370    #[test]
5371    fn reopened_index_keeps_distinct_same_tag_entries() {
5372        let dir = test_dir("reopen_same_tag");
5373        let mut first = [0u8; 16];
5374        first[8] = 0x42;
5375        first[15] = 1;
5376        let mut second = first;
5377        second[15] = 2;
5378
5379        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone()).unwrap();
5380        store
5381            .put(
5382                &TEST_COLLECTION,
5383                &first,
5384                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"first")),
5385            )
5386            .unwrap();
5387        store.sync_all().unwrap();
5388        drop(store);
5389
5390        // The checkpoint has only slots, no retained full identities. The
5391        // second write must hydrate the same-tag candidate and continue its
5392        // probe, rather than replacing the first record's only location.
5393        let reopened = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
5394        reopened
5395            .put(
5396                &TEST_COLLECTION,
5397                &second,
5398                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"second")),
5399            )
5400            .unwrap();
5401        assert!(reopened.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &first).unwrap().is_some());
5402        assert!(reopened.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &second).unwrap().is_some());
5403    }
5404
5405    #[test]
5406    fn checkpoint_index_growth_recovers_hashes_from_indexed_frames() {
5407        // A batch large enough to be a "real" collection. The checkpoint
5408        // this reopen loads stores locations but not home hashes, so
5409        // growing it (via the `put_many` call below) used to rescan every
5410        // pack for this collection instead of recovering hashes from the
5411        // indexed delta frames.
5412        const ENTRIES: usize = 3_072;
5413        let dir = test_dir("checkpoint_growth_recovery");
5414        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone())
5415            .unwrap()
5416            .with_append_policy(crate::shard::AppendPolicy::buffered());
5417        let entries: Vec<_> = (0..ENTRIES)
5418            .map(|i| {
5419                let mut id = [0u8; 16];
5420                let mixed = (i as u64).wrapping_mul(0x9E37_79B9_7F4A_7C15);
5421                id[..8].copy_from_slice(&mixed.to_be_bytes());
5422                id[8] = u8::try_from((i >> 16) & 0xFF).unwrap();
5423                id[9] = u8::try_from((i >> 8) & 0xFF).unwrap();
5424                id[10] = u8::try_from(i & 0xFF).unwrap();
5425                (id, NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"payload")))
5426            })
5427            .collect();
5428        store.put_many(&TEST_COLLECTION, &entries).unwrap();
5429        store.sync_all().unwrap();
5430        drop(store);
5431
5432        let reopened = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone()).unwrap();
5433        let checkpoint_index = reopened
5434            .generation(&TEST_COLLECTION)
5435            .expect("checkpoint collection exists");
5436        assert!(checkpoint_index.index.is_mmap_backed());
5437        let grown = reopened
5438            .grow_checkpoint_index(&TEST_COLLECTION, &checkpoint_index.index)
5439            .unwrap()
5440            .expect("4K checkpoint index can grow");
5441        assert_eq!(grown.len(), ENTRIES);
5442        for (id, _) in &entries {
5443            assert!(grown.lookup(id).is_some(), "recovered hash remains indexed");
5444        }
5445        drop(checkpoint_index);
5446
5447        // Exercise the actual put_many fallback too. `insert_index` fails
5448        // closed once `len >= capacity * 3 / 4`, and the reopened generation
5449        // is mmap-backed (so `LossyIndex::grow` — which needs the original
5450        // homes — can't apply): crossing that threshold therefore always
5451        // routes through `grow_checkpoint_index`, never a silent plain grow
5452        // or a full pack rescan. Compute the threshold from the checkpoint's
5453        // actual on-disk capacity and add enough unique post-reopen entries
5454        // to be certain we cross it, instead of relying on one extra record
5455        // that may land comfortably under it.
5456        let checkpoint_capacity = u64::from(
5457            reopened
5458                .generation(&TEST_COLLECTION)
5459                .expect("checkpoint collection exists")
5460                .index
5461                .capacity(),
5462        );
5463        let threshold = checkpoint_capacity * 3 / 4;
5464        // Add enough unique entries to certainly cross the threshold,
5465        // regardless of exactly how much headroom the checkpoint's
5466        // persisted capacity happens to have.
5467        let needed = threshold.saturating_sub(u64::try_from(ENTRIES).unwrap_or(0)) + 64;
5468        let extra_entries: Vec<_> = (0..needed)
5469            .map(|i| {
5470                let mut id = [0xA5; 16];
5471                id[..8].copy_from_slice(&i.to_be_bytes());
5472                (id, NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"extra")))
5473            })
5474            .collect();
5475        reopened.put_many(&TEST_COLLECTION, &extra_entries).unwrap();
5476        let grown_capacity = reopened
5477            .generation(&TEST_COLLECTION)
5478            .expect("collection still exists")
5479            .index
5480            .capacity();
5481        assert!(
5482            u64::from(grown_capacity) > checkpoint_capacity,
5483            "put_many must have grown the index past its checkpoint capacity"
5484        );
5485        for (id, _) in &extra_entries {
5486            assert!(reopened.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, id).unwrap().is_some());
5487        }
5488        assert!(reopened
5489            .get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &entries[0].0)
5490            .unwrap()
5491            .is_some());
5492    }
5493
5494    /// A writable open must not silently skip a corrupt pack and publish a
5495    /// partial index. The caller needs an error so it can repair or restore
5496    /// the pack before accepting writes.
5497    #[test]
5498    fn test_writable_open_rejects_corrupt_pack_instead_of_skipping_it() {
5499        let dir = test_dir("open_corrupt_pack");
5500        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone()).unwrap();
5501        let id = distinct_id(0x42);
5502        store
5503            .put(
5504                &TEST_COLLECTION,
5505                &id,
5506                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"payload")),
5507            )
5508            .unwrap();
5509        // Commit the buffered frame so the tamper below actually overwrites
5510        // on-disk bytes (the record is otherwise only in the append buffer).
5511        store.sync_all().unwrap();
5512        let path = store.shards.active_shard().path.clone();
5513        drop(store);
5514
5515        let mut bytes = fs::read(&path).unwrap();
5516        let payload_byte =
5517            crate::packfile::HEADER_LEN + 4 + crate::packfile::FRAME_FIXED_LEN as usize;
5518        bytes[payload_byte] ^= 0xff;
5519        fs::write(path, bytes).unwrap();
5520
5521        assert!(PackfileStorage::open(dir).is_err());
5522    }
5523
5524    /// Full and `WriteOnly` keep writing real CRCs, so a reopen scan still
5525    /// verifies them: a physically tampered pack must fail to reopen.
5526    /// `Disabled` frames carry no checksum, so a tampered pack reopens and
5527    /// the engine serves the corrupted bytes as data.
5528    #[test]
5529    fn test_checksum_policy_gates_verification_on_reopen_scan() {
5530        for (policy, expect_reopen_ok) in [
5531            (crate::packfile::ChecksumPolicy::Full, false),
5532            (crate::packfile::ChecksumPolicy::WriteOnly, false),
5533            (crate::packfile::ChecksumPolicy::Disabled, true),
5534        ] {
5535            let dir = test_dir(&format!("checksum_policy_reopen_{policy:?}"));
5536            {
5537                let store = PackfileStorage::open_with_policies(dir.clone(), true, policy).unwrap();
5538                let id = distinct_id(0x52);
5539                store
5540                    .put(
5541                        &TEST_COLLECTION,
5542                        &id,
5543                        &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"tamper target")),
5544                    )
5545                    .unwrap();
5546                let (shard_id, offset) = store
5547                    .generation(&TEST_COLLECTION)
5548                    .unwrap()
5549                    .index
5550                    .lookup(&id)
5551                    .expect("just-written record must be indexed");
5552                // Tampering happens on-disk: commit the buffered frame so the
5553                // tamper site computed from `offset` actually lands in the file.
5554                store.sync_all().unwrap();
5555                let path = store.shards.get_shard(shard_id).unwrap().path.clone();
5556                drop(store);
5557
5558                let mut bytes = fs::read(&path).unwrap();
5559                let payload_byte = usize::try_from(offset)
5560                    .expect("u64 offset fits usize on any supported host")
5561                    .saturating_add(4)
5562                    .saturating_add(crate::packfile::FRAME_FIXED_LEN as usize);
5563                assert!(payload_byte < bytes.len(), "tamper site must be in-file");
5564                bytes[payload_byte] ^= 0xff;
5565                bytes[payload_byte + 1] ^= 0x0f;
5566                fs::write(path, bytes).unwrap();
5567            }
5568
5569            if expect_reopen_ok {
5570                let store = PackfileStorage::open_with_policies(dir, true, policy).unwrap();
5571                let got = store
5572                    .get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &distinct_id(0x52))
5573                    .unwrap()
5574                    .expect("Disabled reopen must serve the record");
5575                assert_eq!(
5576                    got.bytes.as_ref().len(),
5577                    b"tamper target".len(),
5578                    "payload frame must still decode"
5579                );
5580            } else {
5581                let reopened = PackfileStorage::open_with_policies(dir.clone(), true, policy);
5582                match reopened {
5583                    Ok(_) => panic!("reopen scan must reject a tampered pack"),
5584                    Err(err) => {
5585                        assert!(
5586                            matches!(err, std::io::Error { .. }),
5587                            "expected I/O error from scan_and_recover, got {err:?}"
5588                        );
5589                    }
5590                }
5591                // Remove the trusted checkpoint to exercise the read-only
5592                // full-scan fallback. A valid checkpoint deliberately avoids
5593                // scanning frames and verifies them lazily on lookup.
5594                fs::remove_file(PackfileStorage::index_checkpoint_path(&dir)).unwrap();
5595                let read_only = PackfileStorage::open_read_only_with_policies(dir, policy);
5596                assert!(
5597                    read_only.is_err(),
5598                    "read-only recovery must fail closed instead of omitting a corrupt shard"
5599                );
5600            }
5601        }
5602    }

5604    #[test]
5605    fn test_read_survives_append_after_mmap_established() {
5606        let dir = test_dir("stale_mmap_regression");
5607        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
5608
5609        let a = [0xAAu8; 16];
5610        let b = [0xBBu8; 16];
5611        let data_a = NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"aaaa"));
5612        let data_b = NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"bbbb"));
5613
5614        store.put(&TEST_COLLECTION, &a, &data_a).unwrap();
5615
5616        if let Some(gen) = store.generation(&TEST_COLLECTION) {
5617            gen.cache.clear();
5618        }
5619        let got_a = store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &a).unwrap();
5620        assert_eq!(got_a.unwrap().bytes, data_a.bytes);
5621
5622        store.put(&TEST_COLLECTION, &b, &data_b).unwrap();
5623
5624        if let Some(gen) = store.generation(&TEST_COLLECTION) {
5625            gen.cache.clear();
5626        }
5627        let got_b = store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &b).unwrap();
5628        assert_eq!(got_b.expect("B must be found").bytes, data_b.bytes);
5629    }

5754

5758            gen.cache.clear();

5762        reversed_ids.reverse();

5765        assert_eq!(results.len(), 10);

5768            assert_eq!(
5769                results[i].as_ref().expect("record must be found").bytes,
5770                expected.bytes,
5771                "result at position {i} must match the id requested at that position"
5772            );
5773        }

5776    #[test]
5777    fn test_walk_ancestors_stops_at_boundary() {
5778        let dir = test_dir("walk_basic");
5779        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
5780
5781        // A -> B -> C -> D -> E, linear chain, edges point to predecessors.
5782        let ids: Vec<NodeId> = (0..5u8).map(distinct_id).collect();
5783        for (i, id) in ids.iter().enumerate() {
5784            let byte = u8::try_from(i).unwrap();
5785            store
5786                .put(
5787                    &TEST_COLLECTION,
5788                    id,
5789                    &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from(vec![b'A' + byte])),
5790                )
5791                .unwrap();
5792        }
5793        let edges = std::collections::HashMap::from([
5794            (ids[1], vec![ids[0]]),
5795            (ids[2], vec![ids[1]]),
5796            (ids[3], vec![ids[2]]),
5797            (ids[4], vec![ids[3]]),
5798        ]);
5799        let extract = |hash: &[u8; 16], _data: &[u8]| edges.get(hash).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
5800
5801        // frontier=[D], stop_at=[B]: should yield C, D in ancestor-first
5802        // order — stopping at and excluding B, including the D frontier.
5803        let walked: Vec<(NodeId, NodeData)> = store
5804            .walk_ancestors(
5805                &TEST_COLLECTION,
5806                &[ids[3]],
5807                &[ids[1]],
5808                extract,
5809                WalkLimits::default(),
5810            )
5811            .unwrap()
5812            .collect::<Result<_, _>>()
5813            .unwrap();
5814        let bytes: Vec<u8> = walked.iter().map(|(_, d)| d.bytes[0]).collect();
5815        assert_eq!(
5816            bytes,
5817            vec![b'C', b'D'],
5818            "walk must be ancestor-first, excluding the stop_at boundary"
5819        );
5820
5821        // Empty stop_at: walks all the way back to the collection's true roots.
5822        let full: Vec<(NodeId, NodeData)> = store
5823            .walk_ancestors(
5824                &TEST_COLLECTION,
5825                &[ids[4]],
5826                &[],
5827                extract,
5828                WalkLimits::default(),
5829            )
5830            .unwrap()
5831            .collect::<Result<_, _>>()
5832            .unwrap();
5833        let full_bytes: Vec<u8> = full.iter().map(|(_, d)| d.bytes[0]).collect();
5834        assert_eq!(full_bytes, vec![b'A', b'B', b'C', b'D', b'E']);
5835    }
5836
5837    #[test]
5838    fn test_walk_ancestors_branching_dag() {
5839        let dir = test_dir("walk_branch");
5840        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
5841
5842        //   A
5843        //  / \
5844        // B   C
5845        //  \ /
5846        //   D
5847        let ids: Vec<NodeId> = (0..4u8).map(distinct_id).collect();
5848        for (i, id) in ids.iter().enumerate() {
5849            let byte = u8::try_from(i).unwrap();
5850            store
5851                .put(
5852                    &TEST_COLLECTION,
5853                    id,
5854                    &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from(vec![b'A' + byte])),
5855                )
5856                .unwrap();
5857        }
5858        let edges = std::collections::HashMap::from([
5859            (ids[1], vec![ids[0]]),
5860            (ids[2], vec![ids[0]]),
5861            (ids[3], vec![ids[1], ids[2]]),
5862        ]);
5863        let extract = |hash: &[u8; 16], _data: &[u8]| edges.get(hash).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
5864
5865        // frontier=[D], stop_at=[A]: should yield exactly {B, C, D} — A
5866        // excluded as the boundary, both branches merge back in correctly.
5867        let walked: Vec<(NodeId, NodeData)> = store
5868            .walk_ancestors(
5869                &TEST_COLLECTION,
5870                &[ids[3]],
5871                &[ids[0]],
5872                extract,
5873                WalkLimits::default(),
5874            )
5875            .unwrap()
5876            .collect::<Result<_, _>>()
5877            .unwrap();
5878        let mut bytes: Vec<u8> = walked.iter().map(|(_, d)| d.bytes[0]).collect();
5879        // D must come last (ancestor-first order); B/C order between them
5880        // is unconstrained since they're siblings.
5881        assert_eq!(*bytes.last().unwrap(), b'D');
5882        bytes.sort_unstable();
5883        assert_eq!(bytes, vec![b'B', b'C', b'D']);
5884    }
5885
5886    #[test]
5887    fn test_walk_ancestors_never_reaching_stop_at_walks_to_roots() {
5888        // A fork that never crosses the supplied stop_at boundary must not
5889        // be silently truncated there — it's an ancestor walk with stop
5890        // markers, not a from/to span, so it walks to the collection's true
5891        // roots instead.
5892        let dir = test_dir("walk_fork_misses_boundary");
5893        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
5894
5895        //   ROOT
5896        //   /  \
5897        // MAIN  FORK
5898        //  |
5899        // TIP
5900        let root = distinct_id(0);
5901        let main = distinct_id(1);
5902        let fork = distinct_id(2);
5903        let tip = distinct_id(3);
5904        for (id, byte) in [(root, b'R'), (main, b'M'), (fork, b'F'), (tip, b'T')] {
5905            store
5906                .put(
5907                    &TEST_COLLECTION,
5908                    &id,
5909                    &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from(vec![byte])),
5910                )
5911                .unwrap();
5912        }
5913        let edges = std::collections::HashMap::from([
5914            (main, vec![root]),
5915            (fork, vec![root]),
5916            (tip, vec![main]),
5917        ]);
5918        let extract = |hash: &[u8; 16], _data: &[u8]| edges.get(hash).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
5919
5920        // stop_at names `fork`, which TIP's history never crosses — the
5921        // walk from TIP must still reach ROOT rather than stopping short.
5922        let walked: Vec<(NodeId, NodeData)> = store
5923            .walk_ancestors(
5924                &TEST_COLLECTION,
5925                &[tip],
5926                &[fork],
5927                extract,
5928                WalkLimits::default(),
5929            )
5930            .unwrap()
5931            .collect::<Result<_, _>>()
5932            .unwrap();
5933        let mut bytes: Vec<u8> = walked.iter().map(|(_, d)| d.bytes[0]).collect();
5934        bytes.sort_unstable();
5935        assert_eq!(bytes, vec![b'M', b'R', b'T']);
5936    }

5938    #[test]
5939    fn test_walk_ancestors_max_nodes_bounds_a_runaway_walk() {
5940        let dir = test_dir("walk_max_nodes");
5941        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
5942
5943        // A long chain with a stop_at that never gets hit — max_nodes is
5944        // the only thing that keeps this walk from covering the chain.
5945        let ids: Vec<NodeId> = (0..20u8).map(distinct_id).collect();
5946        for (i, id) in ids.iter().enumerate() {
5947            let byte = u8::try_from(i % 26).unwrap();
5948            store
5949                .put(
5950                    &TEST_COLLECTION,
5951                    id,
5952                    &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from(vec![b'a' + byte])),
5953                )
5954                .unwrap();
5955        }
5956        let mut edges = std::collections::HashMap::new();
5957        for i in 1..ids.len() {
5958            edges.insert(ids[i], vec![ids[i - 1]]);
5959        }
5960        let extract = |hash: &[u8; 16], _data: &[u8]| edges.get(hash).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
5961
5962        let walked: Vec<(NodeId, NodeData)> = store
5963            .walk_ancestors(
5964                &TEST_COLLECTION,
5965                &[ids[19]],
5966                &[],
5967                extract,
5968                WalkLimits { max_nodes: Some(5) },
5969            )
5970            .unwrap()
5971            .collect::<Result<_, _>>()
5972            .unwrap();
5973        assert!(
5974            walked.len() <= 5,
5975            "max_nodes must bound the walk even though stop_at was never reached"
5976        );
5977    }
5978
5979    #[test]
5980    fn test_walk_ancestors_survives_concurrent_repack_gc() {
5981        // Regression test for the exact concurrency bug flagged in review:
5982        // an earlier draft resolved each node lazily, at drain time,
5983        // against whatever generation happened to be live *then* — so a
5984        // repack that GC'd a node between building the walk and draining
5985        // it would silently drop that node from the results. Resolving
5986        // eagerly against one frozen generation snapshot up front (what
5987        // walk_ancestors does now) must not exhibit that.
5988        let dir = test_dir("walk_survives_repack");
5989        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
5990
5991        let a = distinct_id(0);
5992        let b = distinct_id(1);
5993        store
5994            .put(
5995                &TEST_COLLECTION,
5996                &a,
5997                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"A")),
5998            )
5999            .unwrap();
6000        store
6001            .put(
6002                &TEST_COLLECTION,
6003                &b,
6004                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"B")),
6005            )
6006            .unwrap();
6007        let edges = std::collections::HashMap::from([(b, vec![a])]);
6008        let extract = |hash: &[u8; 16], _data: &[u8]| edges.get(hash).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
6009
6010        // Build the walk (resolves and captures A and B eagerly)...
6011        let walk = store
6012            .walk_ancestors(&TEST_COLLECTION, &[b], &[], extract, WalkLimits::default())
6013            .unwrap();
6014
6015        // ...then, before draining it, run a repack that only keeps B as
6016        // a live root — A becomes unreachable and gets GC'd out of the
6017        // collection's index/cache entirely.
6018        store.set_live_roots(&TEST_COLLECTION, vec![b]);
6019        let (kept, dropped) = store
6020            .repack_collection_reachable(&TEST_COLLECTION, |_hash, _data| Vec::new())
6021            .unwrap();
6022        assert_eq!((kept, dropped), (1, 1), "repack must have GC'd A");
6023
6024        // The already-built walk must still yield both A and B: it
6025        // captured its data before the repack ran, so it isn't affected
6026        // by the generation swap that just happened.
6027        let results: Vec<(NodeId, NodeData)> = walk.collect::<Result<_, _>>().unwrap();
6028        let mut bytes: Vec<u8> = results.iter().map(|(_, d)| d.bytes[0]).collect();
6029        bytes.sort_unstable();
6030        assert_eq!(
6031            bytes,
6032            vec![b'A', b'B'],
6033            "a walk built before a concurrent repack must not lose nodes that repack GC'd afterward"
6034        );
6035    }

6038    fn test_multiple_records_same_collection() {
6039        let dir = test_dir("multi");

6041

6043            let mut id = [0u8; 16];
6044            id[0] = i;

6046            store.put(&TEST_COLLECTION, &id, &data).unwrap();
6047        }
6048
6049        for i in 0..5u8 {
6050            let mut id = [0u8; 16];
6051            id[0] = i;
6052            let got = store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &id).unwrap().unwrap();
6053            assert_eq!(got.bytes, bytes::Bytes::from(format!("record {i}")));

6057    #[test]
6058    fn test_collection_isolation() {
6059        let dir = test_dir("isolation");
6060        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
6061
6062        let id_a = [0x42u8; 16];
6063        let id_b = [0x43u8; 16];
6064        let data_a = NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"collection A data"));
6065        let data_b = NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"collection B data"));
6066
6067        store.put(&TEST_COLLECTION, &id_a, &data_a).unwrap();
6068        store.put(&OTHER_COLLECTION, &id_b, &data_b).unwrap();
6069
6070        let got_a = store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &id_a).unwrap().unwrap();
6071        let got_b = store.get(&OTHER_COLLECTION, &id_b).unwrap().unwrap();
6072        assert_eq!(got_a.bytes, data_a.bytes);
6073        assert_eq!(got_b.bytes, data_b.bytes);
6074
6075        assert!(store.get(&OTHER_COLLECTION, &id_a).unwrap().is_none());
6076        assert!(store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &id_b).unwrap().is_none());
6077
6078        store.delete_collection(&TEST_COLLECTION).unwrap();
6079        assert!(store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &id_a).unwrap().is_none());
6080        let got_b = store.get(&OTHER_COLLECTION, &id_b).unwrap().unwrap();
6081        assert_eq!(got_b.bytes, data_b.bytes);
6082    }
6083
6084    #[test]
6085    fn test_concurrent_federation_swarm() {
6086        use std::sync::Mutex;
6087        use std::thread;
6088
6089        const NUM_WRITERS: usize = 8;
6090        const EVENTS_PER_WRITER: usize = 500;
6091        const NUM_READERS: usize = 8;
6092        const READS_PER_READER: usize = 2000;
6093
6094        let dir = test_dir("concurrent_federation");
6095        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
6096
6097        let collection = [0x77u8; 16];
6098        let written: Mutex<Vec<(NodeId, bytes::Bytes)>> = Mutex::new(Vec::new());
6099        let read_ok = std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize::new(0);
6100        let read_not_found = std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize::new(0);
6101
6102        thread::scope(|scope| {
6103            for w in 0..NUM_WRITERS {
6104                let store = &store;
6105                let written = &written;
6106                scope.spawn(move || {
6107                    for i in 0..EVENTS_PER_WRITER {
6108                        let mut id = [0u8; 16];
6109                        id[0] = u8::try_from(w).unwrap();
6110                        id[1..9].copy_from_slice(&(i as u64).to_le_bytes());
6111                        let bytes = bytes::Bytes::from(format!("writer {w} event {i}"));
6112                        let data = NodeData::new(bytes.clone());
6113                        store.put(&collection, &id, &data).unwrap();
6114                        written.lock().unwrap().push((id, bytes));
6115                    }
6116                });
6117            }
6118
6119            for _ in 0..NUM_READERS {
6120                let store = &store;
6121                let written = &written;
6122                let read_ok = &read_ok;
6123                let read_not_found = &read_not_found;
6124                scope.spawn(move || {
6125                    for i in 0..READS_PER_READER {
6126                        let snapshot_len = written.lock().unwrap().len();
6127                        if snapshot_len == 0 {
6128                            continue;
6129                        }
6130                        let idx = i % snapshot_len;
6131                        let (id, expected_bytes) = written.lock().unwrap()[idx].clone();
6132                        match store.get(&collection, &id).unwrap() {
6133                            Some(data) => {
6134                                assert_eq!(data.bytes, expected_bytes);
6135                                read_ok.fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
6136                            }
6137                            None => {
6138                                read_not_found.fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
6139                            }
6140                        }
6141                    }
6142                });
6143            }
6144        });
6145
6146        let written = written.into_inner().unwrap();
6147        assert_eq!(written.len(), NUM_WRITERS * EVENTS_PER_WRITER);
6148
6149        let mut lost = Vec::new();
6150        for (id, expected_bytes) in &written {
6151            match store.get(&collection, id).unwrap() {
6152                Some(data) => assert_eq!(data.bytes, *expected_bytes),
6153                None => lost.push(*id),
6154            }
6155        }
6156
6157        eprintln!(
6158            "concurrent federation swarm: {} writes, {} reads ok, {} reads not-found, {} lost",
6159            written.len(),
6160            read_ok.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed),
6161            read_not_found.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed),
6162            lost.len()
6163        );
6164
6165        assert!(
6166            lost.is_empty(),
6167            "{} of {} records lost: {lost:?}",
6168            lost.len(),
6169            written.len()
6170        );
6171    }
6172
6173    /// Regression for the epoch-handoff protocol in `persist_index_checkpoint`:
6174    /// full checkpoint rewrites now hold every collection's `put_mutex` only
6175    /// across the fingerprint/index snapshot and delta-epoch rotation, not
6176    /// across the (unlocked) serialize + fsync + rename. Concurrent `put`s
6177    /// landing in that unlocked window must all still be durable after the
6178    /// next sync and a reopen — none may be silently dropped by the epoch
6179    /// rotation, and none may be lost to a torn straddle between the pack
6180    /// fingerprint and the index snapshot.
6181    #[test]
6182    fn test_concurrent_put_survives_checkpoint_rewrites() {
6183        use std::sync::Mutex;
6184        use std::thread;
6185
6186        const NUM_WRITERS: usize = 6;
6187        const PUTS_PER_WRITER: usize = 300;
6188        // Force many full rewrites during the run: each rewrite thread
6189        // iteration invalidates the delta log (via a collection delete on an
6190        // otherwise-untouched collection) then rewrites, so persistence keeps
6191        // taking the epoch-rotation path instead of the cheap delta append.
6192        const REWRITES: usize = 40;
6193
6194        let dir = test_dir("concurrent_put_checkpoint_rewrite");
6195        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone()).unwrap();
6196
6197        // Seed one throwaway collection per rewrite so each rewrite iteration
6198        // has a fresh structural invalidation to force (deleting an
6199        // already-deleted collection is a no-op and wouldn't force a rewrite).
6200        for r in 0..REWRITES {
6201            let mut collection = [0u8; 16];
6202            collection[0] = 0xEE;
6203            collection[1..9].copy_from_slice(&(r as u64).to_le_bytes());
6204            let mut id = [0u8; 16];
6205            id[15] = 1;
6206            store
6207                .put(&collection, &id, &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from("seed")))
6208                .unwrap();
6209        }
6210        store.sync_all().unwrap();
6211
6212        let written: Mutex<Vec<([u8; 16], NodeId, bytes::Bytes)>> = Mutex::new(Vec::new());
6213
6214        thread::scope(|scope| {
6215            for w in 0..NUM_WRITERS {
6216                let store = &store;
6217                let written = &written;
6218                scope.spawn(move || {
6219                    let mut collection = [0u8; 16];
6220                    collection[0] = 0xAA;
6221                    collection[1] = u8::try_from(w).unwrap();
6222                    for i in 0..PUTS_PER_WRITER {
6223                        let mut id = [0u8; 16];
6224                        id[0] = u8::try_from(w).unwrap();
6225                        id[1..9].copy_from_slice(&(i as u64).to_le_bytes());
6226                        let bytes = bytes::Bytes::from(format!("writer {w} put {i}"));
6227                        let data = NodeData::new(bytes.clone());
6228                        store.put(&collection, &id, &data).unwrap();
6229                        written.lock().unwrap().push((collection, id, bytes));
6230                    }
6231                });
6232            }
6233
6234            scope.spawn(|| {
6235                for r in 0..REWRITES {
6236                    let mut throwaway = [0u8; 16];
6237                    throwaway[0] = 0xEE;
6238                    throwaway[1..9].copy_from_slice(&(r as u64).to_le_bytes());
6239                    store.delete_collection(&throwaway).unwrap();
6240                    store.persist_index_checkpoint_best_effort();
6241                }
6242            });
6243        });
6244
6245        store.sync_all().unwrap();
6246        drop(store);
6247
6248        let reopened = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
6249        let written = written.into_inner().unwrap();
6250        assert_eq!(written.len(), NUM_WRITERS * PUTS_PER_WRITER);
6251
6252        let mut lost = Vec::new();
6253        for (collection, id, expected_bytes) in &written {
6254            match reopened.get(collection, id).unwrap() {
6255                Some(data) => assert_eq!(data.bytes, *expected_bytes),
6256                None => lost.push(*id),
6257            }
6258        }
6259        assert!(
6260            lost.is_empty(),
6261            "{} of {} records lost to a concurrent checkpoint rewrite: {lost:?}",
6262            lost.len(),
6263            written.len()
6264        );
6265    }
6266
6267    /// Deterministic, fast regression guard for the epoch-handoff protocol's
6268    /// actual point: a concurrent `put()` must not block on the checkpoint
6269    /// rewrite's serialize/write/rename, no matter how long that takes.
6270    /// Rather than inferring "not blocked" from a wall-clock race against
6271    /// real disk I/O (slow, and only as reliable as the dataset is large
6272    /// enough to make a real rewrite slow relative to test-machine noise —
6273    /// see the git history on this function for the timing-based version
6274    /// this replaced), this injects an artificial, arbitrarily long delay
6275    /// into the unlocked window via a test-only hook and asserts a `put()`
6276    /// issued after the rewrite has entered that window returns in a small
6277    /// fraction of it. A proper wall-clock benchmark of this same property
6278    /// lives in `benches/storage.rs` (`cargo bench`), where it belongs.
6279    #[test]
6280    fn test_put_does_not_block_on_slow_checkpoint_rewrite() {
6281        use std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool;
6282        use std::thread;
6283        use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
6284
6285        const ARTIFICIAL_DELAY: Duration = Duration::from_millis(300);
6286
6287        let dir = test_dir("put_not_blocked_by_slow_rewrite");
6288        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
6289
6290        // Establish a first checkpoint so the forced rewrite below has
6291        // something real to serialize and rewrite.
6292        let cid = [0x33u8; 16];
6293        store
6294            .put(&cid, &[0u8; 16], &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from("seed")))
6295            .unwrap();
6296        store.sync_all().unwrap();
6297
6298        // Force the next rewrite via a structural invalidation.
6299        let throwaway = [0x44u8; 16];
6300        store
6301            .put(
6302                &throwaway,
6303                &[0u8; 16],
6304                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from("x")),
6305            )
6306            .unwrap();
6307        store.delete_collection(&throwaway).unwrap();
6308
6309        let entered_unlocked_window = std::sync::Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
6310        let put_elapsed = std::sync::Mutex::new(None::<Duration>);
6311
6312        thread::scope(|scope| {
6313            {
6314                let store = &store;
6315                let hook_flag = std::sync::Arc::clone(&entered_unlocked_window);
6316                scope.spawn(move || {
6317                    store
6318                        .test_persist_index_checkpoint_with_delay(ARTIFICIAL_DELAY, &hook_flag)
6319                        .unwrap();
6320                });
6321            }
6322            // Wait for the hook to confirm it has released every lock and is
6323            // now sleeping inside the artificial delay — a real signal from
6324            // the rewrite thread, not a fixed-sleep guess that depends on
6325            // test-machine timing. The put issued below then provably runs
6326            // concurrently with the unlocked rewrite window.
6327            while !entered_unlocked_window.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
6328                thread::yield_now();
6329            }
6330
6331            let started = Instant::now();
6332            store
6333                .put(
6334                    &cid,
6335                    &[1u8; 16],
6336                    &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from("concurrent")),
6337                )
6338                .unwrap();
6339            *put_elapsed.lock().unwrap() = Some(started.elapsed());
6340        });
6341
6342        let put_elapsed = put_elapsed.into_inner().unwrap().unwrap();
6343        assert!(
6344            put_elapsed < ARTIFICIAL_DELAY / 3,
6345            "a put() issued while a rewrite was sleeping in its unlocked window took \
6346             {put_elapsed:?} — it should return almost immediately, not wait anywhere near the \
6347             artificial {ARTIFICIAL_DELAY:?} delay; did the lock scope regress to holding \
6348             put_mutex across the serialize/write/rename again?"
6349        );
6350    }
6351
6352    /// Crash-boundary: a crash between the D0→D1 epoch rotation and C1's
6353    /// checkpoint rename. D1 exists only in the (now-lost) in-memory state;
6354    /// nothing on disk names its fingerprint. The pack bytes for whatever
6355    /// prompted the rotation are already physically flushed (rotation always
6356    /// flushes first), so the reopen's local fingerprint has already moved
6357    /// past D0's sealed tail — the trusted-log check fails and this must fall
6358    /// back to a full rescan (packfiles stay authoritative), never silently
6359    /// lose the data.
6360    #[test]
6361    fn test_crash_between_rotation_and_checkpoint_rename_falls_back_to_rescan() {
6362        let dir = test_dir("crash_between_rotation_and_rename");
6363        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone()).unwrap();
6364
6365        let cid = [0x11u8; 16];
6366        let mut written = Vec::new();
6367
6368        // Round 1: establish C0 via a full (first-ever) rewrite.
6369        for i in 0..50u64 {
6370            let mut id = [0u8; 16];
6371            id[1..9].copy_from_slice(&i.to_le_bytes());
6372            let bytes = bytes::Bytes::from(format!("round1 {i}"));
6373            store.put(&cid, &id, &NodeData::new(bytes.clone())).unwrap();
6374            written.push((id, bytes));
6375        }
6376        store.sync_all().unwrap();
6377
6378        // Round 2: a plain write's sync appends a delta batch continuing C0
6379        // (D0), never rewriting the checkpoint.
6380        for i in 50..100u64 {
6381            let mut id = [0u8; 16];
6382            id[1..9].copy_from_slice(&i.to_le_bytes());
6383            let bytes = bytes::Bytes::from(format!("round2 {i}"));
6384            store.put(&cid, &id, &NodeData::new(bytes.clone())).unwrap();
6385            written.push((id, bytes));
6386        }
6387        store.sync_all().unwrap();
6388
6389        // Round 3: more writes, then simulate a crash exactly between the
6390        // epoch rotation (which flushes these bytes and discards their
6391        // not-yet-appended delta frames) and the checkpoint that would have
6392        // validated the new epoch.
6393        for i in 100..150u64 {
6394            let mut id = [0u8; 16];
6395            id[1..9].copy_from_slice(&i.to_le_bytes());
6396            let bytes = bytes::Bytes::from(format!("round3 {i}"));
6397            store.put(&cid, &id, &NodeData::new(bytes.clone())).unwrap();
6398            written.push((id, bytes));
6399        }
6400        let _ = store.test_rotate_epoch_without_checkpoint();
6401        drop(store); // simulated crash: no checkpoint, no retirement.
6402
6403        let reopened = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
6404        let open = reopened.open_timings().expect("open must record timings");
6405        assert_eq!(
6406            open.path,
6407            OpenPath::FullScan,
6408            "D0's sealed tail can no longer match the post-rotation pack state, so the \
6409             stale-but-still-present log must be rejected wholesale, not partially replayed"
6410        );
6411        for (id, expected_bytes) in &written {
6412            let got = reopened
6413                .get(&cid, id)
6414                .unwrap()
6415                .expect("every write, including the un-appended round 3, survives via the packs");
6416            assert_eq!(got.bytes.as_ref(), expected_bytes.as_ref());
6417        }
6418    }
6419
6420    /// Crash-boundary: a crash between C1's checkpoint rename becoming
6421    /// durable and D0's retirement. Both C1 (+ the fresh, still-empty D1) and
6422    /// the now-stale D0 exist on disk at once; the reopen must select C1 and
6423    /// ignore D0 (base fingerprint no longer matches), and the orphaned D0
6424    /// file must be swept away by the sweep-on-open cleanup.
6425    #[test]
6426    fn test_crash_between_checkpoint_rename_and_retirement_uses_new_checkpoint() {
6427        let dir = test_dir("crash_between_rename_and_retire");
6428        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone()).unwrap();
6429
6430        let cid = [0x22u8; 16];
6431        let mut written = Vec::new();
6432
6433        // Stay well under `NEW_COLLECTION_INDEX_FLOOR`'s 75%-load grow
6434        // threshold (48 entries at the floor of 64): this test exercises
6435        // checkpoint/epoch continuity across a simulated crash, not the
6436        // index's grow() behavior, and a grow mid-round would invalidate
6437        // the delta log this test is asserting still exists on disk.
6438        for i in 0..10u64 {
6439            let mut id = [0u8; 16];
6440            id[1..9].copy_from_slice(&i.to_le_bytes());
6441            let bytes = bytes::Bytes::from(format!("round1 {i}"));
6442            store.put(&cid, &id, &NodeData::new(bytes.clone())).unwrap();
6443            written.push((id, bytes));
6444        }
6445        store.sync_all().unwrap(); // C0, D0 exists once round 2 appends below.
6446
6447        for i in 10..20u64 {
6448            let mut id = [0u8; 16];
6449            id[1..9].copy_from_slice(&i.to_le_bytes());
6450            let bytes = bytes::Bytes::from(format!("round2 {i}"));
6451            store.put(&cid, &id, &NodeData::new(bytes.clone())).unwrap();
6452            written.push((id, bytes));
6453        }
6454        store.sync_all().unwrap(); // D0 now has a committed batch continuing C0.
6455
6456        let old_d0_fingerprint = {
6457            let (_new_fingerprint, old_base_fingerprint) =
6458                store.test_write_checkpoint_without_retire().unwrap();
6459            old_base_fingerprint.expect("this session had a prior epoch (C0/D0) to retire")
6460        };
6461        let d0_path = PackfileStorage::delta_path(&dir, old_d0_fingerprint);
6462        assert!(
6463            d0_path.exists(),
6464            "D0 must still be on disk immediately after the simulated crash point"
6465        );
6466        drop(store); // simulated crash: retire_delta_epoch never ran.
6467
6468        let reopened = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone()).unwrap();
6469        let open = reopened.open_timings().expect("open must record timings");
6470        assert_eq!(
6471            open.path,
6472            OpenPath::Checkpoint,
6473            "C1 is durable and matches the current pack state exactly (no writes happened \
6474             after the snapshot), so the reopen must take the fast path"
6475        );
6476        assert!(
6477            !d0_path.exists(),
6478            "the orphaned, now-unreachable D0 must be swept away by the writable open's cleanup"
6479        );
6480        for (id, expected_bytes) in &written {
6481            let got = reopened
6482                .get(&cid, id)
6483                .unwrap()
6484                .expect("every write survives a reopen onto the new checkpoint");
6485            assert_eq!(got.bytes.as_ref(), expected_bytes.as_ref());
6486        }
6487    }
6488
6489    #[test]
6490    fn test_swizzle_callback() {
6491        static SWIZZLE_CALLS: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
6492        static CACHED_CHILDREN_FOUND: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
6493
6494        fn test_swizzle(
6495            data: &NodeData,
6496            children: &[NodeId],
6497            cached: &[Option<Arc<NodeData>>],
6498        ) -> NodeData {
6499            SWIZZLE_CALLS.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
6500            assert_eq!(children.len(), 2);
6501            for entry in cached {
6502                if entry.is_some() {
6503                    CACHED_CHILDREN_FOUND.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
6504                }
6505            }
6506            data.clone()
6507        }
6508
6509        let dir = test_dir("swizzle");
6510        let store = PackfileStorage::open_with_swizzle(dir, 100, test_swizzle).unwrap();
6511
6512        let parent_id = [0x10u8; 16];
6513        let child_a = [0x20u8; 16];
6514        let child_b = [0x30u8; 16];
6515
6516        let data_a = NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"child A"));
6517        let data_b = NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"child B"));
6518        let parent_data = NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"parent with children"));
6519
6520        store.put(&TEST_COLLECTION, &child_a, &data_a).unwrap();
6521        store.put(&TEST_COLLECTION, &child_b, &data_b).unwrap();
6522        store
6523            .put(&TEST_COLLECTION, &parent_id, &parent_data)
6524            .unwrap();
6525
6526        if let Some(gen) = store.generation(&TEST_COLLECTION) {
6527            gen.cache.clear();
6528        }
6529        store.put(&TEST_COLLECTION, &child_a, &data_a).unwrap();
6530        store.put(&TEST_COLLECTION, &child_b, &data_b).unwrap();
6531
6532        let extract = |_data: &NodeData| -> Vec<NodeId> { vec![child_a, child_b] };
6533
6534        let result = store
6535            .get_swizzled(&TEST_COLLECTION, &parent_id, extract)
6536            .unwrap();
6537        assert!(result.is_some());
6538        let node_ref = result.unwrap();
6539        assert!(node_ref.is_resolved());
6540        assert_eq!(node_ref.structural_hash(), &parent_id);
6541
6542        assert_eq!(SWIZZLE_CALLS.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 1);
6543        assert_eq!(CACHED_CHILDREN_FOUND.load(Ordering::Relaxed), 2);
6544    }

6560    #[test]
6561    fn test_probe_reopen_first_append_sync_path_no_growth() {
6562        // Discriminates reopen-materialization cost from real capacity-growth
6563        // cost: build to a load well under the 75% grow threshold (so the
6564        // post-reopen append batch cannot trigger `index_grow_count`), then
6565        // reopen and append. If sync still takes the checkpoint path here,
6566        // materialization alone invalidates the delta log and a re-base
6567        // frame (which only helps the growth case) buys nothing.
6568        let dir = test_dir("probe_reopen_sync_no_growth");
6569        let total = 2000u32; // 2032 / 4096 is well under the 75% grow threshold.
6570        build_reopen_probe_checkpoint(&dir, total);
6571        {
6572            let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone()).unwrap();
6573            let before = store.stats();
6574            reopen_probe_put_range(&store, total..(total + 32), 32, |_| {
6575                NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"y"))
6576            });
6577            let after_put = store.stats();
6578            let d = store.delta_state.lock();
6579            let room = store.generation(&REOPEN_PROBE_COLLECTION).unwrap();
6580            eprintln!(
6581                "AFTER REOPEN PUT: cap={} len={} load={:.3} clones+={} grows+={} invalids+={} | pending={} invalid={}",
6582                room.index.capacity(),
6583                room.index.len(),
6584                reopen_probe_load_factor(&room),
6585                after_put.put_many_clone_path_calls - before.put_many_clone_path_calls,
6586                after_put.index_grow_count - before.index_grow_count,
6587                after_put.delta_invalidations - before.delta_invalidations,
6588                d.pending.len(),
6589                d.invalid,
6590            );
6591            assert_eq!(
6592                after_put.index_grow_count - before.index_grow_count,
6593                0,
6594                "test setup invariant violated: this batch must not trigger real growth"
6595            );
6596            assert_eq!(
6597                after_put.delta_invalidations - before.delta_invalidations,
6598                0
6599            );
6600            assert!(!d.invalid);
6601            drop(d);
6602            store.sync().unwrap();
6603            let st = store.stats();
6604            let ts = store.sync_timings().unwrap();
6605            eprintln!(
6606                "REOPEN SYNC TIMINGS (no growth): checkpoint={:?} delta={:?} total={:?}",
6607                ts.checkpoint, ts.delta_log, ts.total
6608            );
6609            eprintln!(
6610                "REOPEN SYNC STATS (no growth): ckpt_writes+={} delta_appends+={}",
6611                st.checkpoint_writes - before.checkpoint_writes,
6612                st.delta_appends - before.delta_appends,
6613            );
6614            assert_eq!(st.checkpoint_writes - before.checkpoint_writes, 0);
6615            assert_eq!(st.delta_appends - before.delta_appends, 1);
6616            assert_eq!(ts.checkpoint, std::time::Duration::ZERO);
6617            assert!(ts.delta_log > std::time::Duration::ZERO);
6618        }
6619    }
6620
6621    const REOPEN_PROBE_COLLECTION: [u8; 16] = [0x11; 16];
6622
6623    fn reopen_probe_id(i: u32) -> NodeId {
6624        let mut id = [0u8; 16];
6625        id[0..4].copy_from_slice(&i.to_le_bytes());
6626        id[8..12].copy_from_slice(&(i ^ 0x9E37_79B9).to_le_bytes());
6627        id
6628    }
6629
6630    fn reopen_probe_payload(i: u32) -> NodeData {
6631        let mut payload = vec![0u8; 1024];
6632        let seed = u64::from(i).wrapping_mul(0x9E37_79B9_7F4A_7C15) ^ 0xDEAD_BEEF;
6633        payload[..2].copy_from_slice(&seed.to_le_bytes()[..2]);
6634        NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from(payload))
6635    }
6636
6637    fn reopen_probe_put_range(
6638        store: &PackfileStorage,
6639        range: std::ops::Range<u32>,
6640        batch_size: usize,
6641        payload: impl Fn(u32) -> NodeData,
6642    ) {
6643        let mut batch = Vec::with_capacity(batch_size);
6644        for i in range {
6645            batch.push((reopen_probe_id(i), payload(i)));
6646            if batch.len() == batch_size {
6647                store.put_many(&REOPEN_PROBE_COLLECTION, &batch).unwrap();
6648                batch.clear();
6649            }
6650        }
6651        if !batch.is_empty() {
6652            store.put_many(&REOPEN_PROBE_COLLECTION, &batch).unwrap();
6653        }
6654    }
6655
6656    fn reopen_probe_load_factor(room: &RoomGeneration) -> f64 {
6657        f64::from(u32::try_from(room.index.len()).expect("probe index length fits in u32"))
6658            / f64::from(room.index.capacity())
6659    }
6660
6661    fn build_reopen_probe_checkpoint(dir: &std::path::Path, total: u32) {
6662        let store = PackfileStorage::open_with_cache_and_policies(
6663            dir.to_path_buf(),
6664            0,
6665            true,
6666            packfile::ChecksumPolicy::Full,
6667        )
6668        .unwrap()
6669        .with_append_policy(shard::AppendPolicy::buffered());
6670        reopen_probe_put_range(&store, 0..total, 256, reopen_probe_payload);
6671        store.sync_all().unwrap();
6672        let stats = store.stats();
6673        let room = store.generation(&REOPEN_PROBE_COLLECTION).unwrap();
6674        eprintln!(
6675            "BUILD done: cap={} len={} load={:.3} gen={} | clones={} grows={} ckpt_writes={} delta_appends={}",
6676            room.index.capacity(), room.index.len(), reopen_probe_load_factor(&room), room.generation,
6677            stats.put_many_clone_path_calls, stats.index_grow_count, stats.checkpoint_writes,
6678            stats.delta_appends,
6679        );
6680    }

6682    #[test]
6683    fn test_probe_reopen_first_append_sync_path() {
6684        let dir = test_dir("probe_reopen_sync");
6685        let total = 3051u32;
6686        build_reopen_probe_checkpoint(&dir, total);
6687        {
6688            let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone()).unwrap();
6689            let d = store.delta_state.lock();
6690            eprintln!(
6691                "REOPEN delta_state: base_fingerprint={:?} invalid={} pending={} log_bytes={}",
6692                d.base_fingerprint,
6693                d.invalid,
6694                d.pending.len(),
6695                d.log_bytes
6696            );
6697            let room = store.generation(&REOPEN_PROBE_COLLECTION).unwrap();
6698            eprintln!(
6699                "REOPEN room: cap={} len={} load={:.3} gen={} base_gen={:?}",
6700                room.index.capacity(),
6701                room.index.len(),
6702                reopen_probe_load_factor(&room),
6703                room.generation,
6704                d.base_generations.get(&REOPEN_PROBE_COLLECTION),
6705            );
6706            drop(d);
6707            drop(room);
6708
6709            let before = store.stats();
6710            reopen_probe_put_range(&store, total..(total + 32), 32, |_| {
6711                NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"y"))
6712            });
6713            let after_put = store.stats();
6714            let d = store.delta_state.lock();
6715            eprintln!(
6716                "AFTER REOPEN PUT: clones+={} grows+={} invalids+={} | pending={} invalid={}",
6717                after_put.put_many_clone_path_calls - before.put_many_clone_path_calls,
6718                after_put.index_grow_count - before.index_grow_count,
6719                after_put.delta_invalidations - before.delta_invalidations,
6720                d.pending.len(),
6721                d.invalid,
6722            );
6723            assert_eq!(
6724                after_put.put_many_clone_path_calls - before.put_many_clone_path_calls,
6725                1
6726            );
6727            assert_eq!(after_put.index_grow_count - before.index_grow_count, 1);
6728            assert_eq!(
6729                after_put.delta_invalidations - before.delta_invalidations,
6730                1
6731            );
6732            assert!(d.invalid);
6733            drop(d);
6734            store.sync().unwrap();
6735            let st = store.stats();
6736            let ts = store.sync_timings().unwrap();
6737            eprintln!(
6738                "REOPEN SYNC TIMINGS: flush={:?} fsync={:?} checkpoint={:?} delta={:?} total={:?}",
6739                ts.pack_flush, ts.pack_fsync, ts.checkpoint, ts.delta_log, ts.total
6740            );
6741            eprintln!(
6742                "REOPEN SYNC STATS: ckpt_writes+={} delta_appends+={}",
6743                st.checkpoint_writes - before.checkpoint_writes,
6744                st.delta_appends - before.delta_appends,
6745            );
6746            assert_eq!(st.checkpoint_writes - before.checkpoint_writes, 1);
6747            assert_eq!(st.delta_appends - before.delta_appends, 0);
6748            assert!(ts.checkpoint > std::time::Duration::ZERO);
6749            assert_eq!(ts.delta_log, std::time::Duration::ZERO);
6750
6751            reopen_probe_put_range(&store, (total + 32)..(total + 288), 256, |_| {
6752                NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"z"))
6753            });
6754            store.sync().unwrap();
6755            let ts = store.sync_timings().unwrap();
6756            eprintln!(
6757                "SECOND SYNC TIMINGS: checkpoint={:?} delta={:?} total={:?}",
6758                ts.checkpoint, ts.delta_log, ts.total
6759            );
6760            assert_eq!(ts.checkpoint, std::time::Duration::ZERO);
6761            assert!(ts.delta_log > std::time::Duration::ZERO);
6762        }
6763    }

6799    #[test]
6800    fn test_put_many_publishes_successive_batches() {
6801        // Regression coverage for successful copy-on-write batch publication:
6802        // every record remains readable across several batches that need no
6803        // index growth.
6804        let dir = test_dir("put_many_successive_batches");
6805        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
6806
6807        for batch in 0..8u32 {
6808            let entries: Vec<_> = (0..20u32)
6809                .map(|i| {
6810                    let mut id = [0u8; 16];
6811                    id[0..4].copy_from_slice(&batch.to_le_bytes());
6812                    id[4..8].copy_from_slice(&i.to_le_bytes());
6813                    (
6814                        id,
6815                        NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from(format!("v{batch}-{i}"))),
6816                    )
6817                })
6818                .collect();
6819            store.put_many(&TEST_COLLECTION, &entries).unwrap();
6820        }
6821
6822        for batch in 0..8u32 {
6823            for i in 0..20u32 {
6824                let mut id = [0u8; 16];
6825                id[0..4].copy_from_slice(&batch.to_le_bytes());
6826                id[4..8].copy_from_slice(&i.to_le_bytes());
6827                let got = store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &id).unwrap().unwrap();
6828                assert_eq!(got.bytes, bytes::Bytes::from(format!("v{batch}-{i}")));
6829            }
6830        }
6831    }
