6833    #[test]
6834    fn test_runtime_stats_counters_round_trip() {
6835        let dir = test_dir("runtime_stats_counters");
6836        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
6837
6838        // Fresh store: every counter starts at zero (open_count counts the
6839        // one store assembled, not work).
6840        let snapshot = store.stats();
6841        assert_eq!(snapshot.open_count, 1);
6842        assert_eq!(snapshot.put_calls, 0);
6843        assert_eq!(snapshot.put_many_calls, 0);
6844        assert_eq!(snapshot.sync_calls, 0);
6845        assert_eq!(snapshot.get_calls, 0);
6846
6847        // Read counters are opt-in: with stats disabled a get is invisible.
6848        store
6849            .put(
6850                &TEST_COLLECTION,
6851                &[0u8; 16],
6852                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from("solo")),
6853            )
6854            .unwrap();
6855        assert_eq!(store.stats().put_calls, 1);
6856        assert!(store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &[0u8; 16]).unwrap().is_some());
6857        assert_eq!(store.stats().get_calls, 0);
6858        assert_eq!(store.stats().get_misses, 0);
6859
6860        // Enable read tracking, then a hit and a miss both count.
6861        store.set_stats_enabled(true);
6862        assert!(store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &[0u8; 16]).unwrap().is_some());
6863        assert!(store
6864            .get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &[0xFFu8; 16])
6865            .unwrap()
6866            .is_none());
6867        let snapshot = store.stats();
6868        assert_eq!(snapshot.get_calls, 2);
6869        assert_eq!(snapshot.get_misses, 1);
6870
6871        // Batched writes use copy-on-write so a failed later append cannot
6872        // expose an earlier record from the same batch. Every call is thus
6873        // charged to the clone path.
6874        let batches: Vec<Vec<(NodeId, NodeData)>> = (0..8u32)
6875            .map(|batch| {
6876                (0..8u32)
6877                    .map(|i| {
6878                        let mut id = [2u8; 16];
6879                        id[0..4].copy_from_slice(&batch.to_le_bytes());
6880                        id[4..8].copy_from_slice(&i.to_le_bytes());
6881                        (
6882                            id,
6883                            NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from(format!("b{batch}-{i}"))),
6884                        )
6885                    })
6886                    .collect()
6887            })
6888            .collect();
6889        for entries in &batches {
6890            store.put_many(&SECOND_COLLECTION, entries).unwrap();
6891        }
6892        let snapshot = store.stats();
6893        assert_eq!(snapshot.put_many_calls, 8);
6894        assert_eq!(snapshot.put_many_records, 64);
6895        assert!(snapshot.put_many_bytes > 0);
6896        assert_eq!(snapshot.put_many_clone_path_calls, 8);
6897        assert_eq!(snapshot.put_many_fast_path_calls, 0);
6898        // 64 distinct records into a floor-sized 64-slot index must cross the
6899        // grow threshold at least once.
6900        assert!(snapshot.index_grow_count >= 1);
6901        assert!(snapshot.index_clone_time > std::time::Duration::ZERO);
6902
6903        // Every put_many byte is the sum of entry payloads.
6904        let expected_bytes: u64 = batches
6905            .iter()
6906            .flatten()
6907            .map(|(_, data)| data.bytes.len() as u64)
6908            .sum();
6909        assert_eq!(snapshot.put_many_bytes, expected_bytes);
6910
6911        // reset_stats zeroes the runtime counters but not open_count.
6912        store.reset_stats();
6913        let snapshot = store.stats();
6914        assert_eq!(snapshot.open_count, 1);
6915        assert_eq!(snapshot.put_many_calls, 0);
6916        assert_eq!(snapshot.get_calls, 0);
6917        assert_eq!(snapshot.get_misses, 0);
6918
6919        // Sync accounting: the first (structurally invalidated) sync rewrites
6920        // the checkpoint; a later clean batch sync appends the delta log.
6921        store.sync().unwrap();
6922        assert_eq!(store.stats().sync_calls, 1);
6923        assert_eq!(store.stats().checkpoint_writes, 1);
6924        assert_eq!(store.stats().delta_appends, 0);
6925        store.put_many(&SECOND_COLLECTION, &batches[0]).unwrap();
6926        store.sync().unwrap();
6927        let snapshot = store.stats();
6928        assert_eq!(snapshot.sync_calls, 2);
6929        assert_eq!(snapshot.delta_appends, 1);
6930    }
6931
6932    #[test]
6933    fn test_put_many_growth_mid_batch_keeps_records_inserted_before_the_grow() {
6934        // Exercises the fast path's trickiest transition: some records in
6935        // the batch insert via the live, shared index (no clone), then a
6936        // later record in the *same* batch forces a grow — which must
6937        // materialize an owned index that still contains everything the
6938        // live path already applied, not just what comes after the grow.
6939        let dir = test_dir("put_many_growth_mid_batch");
6940        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
6941
6942        // Get the collection's live index past its NEW_COLLECTION_INDEX_FLOOR
6943        // starting capacity via ordinary put_many calls (fast path), then
6944        // issue one big batch that must cross the 75%-load grow threshold
6945        // partway through.
6946        let seed: Vec<_> = (0..40u32)
6947            .map(|i| {
6948                let mut id = [1u8; 16];
6949                id[4..8].copy_from_slice(&i.to_le_bytes());
6950                (id, NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from(format!("seed{i}"))))
6951            })
6952            .collect();
6953        store.put_many(&TEST_COLLECTION, &seed).unwrap();
6954
6955        let growth_batch: Vec<_> = (0..200u32)
6956            .map(|i| {
6957                let mut id = [2u8; 16];
6958                id[4..8].copy_from_slice(&i.to_le_bytes());
6959                (id, NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from(format!("grow{i}"))))
6960            })
6961            .collect();
6962        store.put_many(&TEST_COLLECTION, &growth_batch).unwrap();
6963
6964        for (id, data) in seed.iter().chain(&growth_batch) {
6965            let got = store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, id).unwrap().unwrap();
6966            assert_eq!(
6967                &got.bytes, &data.bytes,
6968                "record must survive the mid-batch grow"
6969            );
6970        }
6971    }
6972
6973    #[test]
6974    fn test_index_offset_gate_rejects_unrepresentable_offsets() {
6975        let hash = [0x5A; 16];
6976        assert!(check_index_offset(0, &hash, PACK_INDEX_OFFSET_LIMIT).is_ok());
6977        assert!(check_index_offset(0, &hash, PACK_INDEX_OFFSET_LIMIT + 1).is_err());
6978        assert!(check_index_offset(0, &hash, u64::MAX).is_err());
6979    }

6981    #[test]
6982    fn test_refresh_collection_multi_worker_visibility() {
6983        let dir = test_dir("refresh_collection_multi_worker");
6984        let writer = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone()).unwrap();
6985
6986        // Open a read-only instance BEFORE the writer writes the data.
6987        let reader = PackfileStorage::open_read_only(dir.clone()).unwrap();
6988
6989        let id = [0xAA; 16];
6990        writer
6991            .put(
6992                &TEST_COLLECTION,
6993                &id,
6994                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"multi_worker_test")),
6995            )
6996            .unwrap();
6997        writer.sync().unwrap();
6998
6999        // Reader's in-memory index should be completely unaware of the new write.
7000        assert!(reader.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &id).unwrap().is_none());
7001
7002        // Trigger the external refresh, simulating a cache invalidation signal.
7003        reader.refresh_collection(&TEST_COLLECTION).unwrap();
7004
7005        // Reader should now have correctly loaded the delta and rebuilt its index.
7006        let got = reader.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &id).unwrap().unwrap();
7007        assert_eq!(got.bytes, bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"multi_worker_test"));
7008        assert_eq!(
7009            reader
7010                .collection_index_info(&TEST_COLLECTION)
7011                .expect("refreshed collection exists")
7012                .2,
7013            u32::try_from(NEW_COLLECTION_INDEX_FLOOR).unwrap(),
7014            "a refresh rebuild keeps the same minimum capacity as a new collection"
7015        );
7016    }

7018    #[test]
7019    fn test_scan_existing_skips_malformed_filenames() {
7020        let dir = test_dir("scan_existing_junk");
7021        std::fs::write(dir.join("nounderscore.pack"), b"").unwrap();
7022        std::fs::write(dir.join("aabb_00.pack"), b"").unwrap();
7023        std::fs::write(dir.join("zzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzz_00.pack"), b"").unwrap();
7024        std::fs::write(dir.join("00000000000000000000000000000000_gg.pack"), b"").unwrap();
7025        {
7026            use std::ffi::OsStr;
7027            use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt;
7028            let path = dir.join(OsStr::from_bytes(b"\xff\xfe.pack"));
7029            std::fs::write(path, b"").unwrap();
7030        }
7031
7032        let valid_path = dir.join("pack_0000000000000000.pack");
7033        let mut buf = Vec::new();
7034        packfile::write_header(&mut buf, 0).unwrap();
7035        packfile::write_record(
7036            &mut buf,
7037            &packfile::Record {
7038                collection_id: [0x01; 16],
7039                hash: [0xAA; 16],
7040                data: bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"hello"),
7041            },
7042        )
7043        .unwrap();
7044        std::fs::write(&valid_path, &buf).unwrap();
7045
7046        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
7047        let gen = store.generation(&[0x01u8; 16]).unwrap();
7048        assert_eq!(gen.index.len(), 1);
7049    }

7051    #[test]
7052    fn test_delete_collection_preserves_other_collection_cache() {
7053        let dir = test_dir("delete_collection_cache");
7054        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone()).unwrap();
7055
7056        let collection_a = TEST_COLLECTION;
7057        let collection_b = OTHER_COLLECTION;
7058        let id_a = [0x10u8; 16];
7059        let id_b = [0x20u8; 16];
7060        let data_a = NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"collection A data"));
7061        let data_b = NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"collection B data"));
7062
7063        store.put(&collection_a, &id_a, &data_a).unwrap();
7064        store.put(&collection_b, &id_b, &data_b).unwrap();
7065
7066        assert!(store.get(&collection_a, &id_a).unwrap().is_some());
7067        assert!(store.get(&collection_b, &id_b).unwrap().is_some());
7068
7069        let gen_b_before = store.generation(&collection_b).unwrap();
7070        let hits_b_before = gen_b_before.cache.hits();
7071
7072        store.delete_collection(&collection_a).unwrap();
7073
7074        assert!(store.get(&collection_a, &id_a).unwrap().is_none());
7075        assert!(store.generation(&collection_a).is_none());
7076        assert!(store.generation(&collection_b).is_some());
7077        assert!(store.get(&collection_b, &id_b).unwrap().is_some());
7078
7079        let gen_b_after = store.generation(&collection_b).unwrap();
7080        assert!(gen_b_after.cache.hits() > hits_b_before);
7081    }

7083    #[test]
7084    fn test_repack_collection_reachable_no_roots_preserves_diamond_dag_in_topo_order() {
7085        let dir = test_dir("repack_topo");
7086        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
7087
7088        let mut id_a = [0u8; 16];
7089        id_a[0] = 1;
7090        let mut id_b = [0u8; 16];
7091        id_b[0] = 2;
7092        let mut id_c = [0u8; 16];
7093        id_c[0] = 3;
7094        let mut id_d = [0u8; 16];
7095        id_d[0] = 4;
7096
7097        store
7098            .put(
7099                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7100                &id_a,
7101                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"A")),
7102            )
7103            .unwrap();
7104        store
7105            .put(
7106                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7107                &id_b,
7108                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"B")),
7109            )
7110            .unwrap();
7111        store
7112            .put(
7113                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7114                &id_c,
7115                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"C")),
7116            )
7117            .unwrap();
7118        store
7119            .put(
7120                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7121                &id_d,
7122                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"D")),
7123            )
7124            .unwrap();
7125
7126        let edges = std::collections::HashMap::from([
7127            (id_b, vec![id_a]),
7128            (id_c, vec![id_a]),
7129            (id_d, vec![id_b, id_c]),
7130        ]);
7131
7132        // No live roots configured for TEST_COLLECTION: preserves everything,
7133        // still deduplicated and topologically ordered.
7134        let result = store.repack_collection_reachable(&TEST_COLLECTION, |hash, _data| {
7135            edges.get(hash).cloned().unwrap_or_default()
7136        });
7137        let (kept, dropped) = result.unwrap();
7138        assert_eq!(kept, 4);
7139        assert_eq!(dropped, 0);
7140
7141        for (id, expected) in [
7142            (id_a, b"A".as_slice()),
7143            (id_b, b"B".as_slice()),
7144            (id_c, b"C".as_slice()),
7145            (id_d, b"D".as_slice()),
7146        ] {
7147            let got = store
7148                .get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &id)
7149                .unwrap()
7150                .expect("record missing after topo repack");
7151            assert_eq!(got.bytes.as_ref(), expected);
7152        }
7153    }

7155    #[test]
7156    fn test_repack_collection_reachable_drops_unreachable_records() {
7157        let dir = test_dir("repack_reachable_gc");
7158        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
7159
7160        // Live chain: root -> p1 -> p0 (p0 has no further dependencies).
7161        let root = distinct_id(1);
7162        let p1 = distinct_id(2);
7163        let p0 = distinct_id(3);
7164
7165        // Garbage island, unreachable from root: garbage -> garbage_dep.
7166        let garbage = distinct_id(4);
7167        let garbage_dep = distinct_id(5);
7168
7169        for (id, bytes) in [
7170            (root, b"root".as_slice()),
7171            (p1, b"p1".as_slice()),
7172            (p0, b"p0".as_slice()),
7173            (garbage, b"garbage".as_slice()),
7174            (garbage_dep, b"garbage_dep".as_slice()),
7175        ] {
7176            store
7177                .put(
7178                    &TEST_COLLECTION,
7179                    &id,
7180                    &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(bytes)),
7181                )
7182                .unwrap();
7183        }
7184
7185        let edges = std::collections::HashMap::from([
7186            (root, vec![p1]),
7187            (p1, vec![p0]),
7188            (garbage, vec![garbage_dep]),
7189        ]);
7190
7191        store.set_live_roots(&TEST_COLLECTION, vec![root]);
7192
7193        let (kept, dropped) = store
7194            .repack_collection_reachable(&TEST_COLLECTION, |hash, _data| {
7195                edges.get(hash).cloned().unwrap_or_default()
7196            })
7197            .unwrap();
7198
7199        assert_eq!(kept, 3, "root, p1, p0 should survive");
7200        assert_eq!(dropped, 2, "garbage and garbage_dep should be collected");
7201
7202        for id in [root, p1, p0] {
7203            assert!(
7204                store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &id).unwrap().is_some(),
7205                "live record missing after reachable repack"
7206            );
7207        }
7208        for id in [garbage, garbage_dep] {
7209            assert!(
7210                store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &id).unwrap().is_none(),
7211                "garbage record survived reachable repack"
7212            );
7213        }
7214
7215        let stats = store.repack_stats();
7216        assert_eq!(stats.repack_count, 1);
7217        assert_eq!(stats.kept_total, 3);
7218        assert_eq!(stats.dropped_total, 2);
7219        assert_eq!(store.repack_count_for_collection(&TEST_COLLECTION), 1);
7220
7221        // A second repack uses the incremental path: it clones the
7222        // previous live_map (3 entries from the first repack's output)
7223        // and scans only newly-appended bytes (none here).  The garbage
7224        // entries in the old shard are never re-scanned — the live_map
7225        // already excludes them, so dropped is 0.  This is the O(n)
7226        // behavior: each repack does bounded work proportional to new
7227        // bytes, not proportional to total shard size.
7228        let (kept2, dropped2) = store
7229            .repack_collection_reachable(&TEST_COLLECTION, |hash, _data| {
7230                edges.get(hash).cloned().unwrap_or_default()
7231            })
7232            .unwrap();
7233        assert_eq!((kept2, dropped2), (3, 0));
7234
7235        let stats2 = store.repack_stats();
7236        assert_eq!(stats2.repack_count, 2);
7237        assert_eq!(stats2.kept_total, stats.kept_total + kept2 as u64);
7238        assert_eq!(stats2.dropped_total, stats.dropped_total + dropped2 as u64);
7239        assert_eq!(store.repack_count_for_collection(&TEST_COLLECTION), 2);
7240    }
7241
7242    /// Verify the root-removal fallback: if a live root is removed between
7243    /// repacks, nodes exclusively reachable through it must not stay in the
7244    /// cached live set indefinitely. Without the fallback, the incremental
7245    /// adjacency cache only grows and those nodes would never be collected.
7246    #[test]
7247    fn test_repack_incremental_root_removal_forces_full_sweep() {
7248        let dir = test_dir("repack_root_removal_fallback");
7249        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
7250
7251        // Graph:
7252        //   root_a -> shared -> base
7253        //   root_b -> shared -> base
7254        //   orphan_a -> orphan_dep    (reachable only via root_a)
7255        //
7256        // First repack: both root_a and root_b are live. Everything survives.
7257        // Second repack: only root_b is live. orphan_a and orphan_dep must be
7258        // collected. Without the root-removal fallback they would remain in the
7259        // incremental live set permanently.
7260        let root_a = distinct_id(1);
7261        let root_b = distinct_id(2);
7262        let shared = distinct_id(3);
7263        let base = distinct_id(4);
7264        let orphan_a = distinct_id(5);
7265        let orphan_dep = distinct_id(6);
7266
7267        for (id, bytes) in [
7268            (root_a, b"root_a".as_slice()),
7269            (root_b, b"root_b".as_slice()),
7270            (shared, b"shared".as_slice()),
7271            (base, b"base".as_slice()),
7272            (orphan_a, b"orphan_a".as_slice()),
7273            (orphan_dep, b"orphan_dep".as_slice()),
7274        ] {
7275            store
7276                .put(
7277                    &TEST_COLLECTION,
7278                    &id,
7279                    &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(bytes)),
7280                )
7281                .unwrap();
7282        }
7283
7284        let edges = std::collections::HashMap::from([
7285            (root_a, vec![shared, orphan_a]),
7286            (root_b, vec![shared]),
7287            (shared, vec![base]),
7288            (orphan_a, vec![orphan_dep]),
7289        ]);
7290        let extract = |hash: &[u8; 16], _data: &[u8]| -> Vec<[u8; 16]> {
7291            edges.get(hash).cloned().unwrap_or_default()
7292        };
7293
7294        // First repack: both roots live — everything reachable from either root
7295        // survives and the incremental adjacency cache is populated.
7296        store.set_live_roots(&TEST_COLLECTION, vec![root_a, root_b]);
7297        let (kept1, dropped1) = store
7298            .repack_collection_reachable(&TEST_COLLECTION, extract)
7299            .unwrap();
7300        assert_eq!(kept1, 6, "all nodes should survive with both roots live");
7301        assert_eq!(dropped1, 0);
7302
7303        // Now remove root_a — switch to root_b only.
7304        // orphan_a and orphan_dep are now exclusively reachable through root_a,
7305        // which was removed. The root-removal fallback must trigger a full BFS
7306        // sweep so they are actually collected, rather than remaining in the
7307        // stale incremental live set forever.
7308        store.set_live_roots(&TEST_COLLECTION, vec![root_b]);
7309        let (kept2, dropped2) = store
7310            .repack_collection_reachable(&TEST_COLLECTION, extract)
7311            .unwrap();
7312        assert_eq!(
7313            kept2, 3,
7314            "root_b, shared, base should survive; root_a, orphan_a, orphan_dep must be GC'd"
7315        );
7316        assert_eq!(
7317            dropped2, 3,
7318            "root_a, orphan_a, and orphan_dep must be collected once root_a is removed from live roots"
7319        );
7320
7321        assert!(
7322            store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &orphan_a).unwrap().is_none(),
7323            "orphan_a must be gone after root_a was removed"
7324        );
7325        assert!(
7326            store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &orphan_dep).unwrap().is_none(),
7327            "orphan_dep must be gone after root_a was removed"
7328        );
7329        assert!(
7330            store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &root_a).unwrap().is_none(),
7331            "root_a itself has no incoming edges so it is unreachable from root_b"
7332        );
7333        assert!(
7334            store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &root_b).unwrap().is_some(),
7335            "root_b must still be present"
7336        );
7337        assert!(
7338            store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &shared).unwrap().is_some(),
7339            "shared must still be present"
7340        );
7341        assert!(
7342            store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &base).unwrap().is_some(),
7343            "base must still be present"
7344        );
7345    }
7346
7347    /// Repack drops unreachable *records* from the index (`dropped` count),
7348    /// but today nothing ever retires the *shard file* they lived in: no
7349    /// code path sets `Shard::is_current` to `false` or clears the pool's
7350    /// slot for it (see `Shard::drop`'s doc and
7351    /// `test_drop_deletes_retired_shard_only_after_last_reference` in
7352    /// shard.rs, which has to simulate retirement manually because nothing
7353    /// production triggers it). `rotate()`'s error message says "repack to
7354    /// reclaim", but repack currently reclaims nothing at the shard level.
7355    ///
7356    /// This fills a small fixed number of pool slots (4), repacks away
7357    /// everything in the non-active ones (100% garbage, nothing live left),
7358    /// and then expects one more rotation to succeed by reusing a
7359    /// now-empty slot.
7360    #[test]
7361    fn test_repack_reclaims_shard_slots_for_rotation() {
7362        // Use a fixed small count rather than MAX_SHARDS — with 4096 slots
7363        // and 256MB each that would be a 1TB test.
7364        const TEST_SHARDS: usize = 4;
7365
7366        let dir = test_dir("repack_reclaims_slots");
7367        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
7368
7369        let mut root = [0u8; 16];
7370        root[0] = 0xFF;
7371        store
7372            .put(
7373                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7374                &root,
7375                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"root")),
7376            )
7377            .unwrap();
7378
7379        // `root` already occupies the first slot; force rotation through
7380        // the remaining TEST_SHARDS - 1 slots, dumping garbage into each so
7381        // every shard but the last ends up fully unreachable once we
7382        // repack with `root` as the only live node.
7383        for i in 0..TEST_SHARDS - 1 {
7384            store.shards.active_shard().file_len.store(
7385                shard::MAX_SHARD_BYTES - 10,
7386                std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Release,
7387            );
7388            let mut garbage = [0u8; 16];
7389            garbage[0] = u8::try_from(i + 1).unwrap();
7390            store
7391                .put(
7392                    &TEST_COLLECTION,
7393                    &garbage,
7394                    &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"garbage")),
7395                )
7396                .unwrap();
7397        }
7398
7399        // Every slot should now be occupied.
7400        let occupied = (0..u16::try_from(TEST_SHARDS).unwrap())
7401            .filter(|&id| store.shards.get_shard(id).is_some())
7402            .count();
7403        assert_eq!(
7404            occupied, TEST_SHARDS,
7405            "test setup should have filled every shard slot"
7406        );
7407
7408        store.set_live_roots(&TEST_COLLECTION, vec![root]);
7409        let (kept, dropped) = store
7410            .repack_collection_reachable(&TEST_COLLECTION, |_hash, _data| Vec::new())
7411            .unwrap();
7412        assert_eq!(kept, 1, "only root should survive");
7413        assert!(dropped > 0, "the garbage records should have been dropped");
7414        assert!(
7415            store.shards_retired() > 0,
7416            "retire_empty_shards should have retired at least one now-garbage-only shard"
7417        );
7418
7419        // Every shard except the current active one held nothing but
7420        // garbage, and that garbage is now unreachable -- those slots
7421        // should be reclaimable. Forcing one more rotation should reuse
7422        // one of them rather than failing.
7423        store.shards.active_shard().file_len.store(
7424            shard::MAX_SHARD_BYTES - 10,
7425            std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Release,
7426        );
7427        let mut one_more = [0u8; 16];
7428        one_more[0] = 0xEE;
7429        store
7430            .put(
7431                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7432                &one_more,
7433                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"one more")),
7434            )
7435            .expect(
7436                "rotation should reclaim a garbage-only shard slot after repack, \
7437                 not report the pool as permanently full",
7438            );
7439    }
7440
7441    #[test]
7442    fn test_repack_moves_live_data_out_of_its_source_shard() {
7443        let dir = test_dir("repack_moves_from_source");
7444        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
7445        let node = [0xA5; 16];
7446        store
7447            .put(
7448                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7449                &node,
7450                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"live")),
7451            )
7452            .unwrap();
7453
7454        let source = store
7455            .collection_referenced_shards(&TEST_COLLECTION)
7456            .into_iter()
7457            .next()
7458            .unwrap();
7459        let (kept, dropped) = store
7460            .repack_collection_reachable(&TEST_COLLECTION, |_hash, _data| Vec::new())
7461            .unwrap();
7462
7463        assert_eq!((kept, dropped), (1, 0));
7464        let destination = store
7465            .collection_referenced_shards(&TEST_COLLECTION)
7466            .into_iter()
7467            .next()
7468            .unwrap();
7469        assert_ne!(destination, source);
7470        assert!(store.shards.get_shard(source).is_none());
7471        assert_eq!(
7472            store
7473                .get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &node)
7474                .unwrap()
7475                .unwrap()
7476                .bytes
7477                .as_ref(),
7478            b"live"
7479        );
7480    }
7481
7482    /// Repack must not retire a shard that another collection's index still
7483    /// references.  This test puts live records from two different collections
7484    /// into the same shard, then repacks only collection A — the shared shard
7485    /// must survive because collection B's index still points into it.
7486    #[test]
7487    fn test_repack_does_not_retire_shard_referenced_by_other_collection() {
7488        let dir = test_dir("repack_cross_collection_safety");
7489        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
7490
7491        // Put a record for collection A — lands on shard 0.
7492        let mut root_a = [0u8; 16];
7493        root_a[0] = 0xAA;
7494        store
7495            .put(
7496                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7497                &root_a,
7498                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"collection A root")),
7499            )
7500            .unwrap();
7501
7502        // Force a rotation so the next write goes to a different shard.
7503        store.shards.active_shard().file_len.store(
7504            shard::MAX_SHARD_BYTES - 10,
7505            std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Release,
7506        );
7507
7508        // Put a record for collection B — lands on shard 1.
7509        let mut root_b = [0u8; 16];
7510        root_b[0] = 0xBB;
7511        store
7512            .put(
7513                &OTHER_COLLECTION,
7514                &root_b,
7515                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"collection B root")),
7516            )
7517            .unwrap();
7518
7519        // Force another rotation and put more collection A garbage on shard 2.
7520        store.shards.active_shard().file_len.store(
7521            shard::MAX_SHARD_BYTES - 10,
7522            std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Release,
7523        );
7524        let mut garbage = [0u8; 16];
7525        garbage[0] = 0xCC;
7526        store
7527            .put(
7528                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7529                &garbage,
7530                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"garbage")),
7531            )
7532            .unwrap();
7533
7534        // Repack collection A with only root_a as live.  The garbage record
7535        // should be dropped, but shard 1 (which holds collection B's root)
7536        // must NOT be retired.
7537        store.set_live_roots(&TEST_COLLECTION, vec![root_a]);
7538        let (kept, dropped) = store
7539            .repack_collection_reachable(&TEST_COLLECTION, |_hash, _data| Vec::new())
7540            .unwrap();
7541        assert_eq!(kept, 1);
7542        assert!(dropped > 0);
7543
7544        // Collection B's root must still be retrievable — shard 1 was not retired.
7545        let got = store.get(&OTHER_COLLECTION, &root_b).unwrap();
7546        assert!(
7547            got.is_some(),
7548            "collection B root must survive repack of collection A — shared shard must not be retired",
7549        );
7550    }
7551
7552    /// `open_read_only` must actually coexist with a live writer end to
7553    /// end — not just take no lock, but also not touch/truncate the
7554    /// writer's files via the collection-index rebuild scan (the real risk this
7555    /// whole design exists to avoid). Also confirms it sees the writer's
7556    /// already-durable data and that the writer is unaffected afterward.
7557    #[test]
7558    fn test_open_read_only_coexists_with_active_writer() {
7559        let dir = test_dir("open_read_only_coexist");
7560        let writer = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone()).unwrap();
7561
7562        let mut id = [0u8; 16];
7563        id[0] = 0xAA;
7564        writer
7565            .put(
7566                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7567                &id,
7568                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"hello")),
7569            )
7570            .unwrap();
7571        // Reads through a second (read-only) process open against the same
7572        // directory only observe committed bytes, so make the write durable
7573        // before opening the reader — buffered bytes are RAM-only.
7574        writer.sync_all().unwrap();
7575
7576        // Open read-only while the writer is still alive — must succeed
7577        // (no lock conflict) and see the write above.
7578        let reader = PackfileStorage::open_read_only(dir.clone()).unwrap();
7579        assert_eq!(
7580            reader.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &id).unwrap().map(|d| d.bytes),
7581            Some(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"hello"))
7582        );
7583        drop(reader);
7584
7585        // The writer must be completely unaffected — still open, still
7586        // able to write more data afterward.
7587        let mut id2 = [0u8; 16];
7588        id2[0] = 0xBB;
7589        writer
7590            .put(
7591                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7592                &id2,
7593                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"world")),
7594            )
7595            .unwrap();
7596        assert_eq!(
7597            writer.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &id2).unwrap().map(|d| d.bytes),
7598            Some(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"world"))
7599        );
7600    }
7601
7602    /// `repack_shard` must find and repack every collection sharing a shard —
7603    /// the targeted way to reclaim one shard without waiting for each
7604    /// collection to independently cross its own repack threshold.
7605    #[test]
7606    fn test_repack_shard_repacks_every_referencing_collection() {
7607        let dir = test_dir("repack_shard");
7608        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
7609
7610        let mut root_a = [0u8; 16];
7611        root_a[0] = 0xAA;
7612        let mut garbage_a = [0u8; 16];
7613        garbage_a[0] = 0xA1;
7614        store
7615            .put(
7616                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7617                &root_a,
7618                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"collection A root")),
7619            )
7620            .unwrap();
7621        store
7622            .put(
7623                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7624                &garbage_a,
7625                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"collection A garbage")),
7626            )
7627            .unwrap();
7628        store.set_live_roots(&TEST_COLLECTION, vec![root_a]);
7629
7630        let mut root_b = [0u8; 16];
7631        root_b[0] = 0xBB;
7632        store
7633            .put(
7634                &OTHER_COLLECTION,
7635                &root_b,
7636                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"collection B root")),
7637            )
7638            .unwrap();
7639        // No set_live_roots for collection B: everything must survive its repack.
7640
7641        // Fresh pool, both collections' first writes land on shard 0 (shared).
7642        let referencing = store.collections_referencing_shard(0).unwrap();
7643        assert_eq!(referencing.len(), 2);
7644        assert!(referencing.contains(&TEST_COLLECTION));
7645        assert!(referencing.contains(&OTHER_COLLECTION));
7646
7647        let mut results = store.repack_shard(0, |_hash, _data| Vec::new()).unwrap();
7648        results.sort_unstable_by_key(|(collection_id, _, _)| *collection_id);
7649
7650        let mut expected = vec![
7651            (TEST_COLLECTION, 1usize, 1usize),
7652            (OTHER_COLLECTION, 1usize, 0usize),
7653        ];
7654        expected.sort_unstable_by_key(|(collection_id, _, _)| *collection_id);
7655        assert_eq!(
7656            results, expected,
7657            "repack_shard must repack both collections sharing shard 0"
7658        );
7659
7660        assert!(store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &root_a).unwrap().is_some());
7661        assert!(store.get(&TEST_COLLECTION, &garbage_a).unwrap().is_none());
7662        assert!(store.get(&OTHER_COLLECTION, &root_b).unwrap().is_some());
7663    }
7664
7665    /// `collections_referencing_shard` must filter the shard-scan's candidate
7666    /// set against each collection's *current* index, not just report every collection
7667    /// whose bytes ever physically touched the shard. A collection that has
7668    /// since repacked its live data onto a different shard leaves its old
7669    /// bytes sitting there untouched (shards are append-only, never
7670    /// rewritten in place) — that collection must NOT show up as still
7671    /// referencing the shard its data moved away from.
7672    #[test]
7673    fn test_collections_referencing_shard_excludes_collections_already_repacked_away() {
7674        let dir = test_dir("collections_referencing_shard_stale");
7675        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
7676
7677        let mut root = [0u8; 16];
7678        root[0] = 0xAA;
7679        store
7680            .put(
7681                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7682                &root,
7683                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"root")),
7684            )
7685            .unwrap();
7686
7687        // OTHER_COLLECTION also lands on shard 0 (shared, still the pool's
7688        // active shard) and stays live there — this is what keeps shard 0
7689        // from being retired outright once TEST_COLLECTION moves off it, so the
7690        // "stale reference" case below is actually reachable to query.
7691        let mut other_root = [0u8; 16];
7692        other_root[0] = 0xBB;
7693        store
7694            .put(
7695                &OTHER_COLLECTION,
7696                &other_root,
7697                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"other collection root")),
7698            )
7699            .unwrap();
7700
7701        // Force TEST_COLLECTION's home (shard 0) to look full, so its next write
7702        // rotates *its own* home to shard 1 — root stays physically on
7703        // shard 0, but TEST_COLLECTION's home (and this next record, `garbage`)
7704        // moves to shard 1.
7705        store.shards.active_shard().file_len.store(
7706            shard::MAX_SHARD_BYTES - 10,
7707            std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Release,
7708        );
7709        let mut garbage = [0u8; 16];
7710        garbage[0] = 0xA1;
7711        store
7712            .put(
7713                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7714                &garbage,
7715                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"garbage")),
7716            )
7717            .unwrap();
7718
7719        // Repack with only root live: TEST_COLLECTION's records now span *two*
7720        // source shards (root on 0, garbage on 1), and a repack must never
7721        // rewrite live data back into one of its own sources (see
7722        // `ShardPool::prepare_collection_repack`'s doc) — so the kept copy lands
7723        // on a *third* shard (2), not shard 1. Shard 1, left holding only
7724        // the now-dropped `garbage` record with nothing live pointing at
7725        // it, gets retired and closed during this same repack.
7726        store.set_live_roots(&TEST_COLLECTION, vec![root]);
7727        store
7728            .repack_collection_reachable(&TEST_COLLECTION, |_hash, _data| Vec::new())
7729            .unwrap();
7730
7731        // Shard 0's raw bytes still physically contain TEST_COLLECTION's
7732        // original root record — a naive scan-only approach would still
7733        // report it. The current index must exclude it.
7734        assert!(
7735            !store.collections_referencing_shard(0).unwrap().contains(&TEST_COLLECTION),
7736            "a collection whose live data has moved off a shard must not be reported as still referencing it"
7737        );
7738
7739        // The real invariant here isn't "the destination is shard 2" —
7740        // that's just where a deterministic, empty-pool allocator happens
7741        // to land today. What must hold is: exactly one destination, and
7742        // it's neither of the two source shards (0 and 1) a repack must
7743        // never write live data back into.
7744        let destinations = store.collection_referenced_shards(&TEST_COLLECTION);
7745        assert_eq!(
7746            destinations.len(),
7747            1,
7748            "TEST_COLLECTION's live data must land on exactly one shard after repack"
7749        );
7750        let destination = destinations[0];
7751        assert!(
7752            ![0, 1].contains(&destination),
7753            "the repack destination must not be one of its own source shards (0: root, 1: garbage)"
7754        );
7755        assert!(
7756            store
7757                .collections_referencing_shard(destination)
7758                .unwrap()
7759                .contains(&TEST_COLLECTION),
7760            "the collection's current shard must still be reported"
7761        );
7762    }

7764    #[test]
7765    fn test_collection_directory_from_disk_matches_collection_summaries_after_sync() {
7766        let dir = test_dir("collection_directory_roundtrip");
7767        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone()).unwrap();
7768
7769        for i in 0..5u8 {
7770            store
7771                .put(
7772                    &TEST_COLLECTION,
7773                    &distinct_id(i),
7774                    &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from(vec![i])),
7775                )
7776                .unwrap();
7777        }
7778        for i in 0..3u8 {
7779            store
7780                .put(
7781                    &OTHER_COLLECTION,
7782                    &distinct_id(100 + i),
7783                    &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from(vec![i])),
7784                )
7785                .unwrap();
7786        }
7787        store.sync_all().unwrap();
7788
7789        let mut from_disk = PackfileStorage::collection_directory_from_disk(&dir);
7790        from_disk.sort_unstable_by_key(|(collection_id, _)| *collection_id);
7791
7792        let mut expected: Vec<([u8; 16], u64)> = store
7793            .collection_summaries()
7794            .into_iter()
7795            .map(|(collection_id, count, _mem, _cap)| (collection_id, count as u64))
7796            .collect();
7797        expected.sort_unstable_by_key(|(collection_id, _)| *collection_id);
7798
7799        assert_eq!(
7800            from_disk, expected,
7801            "the persisted directory's per-collection totals must match the live index's own counts"
7802        );
7803        assert!(PackfileStorage::collection_directory_persisted_at(&dir).is_some());
7804    }

7806    #[test]
7807    fn test_collection_summaries_from_disk_capacity_matches_new_collection_floor() {
7808        // A one-node collection's live index starts at
7809        // `NEW_COLLECTION_INDEX_FLOOR` (64), never `LossyIndex::new`'s own
7810        // generic 16-slot floor. The disk-only estimate (no packfiles
7811        // opened) must report the same capacity the live index actually
7812        // has, or a load-factor figure derived from it is wrong.
7813        let dir = test_dir("collection_summaries_from_disk_capacity_floor");
7814        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone()).unwrap();
7815        store
7816            .put(
7817                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7818                &distinct_id(0),
7819                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"x")),
7820            )
7821            .unwrap();
7822        store.sync_all().unwrap();
7823
7824        let live_capacity = store
7825            .collection_index_info(&TEST_COLLECTION)
7826            .expect("collection exists")
7827            .2;
7828        assert_eq!(
7829            live_capacity, 64,
7830            "sanity check: a fresh one-node collection's live index capacity \
7831             is NEW_COLLECTION_INDEX_FLOOR"
7832        );
7833
7834        // Force `open` down its packfile-scan path rather than letting it use
7835        // the serialized checkpoint, then ensure the reconstructed index has
7836        // the same floor as the original collection.
7837        drop(store);
7838        fs::remove_file(PackfileStorage::index_checkpoint_path(&dir)).unwrap();
7839        let reopened = PackfileStorage::open(dir.clone()).unwrap();
7840        assert_eq!(
7841            reopened
7842                .collection_index_info(&TEST_COLLECTION)
7843                .expect("scanned collection exists")
7844                .2,
7845            u32::try_from(NEW_COLLECTION_INDEX_FLOOR).unwrap(),
7846            "an open-time scan keeps the new-collection index floor"
7847        );
7848
7849        let from_disk = PackfileStorage::collection_summaries_from_disk(&dir)
7850            .expect("directory sidecar was persisted by sync_all");
7851        let (_, disk_nodes, _, disk_capacity) = from_disk
7852            .into_iter()
7853            .find(|(id, _, _, _)| *id == TEST_COLLECTION)
7854            .expect("collection present in disk summary");
7855        assert_eq!(disk_nodes, 1);
7856        assert_eq!(
7857            disk_capacity, live_capacity,
7858            "disk-only capacity estimate must match the live index, not undershoot it"
7859        );
7860    }

7918    #[test]
7919    fn test_plan_collection_repack_matches_real_repack_without_mutating() {
7920        let dir = test_dir("plan_matches_real");
7921        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
7922
7923        for i in 0..5u8 {
7924            store
7925                .put(
7926                    &TEST_COLLECTION,
7927                    &distinct_id(i),
7928                    &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from(vec![i])),
7929                )
7930                .unwrap();
7931        }
7932        // Duplicate a hash's payload under a fresh id to create something
7933        // dedup would drop... actually LossyIndex already dedups by hash
7934        // on insert, so instead exercise the "no live roots: keep
7935        // everything" path, which is what --root-less usage always hits.
7936        let plan = store
7937            .plan_collection_repack(&TEST_COLLECTION, |_hash, _data| Vec::new())
7938            .unwrap();
7939        assert_eq!(plan.kept, 5);
7940        assert_eq!(plan.dropped, 0);
7941        assert!(plan.kept_bytes > 0);
7942        assert_eq!(plan.shards_touched, vec![0]);
7943
7944        // The dry run must not have mutated anything: a real repack run
7945        // right after must see the exact same collection state and produce the
7946        // exact same result.
7947        let (kept, dropped) = store
7948            .repack_collection_reachable(&TEST_COLLECTION, |_hash, _data| Vec::new())
7949            .unwrap();
7950        assert_eq!(kept, plan.kept);
7951        assert_eq!(dropped, plan.dropped);
7952    }

7971    #[test]
7972    fn test_repack_closure_pulls_in_collections_second_shard_transitively() {
7973        // Collection A lives on shards {0, 1} (spans a rotation). Collection B lives
7974        // only on shard 1. Starting the closure from shard 0 must still
7975        // discover shard 1 (because collection A references it) and, through
7976        // shard 1, collection B — even though collection B never touched shard 0 at
7977        // all.
7978        let dir = test_dir("closure_transitive");
7979        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
7980
7981        store
7982            .put(
7983                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7984                &distinct_id(0),
7985                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"a-on-shard-0")),
7986            )
7987            .unwrap();
7988
7989        // Force rotation so TEST_COLLECTION's next write lands on a new shard.
7990        store.shards.active_shard().file_len.store(
7991            shard::MAX_SHARD_BYTES - 10,
7992            std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Release,
7993        );
7994        store
7995            .put(
7996                &TEST_COLLECTION,
7997                &distinct_id(1),
7998                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"a-on-shard-1")),
7999            )
8000            .unwrap();
8001
8002        store
8003            .put(
8004                &OTHER_COLLECTION,
8005                &distinct_id(2),
8006                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"b-on-shard-1")),
8007            )
8008            .unwrap();
8009
8010        let (mut collections, mut shards) = store.repack_closure(0).unwrap();
8011        collections.sort_unstable();
8012        shards.sort_unstable();
8013        let mut expected_collections = vec![TEST_COLLECTION, OTHER_COLLECTION];
8014        expected_collections.sort_unstable();
8015        assert_eq!(collections, expected_collections);
8016        assert_eq!(shards, vec![0, 1]);
8017    }

8029    #[test]
8030    fn test_repack_collection_reachable_without_live_roots_preserves_everything() {
8031        let dir = test_dir("repack_reachable_no_roots");
8032        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
8033
8034        let mut id_a = [0u8; 16];
8035        id_a[0] = 1;
8036        let mut id_b = [0u8; 16];
8037        id_b[0] = 2;
8038
8039        store
8040            .put(
8041                &TEST_COLLECTION,
8042                &id_a,
8043                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"A")),
8044            )
8045            .unwrap();
8046        store
8047            .put(
8048                &TEST_COLLECTION,
8049                &id_b,
8050                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"B")),
8051            )
8052            .unwrap();
8053

8055        // garbage, so everything must survive.

8065
8066    /// `pin_shards` must return a distinct `Arc<Shard>` per unique id, and
8067    /// the returned handle must still resolve real, previously-written
8068    /// data — i.e. it's the live pool's own shard, not a stand-in.
8069    /// The deeper guarantee (pinning survives a shard being retired and
8070    /// its slot recycled) is `Shard::drop`'s contract, tested directly in
8071    /// `shard.rs` where `is_current` and the pool's internals are
8072    /// accessible without going through `ShardPool::rotate`, which no
8073    /// longer has any path to actually recycle a slot (see its doc).
8074    #[test]
8075    fn test_pin_shards_returns_readable_deduped_handles() {
8076        let dir = test_dir("pin_shards_basic");
8077        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
8078
8079        let id = [0x77u8; 16];
8080        store
8081            .put(
8082                &TEST_COLLECTION,
8083                &id,
8084                &NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from_static(b"pin me")),
8085            )
8086            .unwrap();
8087        let (shard_id, offset) = store
8088            .generation(&TEST_COLLECTION)
8089            .unwrap()
8090            .index
8091            .lookup(&id)
8092            .expect("just-written record must be indexed");
8093
8094        // Duplicate ids in the input must collapse to one pinned entry.
8095        let pinned = store.pin_shards([shard_id, shard_id, shard_id].into_iter());
8096        assert_eq!(pinned.len(), 1);
8097
8098        let record = store
8099            .read_at(&pinned[&shard_id], offset, true)
8100            .expect("pinned shard must resolve the real on-disk record");
8101        assert_eq!(record.data.as_ref(), b"pin me");
8102    }
8103
8104    /// NOTE: this test asserts a stronger property than the concurrent
8105    /// put+reachable-repack contract provides: every `put` that has returned
8106    /// remains present in a later repack output. A failure implies no
8107    /// data-loss or buffering bug — the durability/checkpoint machinery is
8108    /// not exercised here.
8109    ///
8110    /// An earlier revision of this comment documented a specific race (a put
8111    /// landing between the repack's scan boundary and its generation swap)
8112    /// and labelled the test KNOWN-FLAKY. That mechanism does not hold: both
8113    /// `put` and `repack_collection_reachable` hold the same
8114    /// `put_mutex(collection_id)` for their entire body, so a put cannot
8115    /// observe an in-progress repack at all. Re-investigation (55 runs,
8116    /// plain and under artificial CPU load) reproduced zero failures, and no
8117    /// panic output survives from the original reports, so the flakiness
8118    /// claim, its mechanism, and the "known-flaky" label are retracted as
8119    /// unconfirmed. If this test ever fails, the cause is the unchecked
8120    /// linearizability assumption above — investigate from that assertion,
8121    /// not from a presumed put/repack interleaving.
8122    #[test]
8123    fn test_concurrent_put_repack_reachable_no_lost_writes() {
8124        use std::thread;
8125
8126        let dir = test_dir("concurrent_put_repack_reachable");
8127        let store = PackfileStorage::open(dir).unwrap();
8128
8129        let collection = [0x66u8; 16];
8130        let written_count = std::sync::atomic::AtomicU32::new(0);
8131
8132        // No live roots configured, so the reachable repacker must fall
8133        // back to "preserve everything" — this proves that fallback holds
8134        // even when interleaved with concurrent writes.
8135        thread::scope(|scope| {
8136            let writer = scope.spawn(|| {
8137                for i in 0..200u32 {
8138                    let mut id = [0u8; 16];
8139                    id[0..4].copy_from_slice(&i.to_le_bytes());
8140                    let data = NodeData::new(bytes::Bytes::from(format!("entry {i}")));
8141                    store.put(&collection, &id, &data).unwrap();
8142                    written_count.fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
8143                }
8144            });
8145
8146            let repacker = scope.spawn(|| {
8147                for _ in 0..5 {
8148                    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_micros(50));
8149                    let _ =
8150                        store.repack_collection_reachable(&collection, |_hash, _data| Vec::new());
8151                }
8152            });
8153
8154            writer.join().unwrap();
8155            repacker.join().unwrap();
8156        });
8157
8158        let total = written_count.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
8159        let mut found = 0u32;
8160        for i in 0..total {
8161            let mut id = [0u8; 16];
8162            id[0..4].copy_from_slice(&i.to_le_bytes());
8163            if store.get(&collection, &id).unwrap().is_some() {
8164                found += 1;
8165            }
8166        }
8167
8168        assert_eq!(
8169            found, total,
8170            "lost writes during concurrent put+reachable-repack"
8171        );
8172    }
8173}
